Result: 实证结果表明，已对齐 MoE LLM 的路由模式本质上是主题驱动的，而非针对安全性。改变安全行为并不依赖于改变模型的内在路由路径。通过 RASET，研究成功定位并定位了模型中的安全关键专家，证实安全对齐是局部化在模型参数中的。

Conclusion: MoE模型的安全性并非通过将有害请求路由至特定拒绝专家实现，而是表现出主题驱动的路由模式。由于安全性通过参数而非路由分布实现，这种局部化的安全性机制可能被绕过，因此需要开发专家感知的对齐机制来应对此风险。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLMs rely on sparse, router-driven expert activation, yet how safety alignment interacts with routed expert specialization remains underexplored. A common intuition is that safety behavior may be controlled by routing harmful requests to distinct refusal-oriented experts. In this work, we provide empirical evidence for a different picture: routing patterns in aligned MoE LLMs are largely topic-driven, while safety behavior can be altered with little change to the model's intrinsic routing path.
  Motivated by this observation, we present **RASET** (**R**outer-**A**gnostic **S**afety-critical **E**xpert **T**uning), a red-teaming framework that probes safety enforcement that is localized in a small subset of experts while preserving the model's intrinsic routing behavior. **RASET** identifies safety-critical experts via a contrastive routing-sensitivity criterion and applies parameter-efficient tuning only to the selected experts, minimizing semantic disruption relative to router-steering interventions. These results reveal a distinct MoE safety risk, highlighting the need for expert-aware alignment mechanisms.

</details>


### [77] [Personalized Turn-Level User Conversation Satisfaction Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29711)
*Zhefan Wang,Zhiqiang Guo,Weizhi Ma,Min Zhang,Quanjia Yan,Hengliang Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对AI助手在对话轮次中个性化满足度评估困难的问题，提出了利用用户历史记忆与上下文的评估框架及PersTurnBench基准，有效实现了无需实时人类反馈的深度性能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用自动评估指标难以捕捉AI助手在特定轮次中对不同用户的个性化差异化满足，导致模型效果难以在大规模场景下准确量化。

Method: 1. 构建了一个包含用户记忆（User Memories）与目标轮次上下文（Target-turn Context）的对话满足度评估器；2. 采用事后得分校准（Post-hoc calibration）技术；3. 提出PersTurnBench基准，通过固定重演状态（Replay state）对比不同生成模型（通用型与个性化型）的性能。

Result: 研究表明，该个性化记忆与校准方法在序数一致性和识别不满轮次任务上，均超过了现有的监督式、检索式及通用大模型作为裁判（LLM-as-a-judge）的基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过引入个性化评估器和PersTurnBench基准，研究证明了结合用户记忆与上下文可以显著提升对AI助手输出满足度的评估准确性，为无需频繁采集人类反馈的个性化系统性能评估提供了有效方案。

Abstract: User satisfaction with AI assistants is highly personalized: the same response may satisfy one user but disappoint another depending on what each user expects and what they have asked for before. Existing automatic evaluation methods mostly measure generic response quality, making it difficult to judge whether a response satisfies a user at a specific turn. We study this problem as personalized turn-level user conversation satisfaction evaluation. We build a conversation satisfaction evaluator that combines compact user memories with target-turn context to produce satisfaction scores and dissatisfaction-oriented rationales. Meta-evaluation against human satisfaction annotations shows that personalized memory and post-hoc score calibration improve ordinal agreement and dissatisfied-turn detection over supervised, retrieval-based, and generic LLM-as-a-judge baselines. We further introduce PersTurnBench, a personalized turn-level user conversation satisfaction benchmark that uses the verified evaluator to assess generation models via replay. By holding the replay state fixed, PersTurnBench enables controlled comparison of generic generation models and memory-augmented personalized systems without new human labels for every candidate model. The evaluator and benchmark let researchers compare candidate generation models on personalized satisfaction without collecting new user feedback for every model.

</details>


### [78] [Teaching Language Models to Check Grounded Claim Factuality with Human Test-Taking Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29712)
*Yuxuan Ye,Raul Santos-Rodriguez,Edwin Simpson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于阅读理解范式和SLM蒸馏的事实性核查框架，在大幅降低推理成本的同时，保持了高质量的事实核查准确性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事实性核查方法存在局限性：基于蕴含（entailment）的分类器需特定数据集阈值调整，而直接提示LLM的方法不仅计算成本高，且未能充分利用LLM的推理能力。

Method: 1. 将事实性核查重构为“真/假”阅读理解任务，并使用显式的应试策略提示LLM进行高效推理；2. 通过监督微调（SFT）和自修正（self-revision）机制，训练小模型（SLM）以替代高成本的LLM。

Result: 在两项基准测试中表现出竞争力，并在其中一项上达到了新的先进水平（SOTA）；推理成本降低了80%以上。

Conclusion: 通过将事实性核查转化为阅读理解任务并引入SLM替代LLM，实现了在降低计算成本的同时，保持或超越现有先进水平的事实性核查性能，并具备了较好的可解释性。

Abstract: Grounded claim factuality checking is important for large language model (LLM) applications such as retrieval-augmented generation, as it helps users assess the correctness of generated outputs. Existing metrics using entailment classifiers require dataset-specific threshold tuning, while LLM-based approaches often use direct prompting, which underutilises the reasoning capabilities of LLMs. We address this by formulating grounded claim factuality checking as a true/false reading comprehension task and prompting LLMs with explicit test-taking strategies for efficient reasoning. Our method reduces token usage by over 80% compared to unguided open-ended reasoning, and achieves competitive performance to more expensive alternatives across two factuality benchmarks, setting a new state of the art on one. To further reduce inference cost, we train small language models (SLMs) to replace LLMs in the checking pipeline. Using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and a self-revision mechanism, the SLMs learn to improve their factuality judgements. Experimental results show that the resulting SLMs perform on par with strong baselines, combining low inference costs with generating supporting rationales to support interpretability. Code and datasets will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [79] [Leveraging Routing Dynamics in Mixture-of-Experts Models for Efficient Language Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29714)
*Aditi Khandelwal,Marius Mosbach,Verna Dankers,Siva Reddy,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了MoE模型在多语言持续预培训中的路由逻辑，发现语言特异性主要集中在模型末端层，并据此提出一种仅需更新2%参数的高效多语言适配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MoE模型在多语言设置下的专家路由行为和适应机制缺乏研究，尤其是在将英语中心模型转化为多语言模型的持续预训练场景中。

Method: 分析多语言持续预训练阶段MoE的路由动态，发现语言特异性主要存在于末端层。据此提出一种参数高效的适配策略，仅更新末端MoE层中的语言特定专家和共享专家。

Result: 在MultiBLiMP和Belebele基准测试中，该方法在参数更新量少于2%的情况下，性能表现与全参数微调末端层相当，实现了优秀的性能-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了多语言持续预训练中MoE模型的路由机制，验证了“末端层是语言特异性形成的关键”这一假设，通过仅微调部分专家实现了高效的多语言适配。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are widely used to scale language models, yet their expert routing behavior and adaptation in a multilingual setting remain underexplored. In this work, we study multilingual routing dynamics during continual pre-training of an English-centric MoE model on a multilingual corpus, analyzing how expert usage varies across languages. We find that continual multilingual pre-training leads to diffused, language-agnostic routing in early and middle layers, with language specialization primarily emerging in the final layers. We also show that token-level vocabulary overlap between languages plays an important role in how languages are routed. Motivated by these findings, we propose a parameter-efficient adaptation strategy that updates language-specific and shared experts in the final MoE layers. Experiments on MultiBLiMP and Belebele show that our method achieves a strong performance-efficiency trade-off, attaining competitive performance relative to fine-tuning complete final layers, while updating less than 2% of the parameters. Overall, our findings provide insights into where and how language specialization emerges in MoEs during continual pre-training and provide practical insights for low-resource multilingual adaptation. Our code is available at https://github.com/aditi184/moe-routing-adaptation.

</details>


### [80] [User-Aware Active Knowledge Acquisition for Emotional Support Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29715)
*Mufan Xu,Kehai Chen,Jiahao Hu,Xinchao Xu,Muyun Yang,Tiejun Zhao,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对情感支持对话中用户需求模糊的问题，UKA 框架通过心智理论驱动的主动学习，引导系统主动获取信息，从而提升了对话的质量与用户适配性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感支持对话系统难以从多轮对话中准确捕捉弱信号和隐含的用户需求，导致知识获取和泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出“用户感知主动知识获取”（UKA）框架。该方法包含一个基于心智理论（ToM）的不确定性估计机制，用于量化用户需求的不确定性；通过梯度无关（gradient-free）的主动学习策略，引导模型优先生成能够获取更多反馈的响应，并在训练中高效探索符合用户需求的对话知识。

Result: 在多个对话基准测试和模型架构上，UKA 均优于现有的强基线模型，证明了其在对话质量和用户对齐方面的有效性。

Conclusion: UKA 框架通过引入主动学习和心智理论（ToM）不确定性估计，有效解决了多轮对话中用户需求难以把握的问题，显著提升了情感支持对话系统的质量与用户对齐程度。

Abstract: Emotional support plays an important role in dialogue systems, and its success depends on adapting to a user's evolving and implicit needs across multi-turn interactions while leveraging the strong reasoning capacity of large language models. However, since signals about user needs are often weak, indirect, and can only be disambiguated through multi-turn interaction, existing emotional support methods often struggle to acquire and generalize relevant conversational knowledge efficiently. To bridge this gap, we introduce User-Aware Active Knowledge Acquisition (UKA), a gradient-free active dialogue learning framework that explicitly represents uncertainty about user needs and incorporates active learning into both knowledge acquisition and response selection.We propose a Theory-of-Mind uncertainty estimation mechanism that allows the model to prioritize responses, thereby eliciting more informative user feedback. UKA is capable of efficiently exploring user-aligned conversational knowledge during training while maintaining robustness at test time. Experiments across multiple dialogue benchmarks and model architectures demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms strong baselines in dialogue quality and user alignment.

</details>


### [81] [HTAM: Hierarchical Transition-Attended Memory for Operator Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29734)
*Yining Zhang,Mingyang Yi,Chen Wang,Xuwen Xiang,Tianhe Jia,Zedong Dan,Chengqing Zong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对 GPU 算子自动优化中 LLM 难以平衡全局宏观优化与局部具体实现的问题，提出 HTAM 分层记忆框架，通过两层转换图管理优化经验，实现从粗到细的代码自动生成与调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 在优化 GPU 算子时存在“粒度不匹配”问题：粗粒度提示难以转化为代码，细粒度记忆则会导致搜索空间爆炸并掩盖优化瓶颈，缺乏有效的全局与局部经验组织结构。

Method: 提出 HTAM（分层转换注意力记忆）框架，构建两层分层转换图（HTG）来存储全局优化方向、局部具体策略及步骤间的转换经验；采用从粗到细的推导流程：先根据状态和历史选择全局策略，再检索局部经验，最后指导 CUDA 代码生成。

Result: 在 KernelBench 基准测试中，HTAM 在正确率、快速求解率和加速比方面均优于现有的 LLM 基准方法；在多个后端测试及 Robust-KBench 中表现出极强的可迁移性。

Conclusion: HTAM 通过分层记忆机制有效解决了 LLM 在 GPU 算子优化任务中的粒度不匹配问题，验证了结构化记忆在领域内优化任务中的可迁移性和有效性，证实了分层架构在提升复杂代码生成任务质量方面的优势。

Abstract: High-performance GPU kernels are essential for efficient LLM deployment, yet optimizing them remains expertise-intensive. Recent LLM-based code generation makes automatic GPU operator generation promising, but operator optimization remains a hardware-aware search problem. Existing LLM-based methods face a granularity mismatch: coarse hints are reusable but hard to execute, whereas detailed memories are actionable but enlarge the search space and obscure optimization bottlenecks. The key challenge is therefore to organize optimization experience at an appropriate granularity. To address this issue, this paper proposes HTAM (Hierarchical Transition-Attended Memory), a coarse-to-fine framework for LLM-based operator optimization. HTAM builds a two-level Hierarchical Transition Graph (HTG) to organize coarse global directions, detailed local strategies, and transition experience between optimization steps. During each evolution step, HTAM selects a global direction from the current state and recent optimization history, retrieves the corresponding local strategy memory, and uses it to guide concrete CUDA code generation. Experiments on the full KernelBench suite demonstrate that HTAM consistently improves correctness, fast-solution rate, and speedup over LLM-based baselines, while backend and Robust-KBench studies indicate transferable benefits from structured memory.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [82] [Generative Spatiotemporal Intent Sequence Recommendation via Implicit Reasoning in Amap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28888)
*Sicong Wang,Ruiting Dong,Yue Liu,Bowen Zheng,Jun Meng,Jie Li,Shuaijun Guo,Yu Gu,Fanyi Di,Xin Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对生成式时空意图序列推荐（GSISR）中大模型推理延迟高及可行性差的问题，本文提出了 GPlan 框架，通过隐式思维链蒸馏和反事实偏好优化，在轻量化模型中实现了高效且符合物理约束的意图规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景下的用户行为呈现时空依赖的意图流。虽大模型在生成逻辑连贯的意图序列方面表现优异，但其计算延迟高且难以满足复杂动态的时空物理约束，无法直接在工业界落地。

Method: 1. 渐进式隐式思维链蒸馏（Progressive Implicit CoT Distillation）：将大模型的推理过程压缩进保留的潜在语义标记中，以降低推理延迟；2. 时空反事实直接偏好优化（Spatiotemporal Counterfactual DPO）：通过构造反事实上下文-方案对，对模型进行微调，增强其对时空语境的敏感度，减少物理不可行方案的生成。

Result: 离线实验与在线A/B测试表明，GPlan 显著提升了推荐序列的逻辑连贯性和时空语境的响应能力，在保持工业级低延迟的同时，有效平衡了推理性能与执行可行性。

Conclusion: GPlan 通过将大模型的推理能力蒸馏至轻量化模型，并利用反事实偏好优化处理物理约束，成功解决了生成式时空意图推荐中推理延迟高、逻辑不一致及物理不可行的问题。

Abstract: Real-world user behavior rarely consists of isolated actions; instead, it often forms intent flows governed by spatiotemporal dependencies. To provide integrated service recommendations, we focus on the task of Generative Spatiotemporal Intent Sequence Recommendation (GSISR), which aims to generate intent sequences that are logically coherent and physically executable within complex spatiotemporal contexts. While LLMs offer strong reasoning potential for GSISR, direct industrial deployment is limited by high inference latency and context-mismatched or physically infeasible plans. To address these challenges, we propose a generative framework, GPlan, that internalizes LLM reasoning into lightweight models through two components. First, to enable reasoning under strict latency constraints, we introduce Progressive Implicit CoT Distillation, which compresses explicit reasoning processes into reserved latent tokens, allowing small models to inherit complex planning logic without generating long reasoning text. Second, to address the disconnect between general knowledge and real-world constraints, we design Spatiotemporal Counterfactual DPO. By aligning the model with counterfactual context-plan pairs, we improve sensitivity to spatiotemporal context and reduce context-mismatched plans. Offline experiments and online A/B testing demonstrate that our approach improves sequence coherence and context responsiveness. Our implementation and the anonymized GSISR dataset are available at https://github.com/alibaba/GPlan.

</details>


### [83] [Toward User Preference Alignment in LLM Recommendation via Explicit Context Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29141)
*Weizhi Zhang,Wooseong Yang,Yuxin Cui,Zhaohui Guo,Hins Hu,Liangwei Yang,Henry Peng Zou,Qifei Wang,Hanqing Zeng,Jiayi Liu,Yinglong Xia,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 文章呼吁下一代大模型推荐系统应从偏重物品元数据转向充分利用用户显式反馈（评论等），以构建更精准、透明且具备语义理解能力的个性化推荐系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统过于依赖隐式信号（点击、购买），忽视了包含用户决策深层逻辑和语义信息的显式反馈（评论、评价），导致难以理解用户偏好，加剧信息茧房问题。现有大模型推荐多关注物品元数据，未能充分挖掘用户生成的显式文本价值。

Method: 本文为综述及前瞻性研究：回顾了推荐系统的演变范式，阐述了上下文丰富反馈（explicit context feedback）的价值，并提出了将显式用户反馈整合至可扩展的大模型推荐系统的新框架。

Result: 指出通过显式上下文反馈可以改善用户偏好对齐，增强推荐的可解释性，并为设计下一代大模型驱动的推荐系统提供了理论基础和系统框架。

Conclusion: 强调了将显式语义反馈集成到大模型推荐系统中的必要性，并呼吁建立新的基准测试与评估指标，旨在构建更具个性化、透明度和可解释性的下一代推荐系统。

Abstract: Traditional recommender systems (RecSys) primarily infer user preferences from implicit signals (such as clicks, watches, and purchases), often neglecting the rich explicit contextual feedback users provide through verbal text, like comments and reviews. This explicit context feedback captures the nuanced reasons behind user decisions regarding their preferences. In addition, it offers critical heterogeneous information for user preference alignment and more explainable recommendations. Overlooking such signals can lead to misaligned user preferences and further reinforce filter bubbles, as algorithms fail to understand the "semantic context" behind user choices. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) present new opportunities to harness user-generated content for more accurate and diverse recommendations, yet current LLM-based recommendations still focus on using item meta-data and underutilize this resource. In this paper, we advocate for prioritizing explicit context feedback in the next generation of LLM-based RecSys. We review the evolution of recommendation paradigms, highlight the value of context-rich feedback, call for new benchmarks and metrics, and introduce frameworks for integrating explicit user signals into scalable LLM-driven RecSys. Centering on user-preference modeling, we aim to foster more personalized, transparent, and explainable RecSys online platforms.

</details>


### [84] [CrossAlpha: An Annual-Report Benchmark for Cross-Market Factor Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29286)
*Qian Wang,Zhongyi Tong,Nuo Chen,Zhaomin Wu,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CrossAlpha，这是首个用于跨市场因子研究的公开年度报告基准，通过标准化、去偏和时间对齐，实现了跨市场信息对股价预测能力的有效评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融研究缺乏跨市场因子分析的公开基准。构建该基准面临三大挑战：文件语言与监管体系差异、披露信息的共性报告偏见、以及跨市场交易时间对齐的复杂性。

Method: 1. 披露内容蒸馏（Disclosure Distillation）：将多语言、多监管体系下的年报转化为标准化的十类英语业务描述；2. 残差模式图构建（Residual Schema Graph Construction）：利用PCA白化技术构建消除常见报告偏差的跨市场企业对相似度分数；3. 时间对齐评估（Timing-Aligned Evaluation）：结合11年日度量价数据，在可行的跨市场交易协议下进行回测。

Result: 在美日市场测试中，基于跨市场披露信息的同构对策略表现优于行业基准及国内文本基准（ICIR 0.39 vs 0.07-0.18）；在多数目标市场中，跨市场信号均表现出优于国内文本基准的预测潜力。

Conclusion: CrossAlpha 成功地填补了跨市场文本挖掘金融领域的空白，证明了跨市场披露信息具有显著的预测价值，为未来的跨市场金融NLP研究提供了稳健、标准化的基准框架。

Abstract: Cross-market factor research studies whether firm-level signals from one or more markets can predict returns in a target market, but existing public benchmarks do not support cross-market disclosure-to-return evaluation. Building such a benchmark is challenging because filings differ across languages and regulatory systems, disclosure-derived similarity can be biased by common reporting components, and cross-market signals must be evaluated under feasible trading-time alignment. We introduce \textbf{CrossAlpha}, a public annual-report benchmark for cross-market factor research. CrossAlpha addresses these challenges through three corresponding components: \emph{Disclosure Distillation}, which standardises heterogeneous filings into ten-category English business descriptions; \emph{Residual Schema Graph Construction}, which builds PCA-whitened cross-market firm-pair scores from schema-level disclosures; and \emph{Timing-Aligned Evaluation}, which pairs the graph with 11 years of daily OHLCV data to construct forward-return labels under feasible cross-market execution protocols. CrossAlpha covers about 3,600 firms and 10,700 firm-year reports from the United States, Japan, Taiwan, South Korea, and Hong Kong, and releases about 19M directed firm-pair scores. In experiments, disclosure-derived cross-market peers outperform domestic text, industry-code, and return-correlation peers in the US-to-Japan setting (ICIR 0.39 versus 0.07--0.18), and cross-market sources beat the domestic text baseline in most target markets. CrossAlpha offers an open-sourced, reusable, return-grounded benchmark for cross-market financial NLP.

</details>


### [85] [UniNote: A Unified Embedding Model for Multimodal Representation and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29287)
*Jinghan Zhao,Wenwei Jin,Anqi Li,Jintao Tong,Luya Mo,Jiawei Li,Bin Li,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UniNote 是一款专为工业级 I2I（物品到物品）检索设计的统一嵌入模型，通过对比微调和强化学习两阶段训练，成功平衡了检索精度与计算延迟，实现了大规模应用下的性能与效率双提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用多模态嵌入方法在工业 I2I 检索中难以平衡全局语义与细粒度检索的关系，且解耦的嵌入-排序流水线存在效率低下与延迟问题。

Method: 提出 UniNote 模型，采用两阶段训练范式：第一阶段利用对比监督微调（Contrastive SFT）构建基础嵌入；第二阶段引入强化学习（RL）进行对齐以优化排序质量；并结合 Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) 提升效率。

Result: UniNote 在多项 I2I 任务中达到 SOTA 水平。在小红书的实际部署中，结合 MRL 技术，显著提升了检索质量并降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: UniNote 提出了一种工业级 I2I 检索方案，通过两阶段训练和定制化检索策略，在保证检索查准率的同时实现了高性能和高效率，在大规模应用中表现优异。

Abstract: Item-to-Item (I2I) retrieval is a fundamental part of modern content platforms, supporting critical industrial workflows from recommendation engines to content auditing. While multimodal embedding methods have advanced general retrieval, they often falter in I2I scenarios due to the challenges of balancing global content representation with fine-grained local retrieval, the systemic inefficiency of decoupled embedding-and-ranking pipelines, and the inherent trade-offs between model precision and serving latency. To solve these issues, we propose \textbf{UniNote}, a unified embedding model designed for industrial I2I retrieval. Tailored retrieval strategies are introduced to support representation learning over complex, multimodal content at varying granularities. To operationalize these strategies, UniNote employs a two-stage training paradigm: the first stage leverages contrastive SFT to establish robust base embeddings, while the second stage refines ranking quality through a reinforcement learning (RL) process that aligns the model with content relevance. Our results show that UniNote achieves SOTA performance across diverse I2I tasks. Deployed at Xiaohongshu and integrated with Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL), UniNote achieved significant improvements in retrieval quality and cost efficiency in large-scale applications.

</details>


### [86] [ACE: Anisotropy-Controllable Embedding for LLM-enhanced Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29322)
*Dongcheol Lee,Hye-young Kim,Jongwuk Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 嵌入在推荐系统中存在的各向异性问题，本文提出了 ACE 框架，通过线性重构与正则化平衡嵌入的几何分布与语义结构，显著提升了序列推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM-as-Extractor 范式中，LLM 生成的物品嵌入存在严重的各向异性（几何分布不均），导致模型难以有效学习协同过滤信号，限制了推荐性能。

Method: 提出了一种各向异性可控嵌入（ACE）方法，利用线性自编码器（LAE）对 LLM 嵌入进行重塑。通过 L2 正则化约束维度分散度以缓解各向异性，并通过重构损失函数保持语义关系。

Result: 实验结果表明，ACE 在 Recall@20 和 NDCG@20 指标上分别取得了最高 12.4% 和 11.8% 的提升，优于现有的 LLM 增强型序列推荐模型。

Conclusion: ACE 通过线性自编码器实现几何均匀性与语义信息的平衡，有效解决了 LLM 生成嵌入的各向异性问题，显著提升了顺序推荐系统的性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in the LLM-as-Extractor paradigm leverage large language models (LLMs) to transfer semantically rich item embeddings into sequential recommendation (SR) backbones. However, LLM-generated embeddings often suffer from strong anisotropy. Most vectors are concentrated in similar directions, resulting in a geometric imbalance that makes it difficult to adapt to collaborative signals during fine-tuning. To address this challenge, we propose Anisotropy-Controllable Embedding (ACE), which explicitly controls the anisotropy of LLM-generated embeddings. Specifically, ACE utilizes a linear autoencoder (LAE) to reshape the embedding distribution while preserving its semantic structure. In this process, the L2-regularization term mitigates the anisotropy by controlling the dispersion of embedding dimensions, while the reconstruction loss maintains semantic relationships among items. That is, ACE balances geometric uniformity and semantic embedding preservation for more stable learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ACE consistently outperforms existing LLM-enhanced SR models, yielding improvements of up to 12.4% and 11.8% in Recall@20 and NDCG@20, respectively.

</details>


### [87] [FLASH-MAXSIM: IO-Aware Fused Kernels for Late-Interaction Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29517)
*Roi Pony,Adi Raz Goldfarb,Idan Friedman,Daniel Ezer,Udi Barzelay*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Flash-MaxSim 是一种针对后期交互检索模型的高效 GPU 内核，通过消除大规模相似度张量的物化，显著降低了显存需求并提升了训练与推理吞吐量，且能做到检索精度的无损保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ColBERT 和 ColPali 等早期的“后期交互”（late-interaction）检索架构在计算时会产生巨大的查询-文档相似度张量，这不仅导致了极高的显存消耗，还限制了推理和训练的批处理规模。

Method: 提出一种 IO 感知（IO-aware）的融合 GPU 内核（kernel），通过在片上 SRAM 中流式处理查询和文档块（tiles）直接计算 MaxSim 分数，避免了显存中完整相似度张量的物化；并在反向传播中实现了无原子操作（atomic-free）的梯度规约，以及结合了 INT8 量化和变长序列处理技术。

Result: 在 A100/H100 上实现了高达 3.9x-4.7x 的推理加速，最高降低了 16x 的推理显存和 28x 的训练显存占用，且在保持 100% Top-20 排行一致性的前提下，支持了原本无法处理的超大规模语料和 batch size。

Conclusion: Flash-MaxSim 成功克服了 ColBERT/ColPali 等深度检索模型在 GPU 显存和计算效率上的瓶颈，通过内核融合（kernel fusion）技术实现了无损的性能提升，为大规模多模态文档检索提供了高效的工程实现路径。

Abstract: Late-interaction retrieval (ColBERT, ColPali) scores a query against a document with the MaxSim operator: for every query token, the maximum similarity over the document tokens, summed over
  query tokens. The standard implementation materializes the full query-token x document-token similarity tensor in GPU memory; for visual ColPali at 10K documents this tensor alone is 21 GB in
  FP16, created only to be reduced to one score per document and discarded. It exhausts a 40 GB GPU and bounds the achievable batch size in both inference and training. We present
  Flash-MaxSim, an IO-aware fused GPU kernel that computes exactly the same scores without ever materializing the tensor, by streaming query and document tiles through on-chip SRAM and folding
  the row-maximum reduction into the same pass. We extend the IO-aware principle through the training backward pass, an inverse-grid CSR construction that reuses the forward argmax for an
  atomic-free, destination-owned gradient reduction, and through INT8xINT8 quantization and variable-length (padding-free) scoring. Flash-MaxSim is up to 3.9x faster on an A100 (4.7x on an
  H100) than naive PyTorch at matched precision, uses up to 16x less inference memory and ~28x less training memory, unlocks corpus and batch sizes that exhaust PyTorch entirely, preserves the
  exact ranking (100% top-20 agreement with an FP32 reference)

</details>


### [88] [Rec-Distill: An Industrial Distillation Pipeline for Large-Scale Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29755)
*Haoran Ding,Wenlin Zhao,Yuchen Jiang,Juren Li,Jie Zhu,Xinchun Li,Yishujie Zhao,Yi Zhang,Ao Qiao,Jianhui Dong,Cheng Chen,Ziyan Gong,Deping Xie,Peng Xu,Zikai Wang,Yuwei Wang,Huizhi Yang,Zhe Chen,Yuchao Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Rec-Distill 是一种工业级蒸馏流水线，通过一系列优化策略，成功将大规模推荐模型（24B 参数）的性能迁移至轻量级在线模型，在满足生产效率与延迟约束的情况下显著提升了业务效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模推荐模型的效果虽好，但由于工业级生产对延迟和效率的严格限制，难以直接部署；大模型离线训练与高效在线部署之间存在巨大鸿沟。

Method: 提出了 Rec-Distill 蒸馏流水线，包含四项关键技术：解耦训练（decoupled training）、黑盒蒸馏（black-box distillation）、去偏机制（debiasing mechanism）以及针对动态推荐环境的混合批处理/流式处理流水线（hybrid batch-streaming pipeline）。

Result: 在多个真实工业场景中，将教师模型参数扩容至 24B 及 20K 行为序列长度；学生模型成功继承了教师模型大部分增益，最佳情况下蒸馏迁移率超过 60%，并有效转化为实际的业务指标提升。

Conclusion: Rec-Distill 提供了一个实用的框架，成功将大规模推荐模型的性能迁移至轻量级在线服务模型中，弥合了模型扩容与生产部署之间的鸿沟，为未来向更大规模推荐模型发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large recommendation models have demonstrated substantial potential gains under scaling laws, yet these gains are difficult to realize in industrial recommendation systems because real-world deployment requires lightweight models with strict serving efficiency and latency guarantees. This creates a fundamental gap between offline model scaling and online deployment. In this work, we present Rec-Distill, an industrial distillation pipeline that transfers the performance gains of large-scale recommendation modeling to efficient serving models. Rec-Distill combines large-teacher scaling with student-side transfer optimization through decoupled training, black-box distillation, debiasing mechanism, and a hybrid batch-streaming pipeline for dynamic recommendation environments. Across multiple recommendation and advertising scenarios on real-world platforms, our framework scales teacher models up to 24B dense parameters and 20K behavior sequence length, while enabling lightweight students to recover a substantial portion of teacher gains, with distillation transferability exceeding 60% in the best setting. Extensive offline and online experiments further show that these transferred gains consistently translate into measurable business improvements under industrial constraints. These results demonstrate that Rec-Distill provides a practical framework for distilling large-scale recommendation models into deployable, cost-efficient serving systems, while also establishing a reliable path toward scaling recommendation models to even larger regimes in the future.

</details>


### [89] [Uncertainty Quantification for Multimodal Retrieval Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29956)
*Simon Binz,Heydar Soudani,Faegheh Hasibi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对多模态 RAG 系统中不确定性难以量化的问题，本文提出了 LeMUQ 方法，通过分析多模态上下文下的 Token 概率变化来评估模型置信度，显著提升了系统输出的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不确定性量化方法多针对纯文本模型，在多模态 RAG（检索增强生成）场景下表现不佳。多模态 RAG 的不确定性源于检索、视觉理解和输出生成等多个阶段，现有的方法难以有效捕捉这些复杂交互。

Method: 提出了一种名为 LeMUQ（Learnable Multimodal Uncertainty Quantification）的可学习多模态不确定性量化方法。该方法通过输入修改（如移除模态或检索上下文）分析 Token 概率，将这些概率信号编码为 Token，并利用微调后的模型处理这些信号，从而捕捉模态与检索之间的交互关系。

Result: 在多个数据集、检索器和 VLM 的测试中，LeMUQ 表现优于基准方法及其他微调后的 UQ 方法，其 AUROC 指标平均提升了 3.8%，并展现出较强的跨检索设置泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 LeMUQ 能够有效量化多模态 RAG 系统中的不确定性，提高系统输出的可靠性与安全性，为构建更可靠的多模态应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) improves the question answering capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) by incorporating external knowledge and has recently been extended to multimodal settings through Vision-Language Models (VLMs) that integrate visual and textual information. Despite these advances, generated answers can still be incorrect or misleading. Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) methods aim to estimate the reliability of model outputs, but most existing approaches are designed for text-only models and perform poorly in multimodal RAG scenarios. A key challenge is capturing uncertainty arising from multiple stages of the pipeline, including retrieval, visual understanding, and generation. In this work, we show that modeling uncertainty using multimodal and retrieval-aware probability signals improves estimation in multimodal RAG systems. We introduce LeMUQ, a Learnable Multimodal UQ method that analyzes token probabilities under input modifications, such as removing modalities or retrieved context. By encoding these signals as probability tokens and processing them with a finetuned model, our approach captures interactions between modalities and retrieval. Experiments across datasets, retrievers, and VLMs show consistent improvements over baseline and finetuned UQ methods. Our proposed LeMUQ increases the AUROC metric by 3.8% on average. Additionally, our method shows strong generalization performance across different retrieval setups and datasets with mixed results when transferring across different VLMs. Our findings highlight the importance of modeling multimodal uncertainty and provide a step toward more reliable and safer multimodal RAG systems. Code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [90] [No More K-means:Single-Stage Sparse Coding for Efficient Multi-Vector Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30120)
*Lixuan Guo,Yifei Wang,Tiansheng Wen,Aosong Feng,Stefanie Jegelka,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了SSR（单阶段稀疏检索），利用稀疏自动编码器将标记嵌入转换为高维稀疏表示，完全绕过了昂贵的向量聚类，在显著提升存储与检索效率的同时改善了检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多向量检索（MVR）模型在提供高检索精度的同时，因其巨大的向量规模，被迫依赖昂贵的聚类和维度压缩技术，导致索引延迟高且存在不可避免的语义信息损失。

Method: 提出单阶段稀疏检索（SSR）范式，利用稀疏自动编码器（SAE）将标记向量投影为高维稀疏表示，并取代传统的多向量模型（如ColBERT）中基于聚类的索引，利用倒排索引实现高效检索。

Result: 在BEIR基准测试中，SSR相比ColBERTv2索引时间缩短了15倍，检索延迟降低了一半，并在检索准确度上超越了现有先进模型。

Conclusion: 通过引入稀疏自动编码器（SAE）将细粒度特征转化为高维稀疏表示，SSR成功规避了多向量检索中昂贵的聚类过程，在显著降低索引成本和推理延迟的同时提升了检索精度，实现了检索性能的“三赢”。

Abstract: Multi-vector retrieval (MVR) models, exemplified by ColBERT, have established new benchmarks in retrieval accuracy by preserving fine-grained token-level interactions. However, this granularity imposes prohibitive storage and retrieval efficiency bottlenecks: to manage the immense memory footprint and computational overhead of billion-scale token vectors, state-of-the-art systems are forced to rely on aggressive dimension reduction and complex clustering (e.g., K-means). This compromise introduces two critical limitations: excessive indexing latency of clustering large-scale corpora and semantic information loss inherent to compression. In this paper, we propose Single-stage Sparse Retrieval (SSR}, a paradigm shift that replaces expensive clustering with efficient sparse coding. Instead of compressing features into low-dimensional dense vectors, we utilize Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) to project token embeddings into a high-dimensional but highly sparse representation. This transformation enables us to bypass vector clustering entirely and leverage inverted indexing for precise, high-throughput retrieval. Extensive experiments on the BEIR benchmark demonstrate that SSR achieves a "trifecta" of improvements: it reduces indexing time by 15x compared to ColBERTv2, halves retrieval latency, and simultaneously improves retrieval performance over leading baselines.

</details>


### [91] [LexPath: A domain-oriented multi-path framework for legal article retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30205)
*Weixuan Liu,Qingfeng Zhuge,Xuyang Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LexPath 是一个专为法律场景设计的检索框架，通过融合 IRAC 逻辑、法律结构信息及意图重排序机制，解决了传统检索方法在法律条文精准匹配上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用检索方法过度依赖表层词汇或语义相似度，难以区分在文本上相似但法律上无关或不适用的条文，无法满足法律 AI 系统对法律依据可追溯性与准确性的高要求。

Method: 提出 LexPath 框架，包含两个核心模块：1. 多路径检索模块（IRAC 指导的稀疏路径用于关键词扩展，结构指导的稠密路径利用法律层级和引用关系进行训练）；2. 意图感知重排序模块（通过计算查询与条文的意图一致性分数来优化候选排序）。

Result: 在公开基准集及自建的专业领域数据集上，LexPath 的表现均优于现有的词汇检索、稠密检索、混合检索及自适应 RAG 基线模型，且消融实验证实了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: LexPath 通过结合法律逻辑路径与意图感知重排序，有效提升了法律条文检索的准确性，证明了领域特定知识与架构设计在处理复杂法律查询中的必要性。

Abstract: Legal article retrieval is critical for building traceable and reliable legal AI systems, where conclusions must be grounded in specific legal articles. However, existing open-domain retrieval methods rely heavily on surface-level lexical or semantic similarity, making it difficult for them to distinguish legally relevant articles from those that are textually similar but legally inapplicable or misaligned with the user's underlying intent. To bridge this gap, we propose \textsc{LexPath}, a domain-oriented multi-path framework comprising a multi-path retrieval module and an intent-aware reranking module. The retrieval module combines two complementary legal-specific paths to collect candidate articles: an IRAC-guided sparse path that expands queries with legally informative keywords, and a structure-guided dense path trained with hard negatives derived from legal hierarchy and citation relations. Then, the reranking module further refines the candidate ranking by incorporating the intent consistency score between queries and legal articles. We evaluate \textsc{LexPath} on two publicly available benchmarks focusing on general-public queries and a self-constructed benchmark targeting domain-professional scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that \textsc{LexPath} consistently outperforms lexical, dense, hybrid, and adaptive retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) baselines. Ablation studies further verify the effectiveness of each component.

</details>


### [92] [GRASP: Plan-Guided Graph Retrieval with Adaptive Fusion and Reranking on Semi-Structured Knowledge Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30237)
*Yicheng Tao,Yiqun Wang,Xiangchen Song,Xin Luo,Kai Liu,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GRASP 是一个针对半结构化知识库的新型三阶段检索框架，通过融合计划驱动的图检索、条件化融合与重排序增强，显著提升了复杂信息检索任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 SKB 检索系统在利用异构数据（文本与图结构）时存在局限性，如仅将图用于查询扩展、简单的全局加权融合或依赖单一的图遍历生成器，难以有效平衡两类信息的优势。

Method: 提出名为 GRASP 的三阶段检索框架：(1) 基于计划的图检索 (plan-based graph retrieval)；(2) 计划条件下的密集检索融合 (plan-conditioned fusion)；(3) 对融合后的候选集进行微调重排序 (fine-tuned reranker)。

Result: 在 STaRK 基准测试的所有指标上均优于现有的前沿模型，将平均 Hit@1 指标从 62.0 提升到了 73.9，且消融实验证明了该框架的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GRASP 通过引入三阶段检索框架，有效融合了结构化图数据与非结构化文本数据，显著提升了半结构化知识库（SKB）检索任务的性能，并在 STaRK 基准测试中实现了大幅超越现有技术的成果。

Abstract: Semi-structured knowledge bases (SKBs) embed textual documents in a typed graph of entities and relations, and underpin applications such as product search, academic paper search, and precision-medicine inquiries. Existing hybrid retrieval systems on SKBs either use the graph only for query expansion, mix textual and structural branches under a global weighting, or rely on fine-tuned graph-traversal generators. We present GRASP, a three-stage SKB retrieval framework unifying plan-based graph retrieval, plan-conditioned fusion with a dense retriever, and a fine-tuned reranker over the fused candidates. GRASP substantially advances the state of the art on every metric across the three STaRK benchmarks, lifting average Hit@1 from 62.0 to 73.9. Ablation and sensitivity studies further confirm the effectiveness and robustness of GRASP.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Conf-Gen: Conformal Uncertainty Quantification for Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28920)
*Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem,Kevin Zhang,Wei Cui,Marc T. Law,Kin Kwan Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Conformal Generation (Conf-Gen) 框架，首次成功将共形风险控制（CRC）扩展至 LLM 及图像生成器等生成式模型，为生成式 AI 提供了严谨的统计可靠性保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的共形预测（CP）和共形风险控制（CRC）框架主要针对监督学习，而目前 AI 领域的核心进展（如 LLM 和生成模型）属于非监督学习范畴，两者在理论上不直接兼容，亟需一种能为生成式 AI 提供不确定性量化的通用框架。

Method: 引入了 Conformal Generation (Conf-Gen) 框架，通过放宽 CRC 的理论假设，使其能够适配生成式任务。该方法通过将生成过程中的属性转化为可衡量的风险指标，从而实现对生成输出的统计保证。

Result: 通过实验验证了 Conf-Gen 的灵活性，实现了多个前沿应用：包括确保图像生成器输出非记忆图像、确保对话式 AI 提出足够的澄清问题，以及确保 AI 智能体的输出结果是正确的。

Conclusion: Conf-Gen 是一个通用的框架，成功将一致性风险控制（CRC）扩展至非监督生成模型领域，在保证理论约束的前提下，为大语言模型和图像生成器等 AI 系统的可靠性评估提供了严谨的统计学方法。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) and its extension, conformal risk control (CRC), are established frameworks for quantifying uncertainty in supervised machine learning through formal guarantees. However, recent breakthroughs in artificial intelligence (AI) have been driven by unsupervised generative models, such as large language models (LLMs) and image generators, which are not directly compatible with CP or CRC. In this work we introduce conformal generation (Conf-Gen), a general framework adapting CRC to generative tasks while relaxing its theoretical assumptions. Conf-Gen unifies and generalizes previous attempts to apply CP to LLMs, and extends conformal methodology to entirely new domains. We demonstrate the flexibility of Conf-Gen through some novel applications, including obtaining conformal guarantees on: image generators producing non-memorized images, conversational AI systems having asked enough clarifying questions, and the output of AI agents being correct.

</details>


### [94] [Q-ANCHOR: Federated Quantum Learning with ZNE-guided Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30075)
*Hoang M. Ngo,Quan Nguyen,Wanli Xing,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对量子联邦学习在实际硬件中因数据非独立同分布（non-IID）和噪声导致的“双重漂移”问题，本文提出了 Q-ANCHOR 算法，通过结合零噪声外推和状态化校准，显著提升了训练稳定性和模型准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子联邦学习中常用的 FedAvg 算法在面对现实设备时存在“双重漂移”问题（即客户端数据分布不均与量子硬件噪声引起的梯度偏差），导致训练过程出现持久的误差底限和不稳定。

Method: 提出 Q-ANCHOR 架构，结合了零噪声外推（Zero-Noise Extrapolation, ZNE）技术对服务器更新进行锚定，并引入状态化客户端校准机制，以同时抑制客户端数据偏移和硬件诱导的梯度偏差。

Result: 实验表明，与传统的联邦学习基准相比，Q-ANCHOR 实现了更稳定的模型训练，并在理论上证明了其能够有效降低由硬件偏差引起的误差底限。

Conclusion: Q-ANCHOR 有效解决了非独立同分布（non-IID）数据导致的客户端偏移以及量子硬件噪声带来的偏差问题，为实际硬件环境下的量子联邦学习提供了理论支撑和性能更优的算法框架。

Abstract: Quantum Federated Learning (QFL) offers a promising framework to train quantum models across distributed clients while keeping data strictly local. Due to its simplicity and low communication overhead, Federated Averaging (FedAvg) is the standard aggregation choice in QFL literature. However, deploying QFL on practical hardware exposes a severe double-drift phenomenon: the global model is simultaneously derailed by client drift from non-IID data and hardware bias from noisy quantum gradient estimates. In this work, we first analyze the convergence of FedAvg under these realistic conditions, mathematically demonstrating that quantum hardware bias creates a persistent error floor that standard averaging cannot correct. To overcome this limitation, we propose Q-ANCHOR, a quantum-aware federated aggregation architecture that anchors server updates with zero-noise extrapolation while applying stateful client correction to suppress both client drift and hardware-induced bias. Our convergence theory proves that Q-ANCHOR successfully mitigates classical client drift while actively reducing the hardware-bias floor. Experimental results demonstrate that Q-ANCHOR achieves significantly more stable training than conventional FL baselines.

</details>


### [95] [PROTOCOL: Late Interaction Retrieval for Protein Homolog Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29158)
*Gabrielle Cohn,Rohan Gumaste,Minh Hoang,Vihan Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProtoCol 通过引入基于残基级嵌入的“后期交互”检索机制，解决了蛋白质语言模型在处理远程同源性搜索时因均值池化导致的局部特征丢失问题，显著提升了同源检索的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质同源性搜索在“弱同源性”（twilight zone）区域表现不佳；传统的基于蛋白质语言模型（PLM）的检索通常采用池化（Pooling）策略，将序列压缩为单一向量，导致局部基序（motifs）、结构域或关键保守残基的特征丢失。

Method: 提出一种名为 ProtoCol 的模型，将蛋白质表示为残基嵌入（residue embeddings）集合，并在检索阶段采用 ColBERT 风格的后期交互机制（即 MaxSim 操作），逐个比较残基向量而非压缩成单一向量。该方法支持候选集预计算，保持了检索效率。

Result: 在 SCOPe 超家族和 Pfam 家族基准测试中，ProtoCol 的性能显著优于传统序列比对方法、池化型 PLM 方法以及其他单向量基线模型。

Conclusion: “后期交互”（Late Interaction）机制在蛋白质远程同源性搜索中表现优越，能够有效克服传统序列比对和池化表征方法的局限性，是提升远程同源性识别能力的有效手段。

Abstract: Protein homology search underlies function annotation, structure prediction, and evolutionary analysis, but remains challenging in the "twilight zone," where global sequence similarity is weak and classical alignment methods lose sensitivity. Protein language models provide context-aware representations that could improve alignment sensitivity in this regime. However, prior protein embedding-based retrieval pipelines often pool these representations into a single vector, potentially obscuring local motifs, domains, or conserved residues that reveal remote homology. We introduce ProtoCol, a model which represents proteins as sets of residue embeddings and uses ColBERT-style late interaction to test whether residue-level comparison improves homolog retrieval. ProtoCol encodes proteins independently, keeps candidate representations pre-computable, and scores candidates with MaxSim over residue embeddings. On SCOPe superfamily and Pfam clan benchmarks, ProtoCol outperforms sequence-composition, alignment-based, pooled PLM, and trained single-vector baselines, supporting late interaction as an effective retrieval layer for remote homology search.

</details>


### [96] [Optimal Gap-Dependent Regret for Private Stochastic Decision-Theoretic Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29148)
*Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文解决了随机决策在线学习在纯事件级差分隐私下的最优遗憾界问题，提出了一个与视界无关的算法，实现了理论上的最优遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决COLT公开问题：确定在纯事件级差分隐私（pure event-level DP）约束下，随机决策理论在线学习的最优依赖于间隙（gap-dependent）的遗憾界，即达到O((log K/Δ_min) + (log K/ε))的复杂度。

Method: 提出了一种新的算法，采用指数增长的时间块划分策略；在每个时间块内仅采取单一动作；利用数据无关的随机前缀（random prefix）应用指数机制来选择下一时间块的动作；并通过熵势（entropy-potential）分析方法来控制隐私开销带来的遗憾。

Result: 给出了一个与视界无关的纯DP算法，并证明了对于任意视界T，其遗憾界满足 Reg_T ≤ 1000 * ((log K/Δ_min) + (log K/ε))。

Conclusion: 作者成功解决了Hu和Mehta提出的关于随机决策理论在线学习中纯事件级差分隐私的最优遗憾界问题，证明了存在与视界无关（horizon-free）的算法，其遗憾界达到了下界理论的最优形式。

Abstract: We study stochastic decision-theoretic online learning with full information and event-level pure differential privacy. A COLT open problem of Hu and Mehta asks to determine the optimal gap-dependent regret rate for stochastic decision-theoretic online learning under pure event-level differential privacy. For $K$ actions, losses in $[0,1]$, and a unique best action separated from the second-best action by gap $Δ_{\min}$, the known lower bound is of order $
  \frac{\log K}{\min\{Δ_{\min},\varepsilon\}}, $
or equivalently, up to universal constants, of order \[
  \frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}. \] We give a horizon-free pure-DP algorithm and prove the explicit regret bound \[
  \operatorname{Reg}_T
  \le
  1000 \cdot \left(\frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}\right) \] for every horizon $T$. The numerical constant is not optimized. The algorithm partitions time into blocks of exponentially increasing size, plays a single action throughout each block, and chooses the next action by an exponential mechanism applied to a data-independent random prefix of the previous block. The random prefix converts block regret into a sum, over all prefix lengths, of softmax selection errors. A single entropy-potential argument controls all privacy-dominated large-gap actions at cost $\log K/\varepsilon$.

</details>


### [97] [Attention as In-Context Empirical Bayes: A Two-Stage View via Particle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29351)
*Matthew Smart,Soumya Ganguly,Nilava Metya,Alexandre V. Morozov,Anirvan M. Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将注意力机制解释为一种两阶段的“经验贝叶斯推断”过程，即通过深度细化经验分布并在残差连接中完成推断，无需显式建模即可实现接近最优的上下文推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从统计学角度理解注意力Transformer为何能够在上下文中进行推理（in-context inference），特别是在无需显式密度建模的情况下，其内在的动力学机制是什么。

Method: 构造了针对“全令牌损坏（all-token corruption）”的极小注意力Transformer模型，应用经验贝叶斯（Empirical Bayes）解释框架，并利用粒子动力学分析深度对分布的影响。

Result: 1. 表明单步注意力计算的是基于经验分布的核加权后验均值；2. 深度起到细化分布的作用（粒子动力学），残差连接执行后验推断；3. 在特定条件下，经验估计器渐近收敛于贝叶斯最优预测器；4. 证明Transformer深度与推断的噪声水平存在内在的对应关系。

Conclusion: 本文将注意力机制解释为一种基于样本的后验估计过程（即上下文推理），揭示了深度与注意力残差在统计学上的不同作用，证明了无需显式噪声调度也能实现有效去噪，并建立了其与反向扩散过程的联系。

Abstract: We study minimal attention-only transformers under all-token corruption and show they admit a two-stage empirical Bayes interpretation. A single attention step computes a kernel-weighted posterior mean with respect to the empirical distribution defined by the context. Depth refines this distribution through particle dynamics (Stage 1), while a long-range skip-connection carries the noisy input as a query for posterior inference (Stage 2), revealing distinct statistical roles for depth and attention residuals. The framework isolates a minimal setting in which the context itself induces a depth-dependent energy landscape governing in-context inference. We show that effective denoising can emerge without an explicit noise schedule: a fixed kernel bandwidth and finite integration horizon suffice, yielding a principled depth-noise relationship. We further establish a posterior-mean recovery guarantee for a class of well-behaved priors, where the empirical estimator converges to the Bayes-optimal predictor under asymptotic conditions. Connecting these dynamics to reverse-diffusion limits, our results provide a statistical interpretation of attention as in-context inference via sample-based posterior estimation, without explicit density modeling.

</details>


### [98] [On the Optimizer Dependence of Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29387)
*Vansh Ramani,Shourya Vir Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示了神经缩放定律中的缩放指数α并非固定常数，而是取决于优化器的选择；通过使用预处理优化器，可以显著提升模型性能随参数量增加的缩放效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对常被视为固定常数的神经缩放指数α，探讨它是否会随优化器的变化而改变，以及不同优化器（如自然梯度）对模型参数缩放效率的具体影响程度。

Method: 在受控的随机特征回归（random-feature regression）框架下，跨越5种不同优化器变体和6种谱条件（spectral conditions）进行对比实验，测量各条件下的缩放指数α。

Result: 预处理优化器能持续产生更陡峭的缩放曲线（更大的α）；在谱指数s≈1.0时，自然梯度的α≈0.31，远高于梯度下降的α≈0.12，且该差异随模型规模增大而产生复合效应。研究还发现这种性能提升在特定谱条件（如s=1.5附近）达到峰值。

Conclusion: 神经缩放定律中的缩放指数α并非由架构和数据决定的固定常数，而是高度依赖于优化器。研究表明，利用自然梯度等预处理优化器可以显著增大α（即获得更好的缩放表现），且该提升在特定的谱条件下最为明显。建议在预测缩放规律时必须考虑优化器因素，并可通过文中的谱诊断方法评估高级优化器的收益。

Abstract: The scaling exponent $α$ in neural scaling laws $L(N) \propto N^{-α}$ is commonly treated as a fixed constant set by architecture and data. We present evidence that $α$ depends systematically on the optimizer. In controlled random-feature regression experiments -- the canonical theoretical framework for neural scaling -- we measure $α$ across five optimizer variants and six spectral conditions. Preconditioned optimizers consistently yield steeper scaling (larger $α$), with the $α$-shift increasing across most of the tested spectral range, peaking near $s = 1.5$, and remaining large at $s = 2.0$. At $s \approx 1.0$ (characteristic of natural language), the full natural gradient achieves $α\approx 0.31$ versus $α\approx 0.12$ for gradient descent -- a $2.6\times$ larger fitted exponent that, within the random-feature model, compounds with each model-size doubling. Whether and how this exponent shift transfers to large-scale LLM training -- where recent evidence suggests the advantage may attenuate with scale -- remains an important open question. Our results imply that scaling-law forecasts should account for optimizer choice, and we provide a spectral diagnostic predicting when advanced optimizers will pay off.

</details>


### [99] [LoopFM: Learning frOm HistOrical RePresentations of Foundation Model for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29280)
*Shali Jiang,Hua Zheng,Boyang Liu,Laming Chen,Kenny Lov,Chuanqi Xu,Lisang Ding,Qinghai Zhou,Can Cui,Xiaolong Liu,Xiaoyi Liu,Yasmine Badr,Xin Xu,Jiyan Yang,Ellie Dingqiao Wen,Gerard Jonathan Mugisha Akkerhuis,Chenxiao Guan,Rong Jin,Ruichao Qiu,Xian Chen,Shifu Xu,Zhehui Zhou,Ping Chen,Rui Yang,Haicheng Chen,Xiangge Meng,Song Zhou,Dharak Kharod,Shuyu Xu,Qiang Jin,Qiao Yang,Wankun Zhu,Qin Huang,Yuzhen Huang,Darren Liu,Parish Aggarwal,Hui Zhou,Erzhuo Wang,Shuo Chang,Xiaorui Gan,Wenlin Chen,Santanu Kolay,Huayu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对知识蒸馏中信息传递带宽不足的问题，LoopFM 提出将大型模型中间表征作为下游特征输入，在保持架构解耦的同时，显著提升了知识迁移效率和下游应用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏（KD）仅利用单一标量预测进行迁移，导致模型间知识传递受限，丢失了基础模型（FM）中蕴含的丰富中间特征，造成“传递比”（transfer ratio）随着 FM 能力增强而降低。

Method: 提出 LoopFM 框架：将基础模型（FM）的中间嵌入（intermediate embeddings）转化为下游模型（VM）的输入特征（例如用户历史序列）。该方法解耦了 FM 和 VM 的架构，且无需在推理阶段进行实时 FM 推理。同时，作者推导了增益分解和传递比分析的理论框架。

Result: 在三个公开基准测试中，AUC 提升显著（如 TaobaoAd 提升 6%+）；在工业级超大规模系统中，与 KD 叠加使用时，知识传递比翻倍，并在实际业务中取得了 +0.5% 至 +1.22% 的转化率提升。

Conclusion: LoopFM 有效克服了传统知识蒸馏中“高维损失”带来的传递比递减问题。通过利用基础模型（FM）的历史表征，LoopFM 实现了高效且解耦的知识迁移，证明了将 FM 中间数据作为下游模型输入特征的显著价值。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers a single scalar prediction from a large foundation model (FM) to compact vertical models (VMs), suffering from diminishing transfer ratio -- the fraction of FM improvement captured by the VM -- as a single scalar cannot convey the rich intermediate knowledge that larger FMs learn. To address this bottleneck, we propose LoopFM (Learning frOm HistOrical ReP*resentations of FM), a framework that opens a high-bandwidth transfer channel by structuring FM intermediate embeddings as input features (e.g., user history sequence) for downstream VMs, without requiring real-time FM inference at serving and architectural coupling between FM and VM. We provide a theoretical framework for LoopFM with a gain decomposition and transfer-ratio analysis. On three public benchmarks, LoopFM demonstrates strong AUC improvements (e.g., 6\%+ on TaobaoAd) and complementary knowledge transfer capability with KD. On industrial-scale systems (billions of examples, trillion-parameter FMs), LoopFM approximately doubles the knowledge transfer ratio on top of KD, delivering a +0.5\% conversion improvement in Y1H1, and a +1.03\% and +1.22\% conversion improvement from two individual launches respectively in Y1H2.

</details>


### [100] [The Good, the Bad, and the Ugly of Markov Boundary for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29411)
*Shu Wan,Abhinav Gorantla,Huan Liu,K. Selçuk Candan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 尽管马尔可夫边界在理论上是特征选择的最优解，但受限于现有因果发现算法的准确性和计算开销，直接将其应用于表格预测往往并不比使用全量特征更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨如何在表格预测任务中有效利用马尔可夫边界（Markov boundary）来简化特征集，并分析为何现有的因果发现驱动的特征选择难以在实际中超越包含全量特征的模型。

Method: 在包含3450个任务的合成基准数据集（SCM3K）上，对比了在已知“Oracle”边界及通过现有算法恢复的边界下的模型表现，并分析了六种不同回归模型在不同特征空间规模下的预测性能。

Result: 研究发现，虽然利用Oracle边界能显著提升大规模稀疏数据下的预测表现，但通过因果发现算法获取的边界往往因计算效率低下及结构识别偏差，导致预测性能欠佳，甚至不如直接使用全量特征。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫边界理论上对预测有价值，但现有的因果发现算法在实际应用中难以胜过直接使用全量特征。主要原因在于发现算法偏向结构恢复而非预测优化、错误分类的代价不对称，以及存在许多优于“最小边界”的特征组合。未来的研究应聚焦于“面向预测（prediction-aligned）”的特征选择方法。

Abstract: Under standard graphical assumptions, the Markov boundary of a target variable is the smallest set of features that renders every other feature redundant. Once the boundary is observed, the target is conditionally independent of the rest of the table. This is a tempting object for tabular prediction, since it names exactly the columns a model should need. Yet modern regressors are still trained on the full feature set. We ask whether the Markov boundary is genuinely useful for prediction on SCM3K, a 3,450-task synthetic SCM benchmark with feature counts from 40 to 1000 and six SCM families, evaluated with six regressors. The answer is more nuanced than the theory suggests. Restricting a regressor to the oracle boundary often improves prediction substantially, and the improvement grows as the feature space becomes larger and sparser. But the natural pipeline of recovering the boundary with causal discovery and training on the recovered mask does not deliver. Existing estimators exhaust the compute budget before reaching the regime where the boundary helps most, and even where they run they rarely beat the full feature set. We trace this to three causes. Discovery optimizes structural recovery rather than prediction. False negatives and false positives carry sharply asymmetric predictive cost. The exact boundary is only one of many feature sets that beat all features. We then develop what these facts imply for prediction-aligned feature selection and for tabular models that learn to use causal structure.

</details>


### [101] [On the Construction and Implications of Low-Loss Valleys in LoRA-based Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29580)
*Daniel Dold,Emanuel Sommer,Julius Kobialka,Oliver Dürr,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRA-Curve 通过在 LoRA 参数空间内构建分段贝塞尔曲线，成功连接了独立的模型最优解，从而有效估算大模型的认知不确定性并增强了预测的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 LoRA 已成为大模型微调的标准，但其认知不确定性估计仍具挑战。现有研究认为 LoRA 空间缺乏深层模型在权重空间中常见的“低损失谷”连接结构，导致离散集成（如深层集成）效果不佳。该研究旨在探究 LoRA 空间是否存在此类结构，以及能否通过连接最优解来获得更好的性能。

Method: 提出了 LoRA-Curve，这是一种在 LoRA 参数空间内的分段贝塞尔曲线参数化方法。包含两种变体：1）自由配置（联合优化所有控制点）；2）锚定配置（连接独立微调的 LoRA 最优解），并结合了平坦极小值扰动与 Jensen-Shannon 分度正则化器。

Result: 实验表明，线性插值在 LoRA 空间中存在明显的损失壁垒，而 anchored LoRA-Curve 能成功连接独立的 LoRA 最优解通过连续低损失路径。此方法在 Qwen2.5 7B 模型上的推理和分类基准中，显著提升了预测分布的互信息并增强了函数多样性。

Conclusion: LoRA-Curve 证明了在 LoRA 参数空间内存在连续的低损失路径，通过连接独立的 LoRA 最优解，能够有效地捕捉函数多样性，从而在不牺牲性能的前提下提升了模型预测分布的互信息，为大模型的认知不确定性估计提供了新的方法论。

Abstract: While parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods like low-rank adaptation (LoRA) are standard for large language models, principled estimation of epistemic uncertainty remains challenging. Recent results in the LoRA regime suggest that discrete multi-mode approaches such as deep ensembles offer little benefit over single-mode methods. This contradicts broader observations in deep learning, where ensembling independent optima typically improves generalization, and linking these modes through continuous low-loss valleys further enhances Bayesian model averaging (BMA). Whether such structure exists in the LoRA space and whether it yields functional diversity missed by local or discrete methods has not been studied. We introduce LoRA-Curve, a segmented Bézier curve parameterization in the LoRA space, with two variants: a free configuration that jointly optimizes all control points, and an anchored configuration that connects independently fine-tuned LoRA optima. We prove pathwise continuity and Lipschitz regularity of the loss along the curve and empirically show, across reasoning and classification benchmarks with Qwen2.5 7B, that linear interpolation encounters loss barriers, while our anchored multi-segment curves connect independent optima through continuous low-loss valleys. Combined with flat-minima perturbations and a Jensen-Shannon divergence regularizer, LoRA-Curve yields measurably higher mutual information of the predictive distribution without sacrificing performance, and links continuous parameter-space traversal to functional diversity.

</details>


### [102] [The Sample Complexity of Multiclass and Sparse Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29645)
*Liad Erez,Fan Chen,Alon Cohen,Tomer Koren,Yishay Mansour,Shay Moran,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对 $s$-稀疏上下文 bandit 问题，通过 DEC 分析和低方差探索方法，在处理大规模动作空间时，打破了以往研究中对动作空间大小 $|A|$ 的极高次幂依赖，获得了近乎最优的样本复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在上下文 bandit 问题中，传统的样本复杂度分析在处理大规模动作空间时存在较大的改进空间。本文旨在解决 $s$-稀疏奖励条件下的效率问题，即奖励向量的 $L_1$ 范数远小于动作空间大小时，如何实现更高效的策略学习。

Method: 1. 基于决策估计系数 (DEC) 的信息论方法：通过证明 $s$-稀疏条件下模型类具有更优的 DEC 界，从而推导出样本复杂度上界。 2. 基于低方差探索 (Low-variance exploration) 的算法方法：设计了具体且可计算的算法，该方法可进一步扩展至上下文组合半监督 bandit 问题。

Result: 针对函数类 $\Pi$，在 $s$-稀疏约束下实现了 $\tilde{O}((s/\epsilon^2 + |A|/\epsilon)\log|\Pi|/\delta)$ 的样本复杂度，并证明了该界在 Natarajan 类中是近乎最优的。同时，将该方法成功推广至上下文组合半监督 bandit 问题。

Conclusion: 本文建立的样本复杂度上界与下界（在对数因子内）相匹配，显著改进了此前文献中对动作空间基数 $|A|$ 的高阶多项式依赖，确立了 $s$-稀疏设置下的最优统计界。

Abstract: We study contextual bandits in the stochastic i.i.d.\ setting, where a learner observes contexts drawn from an unknown distribution, selects actions from a finite set $A$, and aims to identify an approximately optimal policy from a given class based on bandit feedback. Motivated by bandit multiclass classification with zero-one rewards, we focus on the \emph{$s$-sparse} setting in which, for every context, the reward vector has $L_1$-norm at most $s \ll |A|$. Our main result is the design of algorithms that, with high probability, output an $ε$-optimal policy compared to policy class $Π$ using $\tilde{O} ((s/ε^2 + |A|/ε)\log |Π|/δ)$ samples. We extend this bound to general Natarajan classes and complement it with a matching lower bound (up to logarithmic factors), thereby closing a substantial gap left by prior work (Erez et al., 2024, 2025), which incurred an additional $Θ(|A|^9)$ dependence. We obtain these results via two complementary approaches. First, we analyze contextual bandits through the lens of contextual decision making with structured observations, designing an exploration-by-optimization algorithm whose sample complexity is governed by the \emph{decision-estimation coefficient} (DEC; Foster et al., 2021, 2022). We show that, with $s$-sparse rewards, the induced model class admits a sharp DEC bound that scales with $s$ and directly yields the optimal rate. Since this approach is largely information-theoretic and involves solving complex min-max optimization problems, we also develop a second, more specialized algorithmic method based on a low-variance exploration technique. This approach leads to concrete, tractable algorithms and naturally extends to contextual combinatorial semi-bandits, leading to improved sample complexity guarantees for bandit multiclass list classification.

</details>


### [103] [SCOPE: A Lightweight-training LLM Framework for Air Traffic Control Readback Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29543)
*Qihan Deng,Minghua Zhang,Yang Yang,Zhenyu Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 ATC 复诵差错问题，本文提出 SCOPE 框架，通过冻结 LLM 并引入插件式分类器与上下文学习，实现了高效、高精、低时延且具备可解释性的航空指令监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空管制（ATC）语音复诵纠错对于飞行安全至关重要，但现有传统方法与机器学习模型在复杂的航空短语及高负载环境下泛化能力不足；此外，现有大模型方案存在计算成本高、部署困难等问题，需要一种更高效、准确且可解释的自动化监控方案。

Method: 提出了 SCOPE (Semantic reasoning for Communication via Open-set Plug-in with Examples) 框架：1. 使用冻结的 LLM 作为基座；2. 结合即插即用的开放集分类器；3. 采用精心设计的上下文学习（In-context Learning）机制。该方法无需对大模型进行高成本微调，属于轻量级训练框架。

Result: 在半合成语料数据集上，SCOPE 在少样本（few-shot）设置下实现了 91.05% 的开放集检测准确率，并能纠正 96.63% 的异常复诵，在保持极低延迟的同时优于现有基准模型。

Conclusion: SCOPE 框架通过冻结 LLM 结合即插即用型开放集分类器与上下文学习（ICL），在 ATC 复诵监控任务中实现了高准确度、低延迟与可解释性，为航空安全中自动化监测提供了切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Pilot readback of Air Traffic Control (ATC) voice instructions is a primary safeguard against miscommunication in air transportation. However, readback anomalies remain implicated in approximately 80% of aviation incidents. This vulnerability is further exacerbated by rising traffic volume and elevated cognitive workload, thereby motivating automated readback monitoring by machine. Traditional rule-based and machine learning approaches struggle to generalize across the highly variable and evolving phraseology of air traffic controller-pilot communications. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have opened a new avenue through their strong reasoning and generalization capabilities, existing approaches still face deployment and computational barriers in practice. In this work, we propose Semantic reasoning for Communication via Open-set Plug-in with Examples (SCOPE), a novel lightweight-training LLM framework that advances both the efficiency and accuracy of machine-based ATC readback monitoring. The core idea is to couple a plug-in open-set classifier with a carefully designed in-context learning mechanism on top of a frozen LLM. Extensive experiments on the semi-synthetic communication dataset show that SCOPE attains superior accuracy while delivering the low-latency response required for operational environments. Under a few-shot setting, SCOPE achieves 91.05% accuracy in open-set detection and corrects 96.63% of anomalous readbacks, thereby outperforming the strongest available baselines while providing explanations for its decisions. These findings demonstrate the potential of our framework as a practical pathway toward interpretable and controllable ATC readback monitoring.

</details>


### [104] [CB-SLICE: Concept-Based Interpretable Error Slice Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29836)
*Yael Konforti,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Elaf Almahmoud,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CB-SLICE，这是一种基于概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）的错误切片发现方法，它通过分析语义概念的预测失败来精确识别模型错误切片，从而实现比现有方法更准确、更具解释性的模型调试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有错误切片发现方法（SDMs）生成的解释往往与模型的推理过程脱节，导致其对错误源的定位仅能作为近似，缺乏准确性和深入的分析，限制了模型调试的有效性。

Method: 基于 Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs)，提出 CB-SLICE 方法。该方法利用 CBMs 中人类可理解的语义概念作为桥梁，通过将具有共同概念预测失败模式的样本进行归类，并识别导致切片失效的核心关键词概念，从而挖掘模型错误切片及其根本原因。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，CB-SLICE 不仅在发现已知模型偏差方面优于现有的 SOTA 方法，而且能够提供更丰富、更符合模型实际推理过程的错误原因解释。

Conclusion: CB-SLICE 通过利用 Concept Bottleneck Models 的可解释性结构，有效地弥补了现有切片发现方法（SDMs）在解释深度学习模型错误源时的准确性不足，为模型调试和偏差发现提供了更具忠实度和细粒度的分析工具。

Abstract: Despite strong average-case performance, deep learning models often exhibit systematic errors on specific population groups, known as error slices. Identifying these groups and the root causes of their failures is critical for model debugging and bias mitigation. However, existing error Slice Discovery Methods (SDMs) typically generate explanations disconnected from the model's inference process, thus only approximating the underlying error source and may be inaccurate.
  We address this limitation by leveraging Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs), whose predictions are directly dependent on human-understandable semantic concepts. Since downstream task failures in CBMs commonly arise from concept mispredictions, concept representations provide a strong candidate for error slice identification, offering fine-grained explanations directly linked to the error source. Building on this insight, we introduce CB-SLICE, a concept-based SDM that groups samples with shared concept prediction failures and identifies the keyword concepts most responsible for each slice's failure mode. Across multiple benchmarks, we show that CB-SLICE outperforms state-of-the-art methods in uncovering well-known biases while providing richer and more faithful explanations of model errors.

</details>


### [105] [Open Problem: Separating Geometric and Algorithmic Compression via Cayley-Table Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29885)
*Dongsung Huh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 深度学习缺乏对离散算法规则的归纳偏置；本文提出用 Cayley 表补全作为测试基准，利用张量分解和平坦性先验引导模型对离散代数公理的精确学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习的连续性容量控制（如范数正则化）在连续领域表现出色，但在推演精确算法或离散代数规则时往往失败，这揭示了模型缺乏针对“算法复杂度最小化”的归纳偏置。

Method: 提出以 Cayley 表补全作为测试离散算法归纳偏置的基准；利用算子值张量分解结合平坦性先验（flatness prior）来诱导对精确离散结合律的隐式算法偏置。

Result: 指出了算子值张量分解在实现离散结合律学习上的潜力，并定义了 Cayley 表补全作为离散代数恢复问题的标准测试任务。

Conclusion: 现有的连续性归纳偏置在处理离散代数规则（如 Cayley 表补全）时存在缺陷，未来需要研究如何将平坦性先验推广至发现离散算法公理，以实现无需组合搜索的精确恢复。

Abstract: Modern statistical learning theory and deep learning characterize generalization primarily in terms of continuous capacity control (e.g., norm-based regularization, margin maximization, low-rank bias). While highly successful in continuous domains, deep learning consistently fails to extrapolate exact algorithmic or discrete algebraic rules, reflecting a missing inductive bias toward algorithmic complexity minimization. We propose the Cayley-table completion as the canonical testbed for this missing bias, serving as the discrete algebraic counterpart to matrix completion. Just as matrix factorization combined with weight decay yields an implicit geometric bias toward low linear rank, recent results demonstrate that operator-valued tensor factorizations paired with a flatness prior yield an implicit algorithmic bias toward exact discrete associativity. We pose the open problem of establishing formal exact recovery bounds for Cayley-table completion, and challenge the community to generalize continuous flatness priors to autonomously discover broader discrete algorithmic axioms without combinatorial search.

</details>


### [106] [Ridge Regression from Poisson Resetting: A Renewal Perspective on Spectral Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30059)
*Petar Jolakoski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将随机重置过程与岭回归建立数学映射，证明了重置后的线性梯度流收敛于岭估计，揭示了正则化可以在统计物理动力学中以“重置”形式自然演化，并分析了不同重置分布对谱滤波效果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨非平衡统计物理中的随机重置动力学如何通过动力学行为产生类似统计学习中正则化的效果，旨在通过物理机制理解机器学习中常用算法（如岭回归）的收敛与平稳特性。

Method: 1. 将线性梯度下降流（Gradient Flow）与泊松重置过程相结合，推导出其平稳分布均值与岭回归估计量之间的数学等价关系；2. 利用拉普拉斯变换确定重置率与正则化参数的对应；3. 将指数重置扩展至一般更新（Renewal）重置定律；4. 在Ornstein-Uhlenbeck（OU）噪声环境下引入随机梯度下降（SGD）近似分析其波动和协方差特性。

Result: 1. 证明了重置率为 $r$ 的线性梯度流的平稳均值等同于正则化参数为 $\lambda=r$ 的岭回归估计量；2. 指数重置率是唯一能在各特征方向上严格重现岭回归谱滤波器的更新法则；3. 指出了在引入噪声的SGD近似下，均值层面虽保持一致，但重置过程会产生非零的平稳协方差。

Conclusion: 该研究通过建立随机重置（Stochastic Resetting）与岭回归（Ridge Regression）的等价性，揭示了非平衡统计物理与统计学习之间的深层联系，并证明了指数重置过程在产生岭估计方面的唯一性，同时也探讨了非指数重置法则作为替代谱滤波器的潜力。

Abstract: We connect stochastic resetting from non-equilibrium statistical physics with ridge regularization in statistical learning. For linear gradient flow, resetting to the origin at rate $r$ produces stationary mean $(X^\top X+rI)^{-1}X^\top y$, exactly the ridge estimator with penalty $λ=r$. This uses the known Laplace-transform relationship between ridge regression and exponential-time averaging of gradient flow, with the exponential time now interpreted as the stationary age associated with Poisson resetting. We then extend this identity to general renewal reset laws: the exponential reset time distribution is the unique renewal law whose stationary mean reproduces scalar ridge in every eigendirection as an exact filter identity for every positive curvature, while non-exponential renewal laws generate alternative spectral filters. At the fluctuation level, we study a separate additive Ornstein-Uhlenbeck extension with constant diffusion, interpreted as a stylized SGD approximation. In this setting, the equality holds only at the level of the mean, since the reset process has a nonzero stationary covariance from accumulated OU noise and reset-timing variance, whereas deterministic ridge is a fixed estimator with the same center. Stylized experiments compare the deterministic renewal-induced filters directly and illustrate when filters induced by non-exponential reset-time laws can differ predictively from ridge. The results for the stationary mean and the induced spectral filters are established for continuous-time gradient flow with isotropic resetting on quadratic objectives; the covariance and risk formulas additionally assume additive noise with state-independent covariance.

</details>


### [107] [Learning to Extrapolate to New Tasks: A Relational Approach to Task Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30132)
*Adam Ousherovitch,Yixin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RTE 算法通过学习“任务间的关系转换”而非仅仅拟合数据，使模型能够利用已知任务的转换逻辑，实现对训练分布之外的新任务进行结构化外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习系统虽然擅长内插（Interpolation），但在处理超出训练分布范围的未见任务（如参数外推、长度外推等）时表现较差，即便使用基础模型（Foundation Models）也难以解决这一系统的泛化难题。

Method: 提出了一种名为“关系任务外推器”（Relational Task Extrapolator, RTE）的算法。其核心是将目标任务分解为“已知锚点任务”和“连接锚点与目标的转换算子”，通过学习这种转换关系，实现从已知任务向未知任务的推演。

Result: 在函数预测和序列预测等多项实验中，RTE 在参数外推、长度外推和组合外推等任务上均显著优于现有基准方法。

Conclusion: RTE 通过学习任务间的转换关系，成功将任务外推（Extrapolation）问题转化为任务转换的发现与重用问题，有效地提升了模型在参数、长度和组合性外推场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Modern learning systems excel at interpolation but struggle to generalize to unseen tasks outside the training distribution's support. This failure occurs even in simple settings, such as handling task parameters beyond the training range, and persists despite advances in foundation models. To this end, we develop the Relational Task Extrapolator (RTE), an algorithm designed to enable systematic extrapolation to novel tasks. The key observation is that extrapolation is inherently relational: extrapolating to unseen tasks requires learning how tasks transform into one another. If a model learns the transformation between tasks A and B during training, it can apply that same transformation to relate known tasks to unseen ones at test time. RTE operationalizes this idea by decomposing each target task into a known anchor task and a transformation linking the anchor and target. It then learns a relational operator, mapping an anchor-transformation pair to predictions for the target task. We instantiate RTE across multiple task extrapolation regimes in function prediction, e.g. where target tasks use out-of-range parameters (parameter extrapolation), have greater compositional depth (length extrapolation), and/or recombine function primitives in unseen ways (compositional extrapolation). We further extend RTE to sequence prediction, integrating it into fine-tuning algorithms for foundation models. Across empirical studies, we find that RTE substantially outperforms existing approaches on extrapolation to novel, unseen tasks.

</details>


### [108] [CalArena: A Large-Scale Post-Hoc Calibration Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30188)
*Eugène Berta,David Holzmüller,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 文章针对机器学习模型校准效果不佳的问题，引入了一个包含近2000个实验的标准化基准框架，提出使用PHI作为评价指标，并揭示了多种提升模型概率估计准确性的通用规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分类器校准效果普遍较差；现有事后校准（Post-hoc calibration）方法繁多，但缺乏一致的大规模评估，导致难以判断其实际有效性。

Method: 构建了一个包含约2000个实验的大规模标准化基准测试，涵盖表格和计算机视觉任务，并提出了基于“事后提升”（PHI）的评分准则，作为传统校准误差估计器的替代方案。

Result: 通过目前最全面的实证研究，发现了跨领域的性能一致性模式，并提供了整合后的开源数据、代码及评估工具。

Conclusion: 平滑校准函数优于分箱方法；在多分类高维场景下，专用的校准方法至关重要；通用机器学习模型若无针对性的校准设计，表现不理想。

Abstract: Reliable probability estimates are critical in many machine learning applications, yet modern classifiers are often poorly calibrated. Post-hoc calibration provides a simple and widely used solution, but the large number of proposed methods, combined with small-scale and inconsistent evaluations, makes it difficult to determine which approaches are truly effective in practice. We introduce a large-scale, standardized benchmark for post-hoc calibration, covering nearly 2000 experiments across tabular and computer vision tasks, including binary, multiclass, and large-scale classification settings. Our benchmark aggregates predictions from a diverse set of classical models, modern deep learning architectures, and foundation models, and provides unified, reproducible implementations of dozens of calibration methods within a common evaluation framework. We argue that Post-Hoc Improvement (PHI) in proper scoring rules offers a principled alternative to traditional calibration error estimators for comparing post-hoc methods, capturing both calibration quality and potential degradation to the model's predictive performance. Using this framework, we conduct the most comprehensive empirical study of post-hoc calibration to date. Our results reveal consistent patterns across domains: smooth calibration functions outperform binning-based approaches, dedicated multiclass methods are essential in high-dimensional settings, and generic machine learning models are not competitive without calibration-specific design. To facilitate future research, we release all data, code, and evaluation tools, providing a plug-and-play benchmark for developing and comparing calibration methods.

</details>


### [109] [Statistical Embeddings for Similarity, Retrieval, and Interpretable Alignment of Numeric Tabular Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30289)
*M. Ross Kunz,John Merickel,Keith Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过统计描述符对齐与规范化典型相关分析（CCA）实现异构数值表格数据集嵌入与检索的框架，无需共享特征名称即可实现高精度数据关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）缺乏原生机制来理解和表示异构特征空间下的数值表格数据集。现有方法要么仅局限于单一数据集的预测建模（要求固定变量定义），要么缺乏可解释的跨数据集对齐手段。因此，需要一种能够跨越异构界限、解释对齐机制并整合进RAG流水线的方法。

Method: 1. 构建结构化的探索性数据分析（EDA）描述符来表征数值表格数据集；2. 使用预训练的句子转换模型（Sentence Transformer）将这些描述符嵌入到共享向量空间；3. 使用正则化典型相关分析（Penalized CCA）来评估跨数据集相似性，并识别驱动对齐的关键统计变量；4. 引入可选的差分隐私机制以保护原始数据隐私。

Result: 该方法在涵盖通用基准、材料信息学和核级石墨表征的15个数据集上进行了验证。实验结果显示，P@1检索准确率达到0.9，且在消融实验和不同的差分隐私预算下，近邻检索与聚类结构保持高度稳健。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种端到端的数值表格数据集对齐与检索框架。它通过基于统计描述符（Exploratory Data Analysis descriptors）的嵌入方法，成功地绕过了对共享特征空间的依赖，实现了跨数据集的语义对齐与检索。此方法在处理异构数据源时表现稳健，并支持差分隐私下的敏感数据分析，为数值型数据的检索增强生成（RAG）提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Numeric tabular datasets are the dominant data format in scientific practice, yet large language models lack native mechanisms for representing numeric datasets in a meaningful way across heterogeneous feature spaces. Existing approaches either target predictive modeling over individual datasets, which requires a shared set of variable definitions, or lack mechanisms for interpretable cross-dataset alignment. The proposed methodology characterizes numeric tabular datasets through structured exploratory data analysis descriptors, embeds those descriptors into a shared vector space using a pretrained sentence transformer, and quantifies cross-dataset similarity via Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA). Furthermore, a penalized formulation of CCA is applied to recover sparse, interpretable variable-level correspondences between datasets, identifying which statistical descriptors or variable-level quantities drive cross-dataset alignment without requiring shared variable names or feature conventions. Differential privacy is optionally applied to the descriptor set prior to embedding, supporting deployment in sensitive data contexts without requiring access to raw observations at time of comparison. The methodology is evaluated across 15 datasets spanning general-purpose benchmarks, materials informatics, and nuclear-grade graphite characterization. Results demonstrate a total P@1 score of 0.9, with known nearest-neighbor retrieval and cluster structure remaining robust across embedding ablations and differential privacy budgets. The proposed framework provides a principled pathway for integrating heterogeneous numeric data into retrieval-augmented generation pipelines while preserving statistical context, with direct applications to data-driven algorithm selection and simulation model initialization for unknown datasets.

</details>


### [110] [Momentum Based Reward Design for Low Emission Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29693)
*Chinmay Mundane,Amith Manoharan,Arun Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动量的奖励函数（MBRF）用于自适应交通信号控制，通过引导车辆持续流转而非单纯减少拥堵，显著改善了城市交通效率、减少了环境污染并提升了DRL的训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交通信号控制难以适应动态交通环境，而现有的基于深度强化学习（DRL）的控制方法常因使用基于延迟和队列的奖励函数，导致策略短视或不稳定。

Method: 提出一种基于动量的奖励函数（MBRF），旨在鼓励车辆持续移动而非仅对拥堵进行惩罚；并利用SUMO交通仿真平台进行实验，对比了包括最大压力（Max Pressure）、LQF及常规延迟/队列奖励在内的多种基准。

Result: 相比于传统控制算法和现有的DRL奖励机制，MBRF在降低等待时间、缩短队列长度、提高吞吐量以及控制CO2排放方面表现更佳，且训练过程更加稳定。

Conclusion: 基于动量的奖励函数（MBRF）在自适应交通信号控制中表现优越，能够平衡吞吐量与排放，并展现出比传统基准和基于延迟/队列奖励机制更稳定、更高效的性能。

Abstract: Urban traffic congestion is a growing global issue contributing significantly to long commute times and environmental pollution. Traditional traffic signal control systems often fail to adapt to dynamic traffic conditions. Adaptive traffic signal control can improve urban traffic without changing road infrastructure. Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has shown strong performance for this task, but existing delay and queue-based rewards often produce short-sighted or unstable policies. This paper proposes a Momentum-Based Reward Function (MBRF) that encourages vehicles to keep moving rather than penalizing congestion alone. The method is evaluated in SUMO (Simulation of Urban MObility) using standard traffic metrics such as waiting time, queue length, throughput, and CO2 emissions. Results show that the proposed reward produces better throughput-emission trade-offs and more stable learning behavior than delay or queue-based rewards, as well as classical controllers such as Max Pressure and LQF.

</details>


### [111] [One Mask to Rule Them All: On Hidden Facts after Editing and How to Find Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28839)
*Ali Holmov,Paul Youssef,Nandi Schoots,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过分析ROME和MEMIT等知识编辑方法，作者揭示了其本质是通过抑制后续层的过度注意来实现编辑，而非真正覆写知识，这解释了其泛化能力的局限性，并为防御恶意编辑提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前像ROME和MEMIT这类知识编辑方法主要通过修改MLP权重来更新事实，但其内部机制尚不明确，论文旨在探讨不同编辑是否依赖于某种通用的内在机制。

Method: 研究者通过训练一个紧凑的二值掩码（compact binary mask）来定位修改权重中的关键子集，并分析该掩码在反转编辑效果、阻断编辑成功率（从98%降至38%）方面的作用，从而解析其内在机制。

Result: 研究发现：1. 尽管编辑的事实不同，但不同的编辑操作共享一个关键的功能权重子集；2. 该掩码能反转绝大多数编辑；3. 编辑功能的实现依赖于消除后续层的“过度注意”（overattention）。

Conclusion: ROME和MEMIT等知识编辑方法并非通过“改写”原知识，而是通过“抑制”特定神经元（消除后期图层的过度注意机制）来生效。这种机制解释了为何这些方法无法将知识泛化到相关事实，且该功能子空间可用于恶意编辑的检测与防御。

Abstract: Knowledge editing methods such as ROME and MEMIT update factual associations in transformer models by modifying MLP weights. While evaluated mainly by output behavior, their internal mechanism remains underexplored. We investigate whether edits rely on a common mechanism, regardless of which fact is modified. Despite fact-specific weight changes, we argue that ROME and MEMIT target the same subset of weights critical for maintaining edits. To isolate this subset, we train a compact binary mask over the edited weights. The mask reverses 80% of edits on the training set and over 70% on the test set, confirming that diverse edits share a common functional structure. Our analysis reveals that the mask reverses edits by eliminating overattention in later layers. Additionally, we show that injecting the mask during editing drops editing success from 98% to 38%, demonstrating that this mechanism is necessary for edits to succeed. Our finding that edits suppress rather than overwrite knowledge explains why ROME and MEMIT fail to propagate changes to related facts. The identified common functional subspace informs detection and defense against unwanted edits.

</details>


### [112] [Mechanistic origins of catastrophic forgetting: why RL preserves circuits better than SFT?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28860)
*Jeanmely Rojas Nunez,Viraj Sawant,Nathan Allen,Nomgondalai Amgalanbaatar,Yannis Zongo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从机制层面证实，强化学习相比监督微调能更好地通过保留原始内部计算电路来减轻灾难性遗忘，揭示了性能提升与稳定性之间的机制性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知强化学习（RL）在微调过程中能比监督微调（SFT）更好地保留模型原有能力，但目前尚不清楚这种优势是否对应于内部计算电路的更强保存。研究旨在从机制层面揭示RL为何能缓解“灾难性遗忘”。

Method: 引入“差异化电路脆弱性（Differential Circuit Vulnerability）”指标，用于定量衡量微调过程中模型内部计算头（Head-level）的退化程度。并在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct模型上对比了RL与SFT在科学问答任务中的表现。

Result: 实验表明，SFT在目标任务上适应更快，但对内部电路的破坏性大，导致原有能力遗忘严重；RL则以牺牲一定的任务适应速度为代价，显著保存了原本的模型电路结构。

Conclusion: 强化学习（RL）相比监督微调（SFT）表现出更强的内部电路保留能力，这种机制上的稳定性是RL在处理灾难性遗忘问题时表现更鲁棒的关键原因。研究揭示了任务适应速度与模型原始能力保留之间的机制性权衡（Trade-off）。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) frequently induces catastrophic forgetting of prior capabilities.
