Recent work has shown that reinforcement learning (RL) retains prior capabilities more effectively than supervised fine-tuning (SFT), attributing this to policy-gradient updates remaining closer to the base policy \cite{shenfeld2025rl}. We extend this behavioral account to the mechanistic level and ask whether RL's advantage is mirrored by stronger preservation of internal computational circuits. We introduce differential circuit vulnerability, a head-level measure of how much a circuit degrades under fine-tuning, and use it to compare RL and SFT on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct adapted to scientific question-answering. We find a clear mechanistic trade-off: SFT adapts more rapidly to the target task but produces substantially greater circuit disruption and forgetting of prior capabilities, whereas RL preserves a larger fraction of the base circuit at the cost of slower task adaptation. These findings suggest that circuit preservation may help explain why RL is more robust to catastrophic forgetting. We released our code here: https://github.com/rl-sft-circuit-research/differential-circuit-vulnerability.

</details>


### [113] [Self-Play Reinforcement Learning under Imperfect Information in Big 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28863)
*Aalok Patwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过构建Big 2游戏自博弈强化学习框架，证明了PPO算法在应对非完美信息多人博弈时的优势，并指出熵正则化和当前策略自博弈是提升模型收敛性能的关键策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在非完美信息、奖励稀疏、对手非平稳的多人博弈环境中，强化学习算法的有效性及适应能力。

Method: 构建Big 2四人纸牌游戏的强化学习框架，横向对比PPO（策略梯度类）与蒙特卡洛Q近似、SARSA及Q-learning（值近似类）在相同的环境、输入表示、训练预算及评估协议下的表现。并探讨了熵正则化和不同自博弈策略（如当前策略自博弈、检查点自博弈）对训练的影响。

Result: 1. 在相同限制条件下，PPO算法性能明显优于蒙特卡洛Q近似、SARSA和Q-learning；2. 适度的熵正则化能防止策略过早收敛至确定性，提升性能；3. 当前策略自博弈相比检查点自博弈或固定对手训练，能提供更有效的有限预算课程学习。

Conclusion: Big 2是一个评估深度强化学习在处理非完美信息、多玩家互动、延迟奖励及可变动作空间复杂场景下的优秀受控基准环境。在该场景下，PPO算法展现出优于传统值近似方法的性能，且适当的熵正则化和当前策略自博弈（current-policy self-play）对于训练效果至关重要。

Abstract: Imperfect-information multiplayer games test whether agents can act under hidden information, sparse rewards, and non-stationary opponents. We study these challenges in Big 2, a four-player imperfect-information card game. We develop a self-play RL framework for Big 2 that enables controlled comparisons between policy-gradient and value-approximating agents. Under a common environment, input representation, training budget, and evaluation protocol, PPO outperforms Monte Carlo Q approximation, SARSA, and Q-learning against random, greedy, and heuristic Big 2 opponents. We further find that moderate entropy regularization improves PPO by preventing the policy from becoming overly deterministic, and that current-policy self-play provides a stronger finite-budget curriculum than checkpoint self-play or fixed-opponent training. Together, these results show that Big 2 is a useful controlled setting for studying deep RL under imperfect information, multiplayer interaction, delayed rewards, and variable action sets.

</details>


### [114] [Emergent Semantic Representations in World Models through Physical Interaction without Linguistic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28865)
*Jiayi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现即使没有语言监督，世界模型仅通过物理探索，也能在隐空间中自发形成反映物理世界几何结构的语义表达，核心驱动因素是模型对物理预测任务的执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在没有任何语言监督的情况下，世界模型通过物理探索学习到了什么？作者假设其学习过程是被物理世界的几何结构所组织和驱动的。

Method: 使用基于VAE（变分自编码器）的世界模型在随机体现探索（embodied exploration）中进行训练，通过对比随机初始化编码器、评估空间语义结构（方向准确度、位置RSA）、分析训练过程中的时间检查点，以及通过调整KL正则化系数（beta）进行“双重敲除”实验来验证结论。

Result: 实验表明：1. 训练后的模型其隐空间具备显著的物理空间几何结构，远超随机编码器；2. 预测性能与语义对齐程度呈高度正相关；3. 过强的KL正则化（高beta值）会抑制这种几何结构的形成，导致模型预测能力和语义对齐同时崩溃，降低beta值则可恢复相关能力。

Conclusion: 物理世界的几何结构是世界模型表征的核心组织原则。预测能力的提升与语义对齐是同步发生的，这支持了“共同驱动”假设，即物理预测任务本身即足以诱导出语义表征，无需语言监督。

Abstract: What does a world model learn from physical exploration, without any linguistic supervision? We argue the answer is organized by a single principle: the geometric structure of the physical world. Training a VAE-based world model on random embodied exploration, we find that its latent space develops spatial semantic structure that mirrors physical geometry -- direction accuracy 0.677+-0.029 versus 0.547 for a randomly initialized encoder, and position RSA 0.192+-0.047 versus 0.029 for random encoders (6.6x improvement), showing that training induces genuine structural organization beyond CNN inductive bias. Across 20 temporal checkpoints, prediction performance and semantic alignment co-improve (Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004), consistent with the shared-driver account. We confirm this through a double knockout: standard KL regularization (beta=0.1) forces the encoder away from geometric structure, and both prediction performance and semantic alignment collapse simultaneously to near-chance by step 50,000 -- exactly as the shared-driver account predicts. Reducing beta to 0.001 restores geometric access and recovers both capabilities together. These findings establish physical world geometry as the organizing principle of world model representations, with direct implications for the design of semantically grounded embodied agents.

</details>


### [115] [Continuity and Ordinality Matter: Constraining Time Series Tokens for Effective Time Series Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28866)
*Musheng Li,Ziying Zhang,Cheng jin,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有时间序列大模型忽视数据连续性与序数性的问题，本文提出COM策略，通过引入几何约束来优化令牌嵌入，有效提升了模型的分析与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于令牌（Token-based）的时间序列大模型忽略了时间序列数据固有的连续性和序数性，限制了模型在性能和推理上的表现。

Method: 提出了COM (Continuity and Ordinality Matter) 策略，通过在嵌入的初始化和训练阶段引入几何约束（geometric constraints）来显式保留时间序列的连续性和序数性。

Result: 在多个时间序列分析基准测试中，采用COM策略的模型展现出持续的性能提升，且具有优秀的结果竞争力和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 保留时间序列令牌的连续性和序数性特征对于提升时间序列大模型的性能至关重要，提出的COM策略能有效增强模型表示能力。

Abstract: Token-based time series large language models (TS-LLMs) have emerged as a promising direction for time series analysis and reasoning. However, prior studies largely overlook the inherent continuity and ordinality of time series tokens, which substantially limits model performance. In this paper, we argue that preserving these properties in time series token embeddings is crucial for the effectiveness of token-based TS-LLMs. To this end, we propose COM (Continuity and Ordinality Matter), a continuity- and ordinality-aware strategy that integrates geometric constraints into both the initialization and training stages. Empirical results on multiple time series analysis benchmarks demonstrate that COM consistently improves the performance of token-based TS-LLMs, achieving competitive results and strong generalizability. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/COM .

</details>


### [116] [PrismFlow: Residual Dynamics for Flow Matching in Time-Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28867)
*Junru Zhang,Lang Feng,Jinbo Wang,Xu Guo,Yucheng Wang,Han Yu,Min Wu,Yabo Dong,Duanqing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决流匹配在高复杂性时间序列生成中出现的过度平滑和谱失真问题，PrismFlow 引入了基于柯普曼动态的专家混合模型及胜者全得（WTA）梯度优化策略，显著提升了生成数据的精细度和保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配（FM）方法通常依赖于单一全局向量场估计器，在处理具有多模态、多尺度动态（如振荡和高频变化）的复杂时间序列数据时，会导致训练目标过度平滑，造成谱失真和模式覆盖缺失。

Method: 1. 引入柯普曼（Koopman）启发的动态专家模型：在潜在空间学习残差修正，将非线性演化近似为线性转换；2. 提出置信度感知的胜者全得（WTA）目标函数：选择并仅更新与样本最匹配的专家，强制实现模式专业化；3. 采样阶段：通过全局流场与专家残差结合，保留稳定性的同时恢复高频动态。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，PrismFlow 实现了 SOTA 性能，Context-FID 提升 15.6%，判别分数（Discriminative Score）提升 38.6%，并在小样本场景下表现稳健，同时支持预测和填充任务。

Conclusion: PrismFlow 通过引入柯普曼算子（Koopman）启发的动态专家模型和置信度感知的胜者全得（WTA）训练目标，有效解决了传统流匹配模型在处理复杂多模态时间序列时产生的谱失真和过度平滑问题，实现了更高保真度和精细度的时间序列生成。

Abstract: Generating high-quality time-series data is challenging because real-world signals often exhibit multimodal patterns and multiscale dynamics, including oscillations and high-frequency variations. Flow Matching (FM) offers an efficient alternative to diffusion models, but practical implementations typically rely on a single finite-capacity global vector-field estimator. In such heterogeneous temporal distributions, distinct regimes may pass through nearby flow states while requiring incompatible conditional velocities. A monolithic estimator trained with the standard $\ell_2$ velocity-matching objective may therefore learn an overly smoothed approximation of the local transport field. This estimator-level smoothing can attenuate branch-specific dynamics, leading to spectral distortion and poor mode coverage. To address this, we propose PrismFlow, a new FM method with Koopman-inspired dynamical experts. Each expert learns residual corrections in a latent space where local nonlinear temporal evolution can be approximated by linear transitions. We further propose a confidence-aware Winner-Take-All (WTA) objective that updates only the expert best aligned with each sample while masking gradients to the others, encouraging mode-specific specialization. During sampling, the selected expert adds a residual dynamical correction to the global transport field, preserving FM stability while recovering fine-grained and high-frequency temporal structures. Across various benchmarks, PrismFlow effectively mitigates the spectral contraction in standard FM and achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a 15.6% gain in Context-FID and a 38.6% improvement in Discriminative Score, while remaining robust in low-data settings and effective for forecasting and imputation.

</details>


### [117] [Balancing Multimodal Learning through Label Space Reshaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28869)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Weijie Zhang,Yuanhao Gao,Han Miao,Yongjian Deng,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态学习中存在的模态收敛速度不平衡问题，本文提出了 BMLR 方法，通过调整标签空间而非调整梯度，从根本上平衡了各模态的映射难度，从而提升了整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习中普遍存在的“模态不平衡”问题，即收敛快的模态主导了优化，导致慢模态训练不足。现有方法大多采用被动补偿策略（牺牲强模态性能），缺乏对不平衡根本原因（映射难度差异）的分析。

Method: 提出“平衡多模态标签重塑”（BMLR），通过重塑跨模态标签空间，将不同模态映射到标签空间的难度归一化，从而提高模态间的交互能力并注入更丰富的类间信息。

Result: 在多种架构上进行了广泛实验，结果表明 BMLR 能一致且显著地提升多模态模型的性能，并表现出极强的兼容性。

Conclusion: BMLR 通过从标签空间视角解决模态学习进度不平衡问题，提供了一种更本质的解决方案，显著优于传统的梯度调整或增强加权方法，且具有良好的通用性。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often suffers from modality imbalance, where modalities that converge faster dominate optimization while others remain undertrained. Existing approaches typically mitigate this issue by strengthening the weak modality or adjusting optimization gradients. However, such strategies mainly compensate for optimization rate discrepancies, often at the expense of the strong modality's optimization capacity, without analyzing how these discrepancies arise at the modality level. Based on theoretical insights and empirical observations, we argue that the discrepancy of learning pace arises from differences in the mapping difficulty between modality-specific feature space and the shared label space. To address this issue, we propose Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping (BMLR), the first method that promotes multimodal balance from the label-side design. BMLR reshapes the cross-modal label space to equalize mapping difficulty across modalities, thereby facilitating modality interaction and injecting richer inter-class information into each modality. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures demonstrate that BMLR consistently improves multimodal performance and exhibits strong compatibility with diverse model designs. The source code will be released soon.

</details>


### [118] [Representation Alignment Rests on Linear Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28870)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套“信号-偏差-噪声”的统计架构，用以解释和量化不同AI模型在训练中涌现出的柏拉图式表征对齐（PRH）现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了深入探究柏拉图表征假设（PRH），即不同模型在学习过程中趋向于产生相似的内部表征，作者试图通过信号、偏差和噪声这三个核心维度对这一现象进行量化建模和解释。

Method: 1.信号分析：利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）提取线性对象-属性特征，并对比稀疏与稠密表征的跨模态对齐效果；2.偏差分析：通过引入中心化与归一化技术来修正不同模型间的隐式偏差；3.噪声分析：通过实证研究揭示词频与模型对齐程度之间的强正相关性，论证数据稀缺性导致的表征噪声。

Result: 研究表明：1.稀疏表征能够增强跨模态对齐；2.归一化处理可有效降低由架构和训练差异引起的偏差；3.数据质量和频率直接影响表征的对齐程度，验证了有限样本训练会引入显著的影响对齐的噪声。

Conclusion: 本文通过提出的信号-偏差-噪声三位一体统计框架，改进了线性表征假设（LRH），为理解现代AI架构中涌现的表征对齐现象（即柏拉图表征假设PRH）提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) through a tripartite statistical framework of representations: signal, bias, and noise. {1) Signal:} We propose that Platonic alignment arises from the universal relationship between objects and attributes, which is encoded linearly in representations according to the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH). We provide evidence that LRH helps explain PRH by extracting linear object-attribute features with sparse autoencoders and showing that these sparse representations often exhibit stronger cross-modal alignment than their dense counterparts. {2) Bias:} Models have different implicit biases due to the diverse architectures and training procedures used. We show that this difference can be partially mitigated. Centering and normalization consistently improve cross-model alignment. {3) Noise:} Finite-sample training leads to noise in representations. We provide evidence that representational noise is driven by data scarcity by revealing a strong and consistent positive correlation between word frequency and alignment in LLMs and text embedding models. Synthesizing signal, bias, and noise, we propose a statistical model that refines the Linear Representation Hypothesis and explains further phenomena related to the alignment of representations emerging from diverse modern AI architectures.

</details>


### [119] [Pre-Registering the Detectable Effect: A Paired-MDE Budget for 4-bit Quantization Benchmarks, with a Pilot Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28873)
*Zexin Zhuang,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为量化基准测试提供了一个用于事前规划的简单统计公式（MDE边界），旨在量化评估的可靠性，并指出许多量化性能差异常被模型评测中的随机噪声（采样及提示词变动）所掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化基准测试（如FP16与NF4之间的对比）通常缺乏严谨的统计评估框架，导致研究者难以判断观测到的性能差异是量化引入的实际差异还是随机噪声。

Method: 改编了Miettinen（1968）经典的配对二元样本量计算方法，导出了用于量化基准测试的MDE边界公式，并通过在四种模型和四个基准测试上的实验及其与MMLU提示词模板噪声的对比进行验证。

Result: 推导出了MDE公式 $\delta^{*} \le (z_{1-\alpha/2}+z_{1-\beta})\sqrt{\rho_d/m}$；实证显示，大多数测试中量化带来的差异在统计噪声范围内，且量化引起的差异明显小于由提示词模板变化引入的差异。

Conclusion: 提出了一个量化评测的最小可检测效应（MDE）边界公式，并建议在评测前进行预注册。研究表明，许多量化基准测试中的“不可靠性”实际上是二项式采样噪声，且量化带来的差异往往小于提示词模板变动带来的噪声。

Abstract: This is a planning-method note with an unpaired pilot audit. We adapt the classical paired-binary sample-size calculation (Miettinen, 1968) to quantization benchmarks, giving a conservative minimum detectable effect (MDE) bound $δ^{*} \le (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})\sqrt{ρ_d/m}$ in the paired item count $m$ and the FP16-NF4 disagreement rate $ρ_d$. The bound turns "how reliable is my quantization claim?" into a one-line budget a benchmark designer can commit to before running. We illustrate the bound on four models and four benchmarks ($k=5$ splits of $n=100$), and add a parallel MMLU prompt-template study to put the bound's quantization-noise scale alongside the prompt-noise scale. Assuming $ρ_d=0.10$ (an unmeasured planning value), all observed NF4-FP16 deltas fall below the implied MDE, and most cross-split SDs lie within $\pm 1.5$ pp of the binomial reference $\sqrt{p(1-p)/n}$, so much of the variance reported as "benchmark unreliability" on $n=100$ subsamples is binomial sampling noise. The single borderline cell (OPT-WinoGrande, $|Δ|=3.2$ pp) is below the implied MDE at $ρ_d=0.10$ but above it at $ρ_d=0.05$, illustrating the planning trade-off the bound makes explicit. On MMLU, prompt-template ranges of 2-10 pp meet or exceed the largest observed quantization delta (3.2 pp), so a quantization audit that does not first fix the prompt template absorbs template variance into its noise floor. We complement the bound with a five-line pre-registration template.

</details>


### [120] [Towards Continuous-time Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28880)
*Dennis Thumm,Ruben Wiedemann,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文解决了连续时间因果建模中SDE积分导致的观测依赖问题，提出了轨迹律不变的精细积分框架，相比传统方法在不规则观测数据上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的将离散时间因果Prior-data Fitted Networks扩展到连续时间的方法（如简单地在观测间隔上积分SDE）无法保证轨迹律与观测时刻的独立性，导致其本质上仍是离散时间马尔可夫模型。

Method: 提出了一个三层分类法（离散、朴素观测网格积分、解耦观测的精细网格积分），并构建了基于随机DAG（附带OU过程或小型MLP非线性漂移）的连续时间因果模型；通过消融实验对比了不同编码器与积分器配置的性能表现。

Result: 消融实验表明，在所有8个测试场景中，精细网格积分方法的效果均优于朴素积分法（显著性$p < 1/256$）；精细积分方法下编码器设计不敏感，但在朴素积分下对时间感知能力有强依赖。

Conclusion: 本文通过定义“轨迹律对观测日程的不变性”作为连续时间因果模型的准则，提出了能够实现这一目标的精细网格积分方法，并证实了其在处理非规则观测和干预时的鲁棒性优于传统的离散或朴素积分方法。

Abstract: Extending discrete-time causal Prior-data Fitted Networks for time series to continuous time invites writing the mechanism as a stochastic differential equation (SDE) -- but if the SDE is integrated \emph{once per observation gap}, the trajectory law depends on when it is observed, and the prior remains a discrete-time Markov model in SDE clothing.
  We propose a precise continuity criterion -- trajectory-law invariance to the observation schedule -- together with a three-tier taxonomy (discrete; naive observation-grid integration; fine-grid integration with decoupled observation) and a construction realising the top tier on a random DAG with OU or small-MLP nonlinear drifts, irregular observation schedules, and hard / soft / time-varying interventions.
  A $2 \times 2$ encoder $\times$ integrator ablation, run independently on a linear and a nonlinear prior, finds fine-grid integration beats naive on 8/8 cells (sign-consistency $p < 1/256$) with the gap growing as the eval grid refines; the encoder axis is null with fine integration but time-aware-leading with naive.
  We release the prior and a preliminary zero-shot protocol on pharmacokinetic and physical-system data.

</details>


### [121] [Context Distillation as Latent Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28889)
*Ziyang Zheng,Zeju Li,Xiangyu Wen,Jianyuan Zhong,Junhua Huang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决上下文蒸馏在多记忆管理上的不足，本文提出一种基于LoRA适配器的模块化记忆库架构，通过检索、路由与自门控机制，实现了上下文信息的高效存储与安全激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文蒸馏方法忽略了在非预言机（non-oracle）场景下，如何系统地存储、检索以及安全地激活多个蒸馏后的潜在记忆，导致模型在处理复杂上下文时缺乏灵活性和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出一种将上下文蒸馏为独立LoRA适配器的模块化记忆库方法。具体包括：1. 检索模块根据查询匹配候选适配器；2. 路由机制选择最优适配器；3. 自门控（Self-Gating）机制决定是否激活特定记忆；4. 引入缓存共享（Cache Sharing）技术以降低推理开销。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在多种检索任务中显著优于现有基准模型；同时，自门控机制通过有效屏蔽不必要的潜在记忆激活，进一步增强了模型的整体鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了一个模块化的潜在记忆管理框架，证明了通过独立的LoRA适配器进行上下文蒸馏并结合检索与自门控机制，可以在非预言机环境下实现高效、可控的上下文信息激活，显著提升了模型的鲁棒性与性能。

Abstract: Context distillation compresses contextual information into model parameters, yet existing methods often ignore how multiple distilled latent memories should be stored, retrieved, and safely activated in non-oracle settings. We formulate context distillation as a latent memory management problem. We distill each context into an independent LoRA adapter, forming a modular memory bank that enables explicit memory selection. Given a query, our framework retrieves candidate memories, routes the query to the most suitable adapter, and uses a Self-Gating mechanism to decide whether latent memory should be activated. To improve efficiency, we further introduce cache sharing to reduce management overhead during inference. Experiments show that our method substantially outperforms baselines with retrieval, while Self-Gating improves robustness by deactivate unnecessary latent memories.

</details>


### [122] [Feature Geometry of LoRA Adapters: A Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Representational Divergence in Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28896)
*Prasanth K K*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现LoRA微调会在模型中创建新的、独特的表征结构，这些结构独立于原始模型的预训练特征，现有的可解释性字典难以完全覆盖这些变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LoRA被广泛应用，但人们对其微调引起的内部表征变化及其几何特性缺乏足够的理解，且现有的可解释性工具（SAE）是否适用于微调后的模型尚不明确。

Method: 提出“Delta激活框架”（Delta activation framework）以分离LoRA适配器对残差流的贡献；利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析Gemma-2-9B微调后的表征；通过余弦相似度、主角分析（Principal-angle analysis）和中心核对齐（CKA）评估LoRA诱导空间与预训练特征空间的对齐程度。

Result: 1. LoRA诱导的特征字典与预训练SAE特征之间的几何对齐度较弱；2. 适配器专用的SAE能更有效地重构Delta激活，证明LoRA在残差流中占据了部分独特的表征空间；3. 随着Rank和深度的增加，特征密度增加，但几何差异性相对稳定。

Conclusion: LoRA会在模型残差流中诱导产生与预训练模型不同的、特有的特征结构，这些结构无法被现有的预训练稀疏自编码器（SAE）完全捕获。这对模型可解释性、微调分析及安全性审计提出了新挑战。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting large language models, yet the internal representational changes induced by LoRA fine-tuning remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we investigate the geometry of LoRA-induced representations using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs). We introduce a delta activation framework that isolates the adapter-specific contribution to the residual stream.
  Using Gemma-2-9B with LoRA ranks 4, 8, 16, and 32, we train adapter-specific SAEs across multiple transformer layers and compare their learned feature spaces with pretrained SAE dictionaries. We evaluate representational alignment using cosine similarity between decoder directions, principal-angle analysis of feature subspaces, and Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between activation representations.
  Across layers and ranks, we consistently observe comparatively weak geometric alignment between LoRA-induced feature dictionaries and pretrained SAE features. Adapter-specific SAEs also reconstruct delta activations more effectively than pretrained SAEs, suggesting that LoRA updates occupy partially distinct representational structure within the residual stream. Additionally, feature density increases with rank and depth, while geometric divergence remains relatively stable across ranks.
  These findings provide empirical evidence that LoRA fine-tuning can induce feature structures that are not fully captured by pretrained interpretability dictionaries, with implications for mechanistic interpretability, adaptation analysis, and safety auditing of fine-tuned language models.

</details>


### [123] [Spectral Guidance for Flexible and Efficient Control of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28900)
*Gabriel Moreira,Manuel Marques,João Paulo Costeira,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Spectral Guidance 通过自监督学习捕捉扩散过程中的内在几何特征，实现了一种无需反向传播、高效且通用的扩散模型控制框架，显著提升了生成任务的精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型引导方法往往需要昂贵的反向传播计算，或者在采样过程中缺乏几何一致性。作者旨在通过挖掘扩散过程中保留的内在几何特征，实现更稳定、更高效且无需重训练的精确引导。

Method: 提出了一种基于条件期望算子奇异函数（singular functions）的框架。通过自监督学习提取数据中对控制至关重要的信息特征基底，从而将任意引导信号（标签、CLIP 嵌入或掩码）直接投影到采样轨迹上，无需在采样过程中进行反向传播。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10 上，该方法将条件生成准确率比最强的免训练基线提高了 37 个百分点，同时采样速度提升了 4 倍，且无需辅助模型即可实现空间掩码控制。

Conclusion: Spectral Guidance 是一种高效、无需重新训练且能提供高质量控制的扩散模型引导框架。该方法揭示了扩散过程中的“相位转换”现象，明确了进行引导的最佳时间窗口，并证明了其在分类准确度、采样速度及多模态控制场景下的优越性。

Abstract: We introduce Spectral Guidance, a framework for controlling diffusion models by leveraging the intrinsic geometry of the generative process. As data is progressively corrupted by noise, only a small number of features remain informative for control. We characterize them as the singular functions of a conditional expectation operator and show that they can be learned via a self-supervised objective. Once recovered, this basis enables the projection of arbitrary guidance signals, such as labels, CLIP embeddings, or masks, directly onto the sampling trajectory. This approach allows for stable, high-fidelity control without retraining or denoiser backpropagation during sampling. Empirically, we improve conditional accuracy on CIFAR-10 by 37 percentage points over the strongest training-free baseline while offering $4\times$ faster sampling. Moreover, the same representations that support label and CLIP guidance also enable spatial control, such as mask-based guidance, without auxiliary models. Finally, our framework reveals a phase transition in the generative process, pinpointing the optimal time window for effective guidance.

</details>


### [124] [Cycle-Space Informed Detection of Autoencoded Blind False Data Injection Attacks on Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28912)
*Xin Li,Chenhan Xiao,Jonathan Cohen,Aviad Elyashar,Yang Weng,Rami Puzis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对新型躲避传统检测的数据驱动型AI攻击（FDIA），本文提出了一种基于电力系统拓扑结构与最小环基的检测方案（CSD），通过物理约束实现了对该类隐蔽攻击的有效识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于统计或数据驱动的电力系统异常检测方法容易被能够利用相同数据结构特征的攻击者规避；特别是由自编码器生成的、隐藏在测量零空间中的隐蔽攻击，能够轻易绕过残差检测器。

Method: 1. 构建了一种利用自编码器学习测量流形并在雅可比矩阵零空间生成扰动的盲式虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA）；2. 提出了一种基于拓扑约束的周期空间检测器（CSD），利用最小环基（MCB）对系统状态进行约束。

Result: 在IEEE 14, 30, 57和118节点系统上的仿真表明，CSD能够有效检测出在存在测量噪声情况下的数据驱动型FDIA，且具有良好的稳健性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于最小环基的周期空间检测器（CSD）利用电力系统拓扑约束，无需精确线路参数即可有效检测规避传统检测机制的数据驱动型虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA），并证明了其检测泛化误差的最优性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI-driven data centers and large-scale energy storage systems is increasing the reliance of power system operation on real-time measurement data and automated decision-making. However, many existing detection methods rely on statistical or data-driven analysis of measurements and can fail when attackers exploit the same data structure to craft stealthy perturbations. To illustrate this limitation, we demonstrate a blind False Data Injection Attack (FDIA) in which an Autoencoder learns the measurement manifold and generates perturbations aligned with the Jacobian null space, thereby allowing the attack to evade both residual-based baddata detectors and time-series anomaly detectors. To mitigate data-driven FDIAs which exploit the null space, we propose a topology-informed Cycle-Space Detector (CSD) that leverages the Cycle-Space of the network to impose structural constraints that enhance null space estimation. In addition, we prove that by using the Minimum Cycle Basis (MCB), the proposed CSD achieves the optimal generalization error for attack detection. By exploiting topology-derived cycle constraints rather than relying solely on numerical null space estimation, the proposed method does not require precise line parameters and improves the separation between normal and attacked measurements. Simulation results on IEEE 14-, 30-, 57-, and 118-bus systems demonstrate that the proposed method effectively detects data-driven FDIAs under realistic measurement noise.

</details>


### [125] [CosmicFish-HRM: Adaptive Reasoning via Hierarchical Recurrent Mechanisms in Compact Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28919)
*Venkat Akhil Lakkapragada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CosmicFish-HRM的紧凑型语言模型，通过层级推理模块实现动态计算分配，根据输入复杂度调整推理深度，从而为大规模模型的推理效率问题提供了一种创新解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然具备强大的推理能力，但参数量巨大且推理成本高昂。研究旨在通过紧凑型模型中的自适应推理深度，在降低计算资源消耗的同时保持推理性能。

Method: 提出了CosmicFish-HRM模型，包含一个层级推理模块（HRM），支持根据输入复杂度动态分配推理步数。模型集成了分组查询注意力（GQA）、RoPE位置编码和SwiGLU激活函数等现代Transformer组件，并通过学习机制自动决定何时停止推理。

Result: 实验证明模型能够学习非均匀的推理行为，根据不同任务和输入分配相应的推理深度。在小型规模下验证了推理架构的可行性，并推测随着模型规模扩大，该架构的成本效益将更具优势。

Conclusion: 自适应推理深度为提升小型语言模型的推理能力提供了一条有前景的路径，无需单纯依赖模型参数规模的扩大。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved strong reasoning capabilities, though often at the cost of massive parameter counts and expensive inference. In this work, we explore a different direction: adaptive reasoning depth in compact language models. We present CosmicFish-HRM, a compact language model built around a Hierarchical Reasoning Module (HRM) that dynamically allocates computational effort during inference. Instead of applying fixed computation to every input, the model iterates through high-level and low-level reasoning cycles and learns when to halt based on input complexity. CosmicFish-HRM combines this adaptive reasoning core with modern transformer components including Grouped Query Attention, RoPE, and SwiGLU activations. While the additional reasoning infrastructure introduces overhead at small scale, we hypothesize that this tradeoff becomes increasingly favorable as model size grows and the relative cost of the HRM core diminishes. Our results show that the model learns non-uniform reasoning behavior, allocating different numbers of reasoning steps across tasks and inputs. These findings suggest that adaptive reasoning depth may offer a promising alternative to relying solely on parameter scale for reasoning capability.

</details>


### [126] [Comparing Post-Hoc Explainable AI Methods for Interpreting Black-Box EEG Models in Depression Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28977)
*Antonia Šarčević,Nikolina Frid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对比了五种深度学习模型可解释性方法在 EEG 抑郁症检测中的表现，发现尽管不同方法对大脑区域的归因有部分重叠，但方法选择会显著影响解释结果，提醒研究者在临床应用中需谨慎解读此类解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在基于 EEG 的重度抑郁症（MDD）分类中准确率虽高，但其决策过程缺乏可解释性（黑箱问题），亟需探索适用于医学场景的解释方法及其可靠性。

Method: 研究使用 InceptionTime 架构训练 EEG 分类模型，并应用五种事后可解释性方法（DeepSHAP, Integrated Gradients, GradCAM, Occlusion, Permutation Feature Importance）进行对比分析；采用了基于受试者分层 5 折交叉验证框架，并对 EEG 段和受试者进行了全局归因聚合。

Result: 实验发现各大脑区（尤其是右半球的额叶、颞叶和后叶）具有一致的归因模式。定量比较显示，梯度与扰动类方法具有较高的一致性，而 DeepSHAP 的归因分布较为不同；不同方法间的差异凸显了方法论假设对结果解释的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，虽然不同的事后可解释性方法能捕捉到重叠的特征相关性结构，且结果与既往抑郁症研究一致，但这些方法仍受方法论假设影响，不能直接作为临床诊断的确定性神经生物学标志物。研究强调了可解释性 AI 在精神医学应用中既有价值又存在局限性。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning have enabled increasingly accurate electroencephalography (EEG)-based classification of Major Depressive Disorder (MDD), but the decision-making processes of high-capacity models remain difficult to interpret. This study investigates multiple post-hoc explainability methods applied to an InceptionTime architecture trained for EEG-based MDD detection. The analysis includes Shapley-based, gradient-based, and perturbation-based attribution approaches: DeepSHAP, Integrated Gradients, GradCAM, Occlusion, and Permutation Feature Importance. Explainability analysis was performed within a subject-level stratified 5-fold cross-validation framework using global attribution aggregation across EEG segments and subjects. The evaluated methods revealed partially convergent attribution patterns, with recurring emphasis on frontal, temporal, and posterior EEG regions, particularly in the right hemisphere. Quantitative comparison demonstrated substantial agreement between gradient- and perturbation-based approaches, while DeepSHAP produced comparatively distinct attribution distributions. At the same time, variability between explainability methods highlighted the influence of methodological assumptions on the resulting explanations. Overall, the results suggest that different post-hoc explainability approaches capture partially overlapping relevance structures in EEG-based deep learning models for depression detection. Although the observed attribution patterns are broadly consistent with several previous EEG studies of MDD, the analysis should be interpreted as exploratory rather than evidence of definitive neurophysiological biomarkers or clinical applicability. The study highlights both the usefulness and limitations of post-hoc explainability for interpreting black-box EEG classifiers in psychiatric applications.

</details>


### [127] [Learning Robust and Task-Invariant Functional Representation from fMRI through Siamese Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28990)
*Jiyao Wang,Peiyu Duan,Nicha C. Dvornek,Lawrence H. Staib,Denis Sukhodolsky,Pamela Ventola,James S. Duncan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a powerful tool for investigating human brain function. However, the high cost of data acquisition and the inherent subjectivity of psychiatric rating scales often lead to datasets with small sample sizes and variable label quality, especially when targeting a specific neurological condition. Combined with the inherently high dimensionality of fMRI data, these limitations substantially increase the risk of model overfitting. Recent years have seen growing interest in developing fMRI foundation models by combining multiple datasets; however, the computational resources needed for pretraining and fine-tuning are often prohibitive. We show that a lightweight self-supervised framework yields representations that generalize across diverse downstream tasks, outperforming fully supervised baselines and approaching the performance of large-scale models. We introduce BrainSimSiam, a data-efficient self-supervised representation learning framework that leverages positive-only data pairs to learn robust and generalizable features. We demonstrate that the learned representations achieve strong performance across multiple downstream classification and regression tasks, highlighting the potential of BrainSimSiam for data-limited neuroimaging applications.

</details>


### [128] [FormInv: A Measurement Protocol for Semantic Invariance in Mathematical Reasoning Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29001)
*Nishal Thomas,Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究指出大模型基准测试存在可人为操纵的漏洞（语义改写错误），提出了 FormInv 审计框架以度量模型的语义鲁棒性，发现模型的总精度往往掩盖了其推理的一致性危机，强调应使用 SCR 等不变性指标代替单一的精度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型基准测试（如MathCheck）中存在严重的改写质量问题（语义错误），且由于“无免费基准定理”（No-Free-Benchmark corollary）的存在，基准测试设计者通过选择改写方式即可隐性地操纵模型排名，基准测试缺乏对语义鲁棒性的评估。

Method: 1. 开发了 FormInv，一套自动化审计协议，通过跨模型一致性（cross-model unanimity）检测语义等价改写（paraphrases）中的错误。2. 引入了语义一致性率（SCR）和Cochran's Q来衡量模型在同一数学定理不同表述下的不变性。3. 通过 FormInvSelector 进行机制感知的模型选择。

Result: 1. 审计发现 MathCheck 中存在 3.1% 的语义错误，剔除后导致模型排名发生逆转（如 GPT-4o 掉出前三）。2. 揭示了隐藏的“准确性与语义一致性断层”：模型总分精度差异不大（86%-96%），但语义一致性极差（50%-82%）。3. 证明了可以通过选择特定的改写方式实现对任意模型的排名优化。

Conclusion: 现有的LLM基准测试存在严重的选择偏差和语义鲁棒性问题。通过精细的语义审计（FormInv），研究证明了排名是人为可操控的，建议将语义一致性率（SCR）和Cochran's Q作为衡量模型性能的核心指标，而非单一的总分精度。

Abstract: A paraphrase-quality audit of MathCheck (ICLR 2025) detected 4 semantically incorrect paraphrases in 129 groups (3.1%); removing them drops GPT-4o from rank 2 to rank 4 and elevates Claude Haiku and DeepSeek V3 above it; these ranking changes are invisible to any single-model evaluation. Cross-model unanimity found these errors automatically (>= 3/4 models for MathCheck; >= 6/9 for our primary evaluation) for under $10; in our own dataset the same protocol found that 47% of auto-generated connective-variation paraphrases were semantically incorrect. That flaw compounds a deeper measurement gap: Claude Haiku 4.5 achieves 86% accuracy yet SCR=50%, meaning half its theorems are answered differently under semantically equivalent restatements, while aggregate accuracy across 9 models spans only 86-96% yet Semantic Consistency Rates (SCR) span 50-82% -- a 32-point gap invisible to standard benchmarks. Formally, for any target ranking over 9 frontier models there exists a weighting over paraphrase families that realizes it (No-Free-Benchmark corollary), because no model Pareto-dominates all families -- so benchmark designers who select families are implicitly choosing which model wins. FormInv supplies the audit protocol (replicated on external benchmarks at 100% recall), SCR and per-theorem Cochran's Q as primary invariance measures evaluated on 9 models across 366-811 items (on Lean4-verified theorems), and FormInvSelector for regime-aware model selection.

</details>


### [129] [Causal Intelligence for Constraint-Aware Intervention Design to Induce State Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29008)
*Zixuan Song,Uwe Mueller,Dimitris V. Manatakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COAST 是一种因果智能方法，通过学习结构因果模型和多目标优化，为复杂系统提供可解释的、最优的受限干预设计，以实现预期的系统状态转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数现有的预测模型缺乏机制洞察力，且在系统控制和决策制定方面缺乏统一的原则性框架。

Method: 提出了一种名为 COAST 的方法，结合了：（1）数据驱动的情境特定因果图与结构因果模型（SCM）学习；（2）将分布偏移归因于机制级因果驱动因素；（3）一种新型的约束感知多目标优化策略，平衡转变有效性、干预复杂性和目标状态稳定性。

Result: 在合成基准与真实生物学数据集上，COAST 成功识别了关键因果驱动因素，并能推导出鲁棒的单目标和多目标干预策略，同时提供了透明的机制解释。

Conclusion: COAST 提供了一个模块化、领域不可知的框架，能够通过因果建模识别有效的干预策略，并为复杂的系统状态转变提供可解释的机制依据。

Abstract: Driving a system from one state to another through targeted interventions is a fundamental challenge in science, yet most predictive models offer limited mechanistic insight and no principled framework for decision-making. Here we present COAST (Causally Optimal Actions for State Transitions), a causal-intelligence approach for the in-silico design of constrained interventions that induce user-defined state transitions. Given data characterizing source and target states, COAST learns context-specific causal graphs and structural causal models, attributes observed distributional shifts to mechanism-level causal drivers, and introduces a novel constraint-aware multi-objective optimization formulation that balances transition efficacy, intervention complexity, and target-state stability. The approach is modular and domain-agnostic, integrating feature selection, causal discovery, causal modeling, and intervention identification and evaluation through interchangeable components. Across synthetic benchmarks and real biological datasets, COAST recovers key causal drivers and identifies robust single- and multi-target intervention strategies that achieve desired state transitions, accompanied by transparent mechanistic rationales to guide experimental validation.

</details>


### [130] [Label-Free Reinforcement Learning via Cross-Model Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29009)
*Matt Gorbett,Hossein Shirazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 Cross-Model Entropy (CME) 的无标签强化学习奖励机制，利用独立验证模型对生成器输出来提供高质量、抗干扰的反馈，大幅改善了大模型在开放式任务中的指令遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习奖励机制受限于昂贵的人工标注或仅适用于数学/代码等特定领域的验证器；现有的无标签方法（如自一致性）容易强化模型自身的错误。

Method: 提出跨模型熵 (Cross-Model Entropy, CME)，即计算生成器输出在独立验证模型下的平均对数似然；将 CME 作为一种无需训练的连续奖励信号集成到 GRPO 算法中。

Result: 在 UltraFeedback 上使用 AlpacaEval 2.0 评估，CME 奖励机制在四个模型系列（Qwen, Llama, Gemma, OLMo）和三种训练阶段中均优于基线模型，经调整后的胜率达到 52.5% 至 71.4%。

Conclusion: CME 提供了一种无需人工标记或 ground-truth 的高效奖励机制，能够有效提升大模型在开放式指令遵循任务中的表现，证明了跨模型一致性作为奖励信号的有效性。

Abstract: Post-training large language models with reinforcement learning is bottlenecked by the reward signal. Existing approaches require either ground-truth verifiable rewards, restricting training to domains with automatic correctness checks (e.g., mathematics, code execution), or human preference labels, which are expensive to collect and prone to reward hacking. Recent label-free methods replace ground-truth verifiers with self-referential signals like majority voting or token entropy over a model's own outputs, but risk reinforcing a model's own errors. In this work we propose Cross-Model Entropy (CME), the mean log-likelihood of a generator's response under a separate verifier model, as a label-free reward signal for RL post-training. CME is continuous, training-free, and grounded in the principle that responses a verifier finds unsurprising are likely correct or high quality. Because the verifier is independent of the generator, the signal cannot be gamed through self-consistency. We integrate CME into GRPO with no other changes to the training loop, extending label-free RL to open-ended instruction following -- a regime where self-referential signals are inapplicable or poorly suited. On open-ended instruction following (UltraFeedback prompts, evaluated on AlpacaEval 2.0), CME rewards beat the untrained base in head-to-head LLM-as-Judge comparisons across four model families (Qwen, Llama, Gemma, OLMo) and three training regimes (pretrained, SFT, and instruction-tuned), with tie-adjusted win rates ranging from 52.5% to 71.4%. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [131] [Moment Matching Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29033)
*Yiyan,Liang,Sifei Liu,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对生成式模型在 RL 应用中推理延迟过高的问题，MoMa QL 通过引入 MMD 矩匹配技术，在保证训练稳定性和分布收敛性的前提下，大幅提升了采样效率与在线微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于评分和基于流的生成模型虽然表达能力强，但因其迭代采样过程导致推理延迟高，成为强化学习任务中的计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种名为“矩匹配 Q-学习（MoMa QL）”的框架，利用最大均值差异（MMD）技术，在训练过程中强制要求原始动作分布与目标动作分布匹配所有阶数的统计量（矩）。

Result: 实验表明，MoMa QL 在 D4RL 基准测试中实现了计算效率的提升，并保持了与现有方法相当或更优的性能；特别是在离线到在线的强化学习任务中，因其更快的采样速度表现出更强的在线微调适应能力。

Conclusion: MoMa QL 通过匹配分布的所有矩统计量，有效解决了基于评分和基于流的生成模型在 RL 中的推理延迟问题，在保持性能的同时显著提升了计算效率和在线调优的适应性。

Abstract: Score-based and flow-based generative models exhibit remarkable expressive capacity in capturing complex distributions, and have been extensively deployed in tasks ranging from image generation to reinforcement learning. Nevertheless, these models suffer from prolonged inference latency, which imposes a significant computational bottleneck in RL with iterative sampling. To overcome this limitation, we propose a new framework named Moment Matching Q-Learning (MoMa QL), which utilizes a technique from statistical hypothesis testing known as maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) that intend to match all orders of statistics between the original and target distribution. By enforcing strong regularization on all moment statistics, this algorithm guarantees distribution-level convergence for conditional score function and remains stable under various hyperparameters. Empirically, we show that our method MoMa QL is more computationally efficient with a comparable if not competitive performance in various D4RL tasks. Remarkably, by accelerating the action sampling process for flow-based policies, MoMa QL demonstrates superior performance in offline-to-online RL tasks because of faster and stronger adaptability for online interactive finetuning.

</details>


### [132] [OISD: On-Policy Internal Self-Distillation of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29089)
*Xinyu Liu,Darryl Cherian Jacob,Yang Zhou,Jindong Wang,Pan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OISD 是一种新型的在线策略内部自蒸馏框架，通过将最终输出层的推理行为和注意力模式“向下”蒸馏至中间层，有效增强了强化学习在推理任务中的表现，无需额外特权信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RL 后训练方法多侧重于最终策略的稀疏奖励优化，忽略了模型中间表征中蕴含的预测信号，导致推理能力提升受限。

Method: 提出 OISD（On-policy Internal Self-Distillation）框架，在 GRPO 优化过程中，将最终层作为内部教师，通过“logit 对齐”（转移推理行为）和“注意力对齐”（对齐注意力模式）两种机制，引导中间层的表征优化，并使用有符号优势加权 Jensen-Shannon 散度进行对齐蒸馏。

Result: 在四个数学推理任务上，OISD 相比强力的强化学习基线模型取得了显著且一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: OISD 框架通过将最终策略层的预测信号蒸馏至中间层，并结合 logit 和注意力对齐机制，有效提升了强化学习在复杂推理任务中的性能，证明了利用内部中间表征信号对于强化学习优化的重要性。

Abstract: Recent reinforcement learning (RL) post-training approaches primarily optimize the final output policy using sparse outcome-level rewards, while largely overlooking predictive signals encoded in intermediate representations. In this paper, we introduce a new paradigm called on-policy internal self-distillation and propose the OISD framework, which improves reasoning by transferring on-policy predictive signals from the final layer to intermediate representations. During rollout and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) optimization, the final layer acts as both the policy and a detached internal teacher for selected intermediate layers, which are guided to align with it through two complementary mechanisms: logit alignment, which transfers high-level reasoning behaviors (how to think), and attention alignment, which enforces consistent attention patterns (where to look) from the final layer to the selected intermediate layer, both without requiring external privileged information. Our OISD, together with GRPO, employs signed advantage-weighted Jensen--Shannon alignment to distill informative intermediate representations while preserving policy consistency under a unified acting policy. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of OISD, with substantial and consistent improvements over strong reasoning RL baselines across four mathematical reasoning tasks. The code will be released at https://github.com/THE-MALT-LAB/OISD

</details>


### [133] [Model Merging by Output-Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29101)
*Bethan Evans,Benjamin Etheridge,Stephen Roberts,Jared Tanner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将模型合并统一为凸二次规划问题，消除了启发式设计的局限性，实现了无重训下的多任务模型最优集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型合并方法（如TIES, DARE等）多基于启发式设计，缺乏形式化的最优性保证和理论分析。

Method: 将模型合并问题建模为针对残差更新的凸二次规划（QP），旨在最小化平方输出校准误差。针对多层模型，提出了序列分层算法，并引入了基于残差能量分数的基准选择诊断指标。

Result: 实验表明，所提方法在单层设置中匹配或超越了现有方法，且在语言与视觉任务的基准测试中展现了稳健的性能提升，同时能够有效预测合并后的下游任务效果。

Conclusion: 模型合并可以被形式化为残差更新上的凸二次规划（QP）问题。该框架不仅统一了现有的启发式合并技术，还提供了一种基于校准集的闭式诊断方法，能够预测合并性能，并在多层模型中实现了更优的性能。

Abstract: Model merging combines fine-tuned checkpoints into a single multi-task model without retraining. Existing methods - such as task arithmetic, model soups, TIES, and DARE - are computationally efficient and empirically successful, but rely on heuristic design choices and lack formal optimality guarantees. We show that merging can be formulated as a convex quadratic programme over residual updates, yielding weights that minimise a squared-output calibration objective using calibration inputs and fine-tuned model outputs, and subsuming existing methods as special cases. Our framework yields a closed-form diagnostic - the fraction of residual energy captured by a chosen basis - that predicts downstream merge quality using only the calibration set. Empirically, the QP matches or outperforms existing methods in the single-layer setting, and we characterise when the optimal basis provides significant gains over the cheaper diagonal QP. We extend to multi-layer merging via a sequential layer-wise algorithm and demonstrate consistent gains across language and vision benchmarks.

</details>


### [134] [When and How Long? The Readout-Mediator Angle in Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29126)
*Shreyas Fadnavis,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Felix Wyss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 线性探针可能会因为学习到模型中“与计算逻辑正交”的方向而产生虚假的解释，这表明探针在监测模型安全或理解推理机制时存在严重局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探针虽然能高精度解码表征，但不代表模型真正利用了这些信息。研究旨在揭示这种“线性探针解码出的表征与模型实际使用逻辑不相关”的现象，并警示依赖探针进行可解释性分析和模型安全监控的潜在风险。

Method: 使用分布式对齐搜索（Distributed Alignment Search, DAS）定位模型实际的推理计算子空间；测量探针空间与模型计算子空间的“读出-中介角”（Readout-mediator angle）；利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）进行特征分解以验证语义解耦；并在多个模型规模和领域进行验证。

Result: 研究发现探针学习到的方向与模型实际执行日历日期推理的因果子空间在几何上几乎是正交的（Haar均匀分布）。通过逆向工程发现，模型的推理逻辑位于特定的注意力头偏移和MLP特征转换中，而这些与探针的方向完全分离。此结论在不同模型规模、族系及多种任务下均成立。

Conclusion: 线性探针（Linear Probes）往往学习到的是与模型实际计算逻辑正交（无关）的方向。该研究指出，将探针作为模型运行时的安全监控手段是失效的，因为探针可能报告高置信度，但模型实际上并未利用该信息进行推理。

Abstract: A linear probe can decode a representation almost perfectly and yet be completely irrelevant to how the model uses it. On calendar-date duration reasoning in language models, a $\sin$/$\cos$ probe recovers day-of-year from a layer's activations, yet ablating its direction has no effect on the model's answers -- while ablating a four-dimensional subspace found by Distributed Alignment Search (DAS) at the same layer collapses performance entirely. We measure the angle between these two subspaces -- the \emph{readout-mediator angle} -- and find it indistinguishable from the angle between two random subspaces (the Haar-uniform null), meaning the probe has learned a direction orthogonal to the model's actual computation. Reverse-engineering the circuit reveals why: attention heads route month-grained context through learned QK offsets at ${\pm}30$ and ${\pm}61$ days, and MLPs then convert \emph{when} (absolute date) into \emph{how long} (duration) -- all downstream of the causal subspace the probe never touches. Sparse-autoencoder decomposition confirms the split: probe-aligned and DAS-aligned features encode semantically disjoint concepts with negligible causal overlap. The dissociation replicates across four scales ($1.5$-$9\,$B) and two model families, with preliminary evidence on two further domains (spatial displacement, symbolic arithmetic), suggesting that readout-mediator orthogonality is a general failure mode of probe-based interpretability. This directly undermines proposals to deploy probes as runtime safety monitors: the probe can report high confidence on a direction the model has silently abandoned.

</details>


### [135] [Apertus LLM Family Expansion via Distillation and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29128)
*Andrei Panferov,Davit Melikidze,Martin Jaggi,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过蒸馏与量化技术，以 Apertus 8B 为基础，成功推出了一系列更小规模的 Apertus-v1.1 模型，为满足多样化硬件需求提供了低成本且高性能的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大语言模型（LLM）应用场景广泛，迫切需要模型能够适应不同的预算和硬件资源限制。厂商通常会发布不同规格的模型系列以覆盖这些需求。

Method: 利用现有的开源 Apertus 8B 模型作为基础，通过模型蒸馏技术构建出一系列参数量较小的模型（最高达 4B 参数），并在 1.7T 许可数据集上进行训练和优化。

Result: 成功构建并展示了 Apertus-v1.1 模型系列，实验结果表明该方法在保持较强性能的同时，具备出色的成本效益，能有效覆盖多种计算平台。

Conclusion: 蒸馏与量化是扩展大语言模型系列、满足多样化硬件与预算约束的高效且经济的选择。

Abstract: The wide adoption of LLMs has led to their use in great variety of applications and scenarios, such as chatbot assistants and data annotation, creating the need for the models to satisfy certain budget and hardware constraints. This has led to the trend of LLMs being released in batches consisting of similar models of various sizes for the family of models to adhere to as wide of a range of constraints as possible. In this paper, we validate distillation and quantization as a cost-effective way to expand model families to new sizes and hardware formats. Based on the open-recipe Apertus 8B LLM, we produce Apertus-v1.1 - a distilled family of models with up to 4B parameters trained on 1.7T permissive license tokens. We demonstrate cost-efficiency and strong accuracy performance of our approach for covering large ranges of hardware and systems requirements.

</details>


### [136] [Unveiling Multi-regime Patterns in SciML: Distinct Failure Modes and Regime-specific Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29153)
*Yuxin Wang,Yuanzhe Hu,Xiaokun Zhong,Xiaopeng Wang,Haiquan Lu,Tianyu Pang,Michael W. Mahoney,Yujun Yan,Pu Ren,Yaoqing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种诊断科学机器学习（SciML）模型训练状态的框架，揭示了其普遍存在的三种训练状态，并证明了优化性能在这些状态间存在显著差异，为理解SciML模型失效及优化提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习（SciML）模型在不同超参数设置下会进入不同的训练状态，且表现出显著的质量差异；现有的分析方法难以解释这些复杂行为，缺乏针对性的失效诊断。

Method: 提出了一种“状态感知诊断框架（regime-aware diagnostic framework）”，通过联合分析模型的性能、训练动态以及损失景观（loss-landscape）几何特性来识别和评估不同的训练状态。

Result: 1. 在多种SciML模型、约束条件和优化器设计中发现了一致的三状态结构；2. 优化效果具有状态特异性，不存在单一优化器在所有状态下均表现最优；3. SciML模型存在精细化的失效模式，挑战了对常规损失景观指标的传统解读。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一种分析科学机器学习（SciML）模型失效模式的统一、与任务无关的视角，为提高模型鲁棒性提供了基于“训练状态（regime）”的指导建议。

Abstract: Neural networks trained under different hyperparameter settings can fall into distinct training "regimes," with consistent behavior within regimes and qualitative differences across regimes. In this paper, we study such multi-regime behavior in scientific machine learning (SciML) models through a regime-aware diagnostic framework that jointly analyzes performance, training dynamics, and loss-landscape geometry. We identify three key findings: (i) a consistent three-regime structure emerges across many standard SciML models, different constraint enforcements, and various optimizer designs; (ii) optimization effectiveness is regime-specific, with no single method performing well across all regimes; and (iii) SciML models can exhibit fine-grained failure modes that can challenge conventional interpretations of standard loss-landscape metrics. Our results provide an approach to establish a unified, task-oblivious perspective on failure modes in SciML and to inform regime-aware guidance for improving robustness. We validate these findings across widely-used SciML models, including physics-informed neural networks, neural operators, and neural ordinary differential equations, on benchmarks spanning representative ordinary and partial differential equations.

</details>


### [137] [RUBRIC-ARROW: Alternating Pointwise Rubric Reward Modeling for LLM Post-training in Non-verifiable Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29156)
*Haoxiang Jiang,Zihan Dong,Tianci Liu,Wanying Wang,Ran Xu,Tony Yu,Linjun Zhang,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RUBRIC-ARROW 是一种新型奖励建模框架，通过联合训练生成器与判别器，并利用基于概率的评分和交替式训练方案，解决了主观任务中奖励模型评分不准及平局的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的点对点奖励建模在处理主观、不可验证的任务时，难以给出准确的绝对评分；而现有的基于准则（Rubric-based）的方法过度依赖前沿大模型，且因硬布尔聚合导致评分平局较多。

Method: 1. 提出 RUBRIC-ARROW 框架，联合训练 rubric 生成器和受 rubric 约束的 judge；2. 在 RL 阶段仅利用成对偏好数据；3. 采用基于概率的评分规则以减少平局现象；4. 使用交替式 GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）方案训练点对点评估器。

Result: 实验表明，RUBRIC-ARROW 达到了具有竞争力的奖励建模准确性，并能为下游策略的后训练带来持续的性能提升。

Conclusion: RUBRIC-ARROW 为大模型后训练提供了一种高效、稳健的点对点奖励建模方案，通过联合训练和概率评分机制解决了传统方法在主观评价中的局限性。

Abstract: Pointwise reward modeling offers critical signals for LLM post-training, yet struggles with absolute scoring in subjective, non-verifiable settings. Rubric-based methods address this by decomposing evaluation into explicit criteria, but existing approaches typically depend on frontier LLMs and suffer from ties caused by hard Boolean aggregation. We present RUBRIC-ARROW, an alternating framework that jointly trains a rubric generator and a rubric-conditioned judge, with its RL stage using only pairwise preference data. Our method couples a probability-based scoring rule that reduces ties with phase-specific preference-based rewards and an alternating GRPO scheme that together train the pointwise evaluator. Extensive experiments show that RUBRIC-ARROW achieves competitive reward-modeling accuracy and yields consistent gains for downstream policy post-training.

</details>


### [138] [Parallax: Parameterized Local Linear Attention for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29157)
*Yifei Zuo,Dhruv Pai,Zhichen Zeng,Alec Dewulf,Shuming Hu,Zhaoran Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Parallax 提出了一种参数化的局部线性注意力机制，通过解决 LLA 的扩展性难题与数值不稳定性，并配合硬件优化及与 Muon 优化器的协同设计，实现了在性能与效率上的帕累托改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统注意力机制（Softmax Attention）的计算原始结构长期未变，而现有的局部线性注意力（LLA）因数值不稳定和计算复杂性难以扩展到大语言模型（LLM）的预训练中。

Method: 1. 结构优化：引入参数化局部线性注意力（Parallax），移除了 LLA 的数值求解器，改为学习额外的查询投影（query-like projector）来探测 KV 协方差。
2. 系统工程：开发硬件感知算法，提高算术强度，使其比 FlashAttention 更具计算密集型优势。
3. 协同设计：发掘了与 Muon 优化器的协同效应，实现了架构与优化器的联合优化。

Result: 1. 在 0.6B 和 1.7B 规模预训练中，困惑度（Perplexity）表现优于 Baseline。
2. 解码核心（Decode kernel）性能匹配或超越 FlashAttention 2/3。
3. 证明了在参数量和算力对齐的情况下，Parallax 具有更好的性能表现，且从预训练迁移到下游任务的效果显著。

Conclusion: Parallax 通过参数化重构局部线性注意力，解决了传统 LLA 在数值稳定性和计算效率上的难题。研究证实了架构与优化器（特别是 Muon）之间的协同作用，实现了在同等参数和算力下的帕累托改进（Pareto improvement），为大模型高效注意力机制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become the central paradigm in artificial intelligence, yet the core computational primitive of attention has remained structurally unchanged. Local Linear Attention (LLA) is an attention mechanism derived from nonparametric statistics in the test-time regression framework. In contrast to prior research on efficient attention variants, LLA upgrades the local constant estimate in softmax attention to a local linear estimate, yielding provably superior bias-variance tradeoffs for associative memory. However, LLA has not been scaled in LLM pretraining due to computational and numerical stability concerns. We introduce Parallax, a parameterized Local Linear Attention that is scalable for LLMs. Parallax eliminates the numerical solver in LLA and learns an extra query-like projector that probes the KV covariance. We place Parallax within a family of attention mechanisms connected by the bandwidth, the probe construction and the affine structure. We propose a hardware-aware algorithm that increases the arithmetic intensity over FlashAttention, shifting attention into a more compute bound regime. Our prototype decode kernel matches or outperforms FlashAttention 2/3 across diverse batch sizes and context lengths. We pretrain Parallax at 0.6B and 1.7B scales and find consistent perplexity improvements throughout pretraining with gains that transfer to downstream benchmarks. The advantage persists under both parameter-matched and compute-matched controls, demonstrating a Pareto improvement. We perform careful pretraining ablations and identify a novel phenomenon whereby Muon unlocks the capacity of Parallax. To our knowledge, this is the first empirical demonstration of strong architecture-optimizer codesign for attention mechanisms in the architecture research literature.

</details>


### [139] [Evolutionary Refinement of Generative Graph Topologies: A Hybrid WGAN-GA Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29161)
*James Sargant,Seyedeh Ava Razi Razavi,Renata Dividino,Sheridan Houghten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对图生成任务，该论文提出结合GAN与遗传算法（GA），通过GA对GAN生成的图结构进行后处理优化，有效降低结构偏差，显著提升了合成图的真实性和结构保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于GAN的图生成方法虽然在一定程度上能建模连接性，但在度分布和谱分布等关键全局结构属性上仍存在显著偏差，未能充分保持真实图的结构特征。

Method: 提出了一种混合框架：首先利用基于GNN的GAN生成初始的节点特征和连接模式；随后引入遗传算法 (Genetic Algorithm, GA) 作为后处理步骤，对生成的图结构（边）进行进化优化，以使其在保留多样性的同时更符合真实图的结构分布。

Result: 实验证明，通过遗传算法优化后的图，其综合最大均值差异 (MMD) 指标显著降低，生成的图在结构模式上与真实数据集的匹配度更高。

Conclusion: 通过遗传算法对GAN生成的图进行后处理 refinement 是一种有效且灵活的手段，能够修正图生成模型中残留的结构偏差，从而提升生成图在结构统计特性（如度分布、谱分布）上与真实数据的拟合度。

Abstract: Generating realistic graph-structured data is challenging due to discrete connectivity, varying graph sizes, and class-specific structural patterns. Recent Generative Adversarial Networks (GAN)-based graph generation methods improve edge modelling by learning connectivity and matching class-specific density distributions. However these models still exhibit noticeable deviations such as in degree and spectral distribution when compared to real graphs, indicating that important structural properties are not fully preserved. This work aims to reduce these deviations by refining the graphs produced by an existing GAN-based graph generator framework with a Genetic Algorithm (GA). In the GAN framework, the generator produces both node features and connectivity patterns, while a GNN-based critic evaluates graph realism and class consistency to ensure global structural and class alignment. Building on this foundation, we apply a GA to refine the edges of generated graphs. The refinement process guides synthetic graphs toward closer agreement with real data, while preserving diversity and novelty. Experimental results show that the GA refinement consistently lowers combined Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) compared to the base model, leading to graphs that more closely match real structural patterns. This demonstrates that evolutionary refinement is an effective and flexible way to correct residual structural deviations in GAN-based graph generators, improving their suitability for realistic graph synthesis and data augmentation.

</details>


### [140] [TIMEGATE: Sustainable Time-Boxed Promotion Gates for Continual ML Adaptation Under Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29183)
*Abhijit Chakrabroty,Suddhasvatta Das,Kevin A. Gary,Yash Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 TIMEGATE，一个用于持续学习系统的资源管理策略层，通过动态预算分配和部分评估决策，在保证性能的前提下显著降低了计算、标记和能源成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习系统向持续适应（continual adaptation）演进，频繁的再训练循环带来了高昂的计算、数据标注和能源成本，亟需一种高效的资源管理机制。

Method: 提出了一种名为 TIMEGATE 的策略层，通过对时间、数据标记、训练和评估过程进行预算管理，并引入指标可用性信号 (Metric-availability signal, M) 来动态决策执行部分评估还是完整评估。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 在 Adult 数据集上，标注效率优于训练 2.3 倍；(2) 在 LLaMA-3.1-8B + QLoRA 上性能提升显著 (SST-2 准确率从 0.80 提升至 0.96)；(3) 信号 M 具有高敏感度；(4) 100 次循环模拟节省了 66% 的评估计算成本且无错误提升；(5) 对 LLaMA 进行局部评估节省了 89% 的能耗和运行时间。

Conclusion: TIMEGATE 是一种有效的资源管理策略层，能够在持续学习场景中显著降低计算、标记和评估成本，同时保持模型性能，为资源受限环境下的持续模型进化提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: As machine learning(ML) systems evolve to continual adaptation, each re-training cycle uses compute, annotation, and energy. We introduce TIMEGATE, a policy layer managing adaptation by budgeting time, labeling, training, and evaluation. TIMEGATE emits a metric-availability signal M for partial vs. full-evaluation decisions. We validate: (i) labeling outperforms training by 2.3x on Adult tabular; (ii) it transfers to LLaMA-3.1-8B + QLoRA on SST-2 (accuracy 0.80 to 0.96; M =1 in 35/36 runs); (iii) M is informative, 28-cell sensitivity shows M drops to 0.81 at tight thresholds; (iv) 100-cycle simulation achieves 66% evaluation-compute savings with no silent mis-promotions; (v) 10%-slice evaluation on LLaMA uses 89% less wall-clock and energy on a single H200 (ratios agree to 0.2%).

</details>


### [141] [Influence-Guided Symbolic Regression: Scientific Discovery via LLM-Driven Equation Search with Granular Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29184)
*Evgeny S. Saveliev,Samuel Holt,Nabeel Seedat,David L. Bentley,Jim Weatherall,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IGSR 框架通过引入细粒度的“影响分数”反馈机制，解决了 LLM 进行符号回归时反馈不足的问题，并结合 MCTS 有效提升了复杂函数形式的发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 LLM 的符号回归方法依赖于标量评价指标（如 MSE），无法提供组件级别的细粒度反馈，导致搜索效率低下且难以深入优化方程结构。

Method: 提出了一种“影响引导符号回归”（IGSR）框架，将方程发现分为两个步骤：首先利用 LLM 生成候选基函数，随后通过计算各基函数对泛化准确度的边际贡献（影响分数）进行筛选与剪枝；并将此机制整合进蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）中，以平衡解空间的探索与利用。

Result: 在 LLM-SRBench 等多个基准测试中表现出色，并在处理复杂的生物学数据（DNA 甲基化与 RNA 聚合酶 II 暂停的关系）时发现了具有科学价值的新假设，并得到了实验室湿实验验证。

Conclusion: IGSR 是一种高效的符号回归方法，通过将 LLM 的生成能力与基于影响力的细粒度评估相结合，成功解决了传统评估指标反馈粗糙的问题。它不仅在学术基准测试中表现优异，还在高维生物数据分析中发现了具有实验验证价值的科学新知。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for scientific discovery, yet their application to symbolic regression is often constrained by inefficient search strategies and coarse feedback signals. Current methods typically guide LLMs using scalar metrics (e.g., global Mean Squared Error), which fail to identify which components of a proposed equation are driving performance or causing error. We introduce \textit{Influence-Guided Symbolic Regression} (IGSR), a method that frames equation discovery as an iterative two-step process combining diverse term generation with rigorous selection: an LLM generates candidate basis functions $ψ_j(\mathbf{x})$ for a linear model, which are then evaluated using granular influence scores $Δ_j$. These scores quantify each term's marginal contribution to generalization accuracy, enabling an influence-guided pruning process that systematically refines the model structure. Integrating this mechanism into a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) enables navigating the combinatorial search space while balancing exploration of novel functional forms with exploitation of high-influence components. We demonstrate IGSR's effectiveness on a diverse suite of benchmarks, including LLM-SRBench, pharmacological PKPD models, an epidemiological simulation, and real-world genomic data. Notably, we validate the framework's capacity for genuine discovery in a case study using a high-dimensional biological dataset, in which IGSR identified a novel relationship between DNA methylation and RNA Polymerase II pausing; a hypothesis that was subsequently supported via wet-lab experimentation.

</details>


### [142] [When RL Suppresses Its Own Vocabulary: Recovering Reasoning Diversity in Puzzle-to-Math Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29190)
*Mayug Maniparambil,Arjun Karuvally,Terrence Sejnowski,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了利用约束满足谜题进行RL训练如何提升LLM的跨域推理能力，并提出了一种基于原语跟踪分析框架和新颖性奖励的方案，在不使用任何数学数据的情况下，实现了通用推理能力的显著增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨RLVR在不同领域间的迁移能力及其背后的机制。特别是研究在完全不使用数学数据进行训练的情况下，模型如何通过约束满足谜题训练获得跨域推理能力，并解决RL过程导致的探索性思维（如回溯）受抑制的问题。

Method: 1. 构建推理原语分析框架（9类跨度分类器 + 动机提取），用于跟踪思维链轨迹的演变。2. 采用仅包含约束满足谜题的SFT与GSPO（Generative Sequence Policy Optimization）强化学习流程。3. 引入基于参考模型困惑度的新颖性奖励机制（Novelty Bonus），鼓励模型在RL过程中保留并使用多样化的探索性原语（如假设和回溯）。

Result: 1. 谜题SFT诱导出了推理原语词汇，使OlymMATH-Hard的pass@32提升了7个百分点。2. GSPO通过将原语组合为长链路，进一步提升了6个百分点。3. 引入新颖性奖励机制后，成功恢复了探索性原语，进一步提升了7个百分点。4. 整体方案使7B模型在困难数学任务上的性能从16.0%提升至36.0%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，利用约束满足谜题进行强化学习（RLVR）能够有效诱导模型习得推理原语，并将其跨领域迁移至数学问题中。通过引入新颖性奖励机制（Novelty Bonus），可以防止RL过程中的探索能力退化，从而显著提升大规模语言模型在复杂数学推理任务上的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning using verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves LLM reasoning, but the conditions under which it transfers across domains -- and why it does so -- remain under-explored. We study cross-domain transfer in a 7B model whose SFT and RL post-training stages use only constraint-satisfaction puzzles, with no mathematics problems in the post-training data. To analyze how transfer emerges, we introduce a reasoning primitive-level framework that combines a 9-class span classifier with motif extraction, allowing us to segment chain-of-thought traces into primitive motifs and track their evolution across training stages and domains. We find that puzzle SFT induces a reasoning-primitive vocabulary, yielding a $+7$pp \texttt{pass@32} gain on OlymMATH-Hard. Vanilla GSPO then composes these primitives into longer compute-verify chains, adding a further $+6$pp. However, this RL stage also suppresses exploratory primitives such as \textit{hypothesize} and \textit{backtrack}. To address this, we introduce a novelty bonus that rewards diverse correct rollouts, using perplexity under the reference model as a signal. This restores recovery primitives during RL and adds a further $+7$pp \texttt{pass@32} relative to vanilla GSPO. Finally, the end-to-end recipe raises the hard-math capability ceiling from $16.0\%$ at the OLMo3-7B-Instruct-SFT base to $36.0\%$, without adding any mathematics problems during the SFT or RL stages.

</details>


### [143] [Auditing Training Data in Generative Music Models via Black-Box Membership Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29202)
*Yi Chen Liu,Jiawei Yu,Kexin Cao,Syed Irfan Ali Meerza,Trishika Movva,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种黑盒成员推理方法，通过对比目标模型的生成结果与已知候选音频的特征相似度，能以高精度检测该音频是否被用于模型训练，解决了生成式音乐模型的数据归属透明度难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本到音乐生成模型普遍缺乏数据溯源与训练透明度，导致无法验证特定音频样本是否被用于模型训练，引发了在版权、数据来源及授权方面的严重担忧。

Method: 开发了一种“音乐审计器（Music Auditor）”：通过利用目标模型对特定标题（caption）的生成输出与候选音频在特征空间中的语义及结构对齐程度来判断成员关系。研究者使用影子模型（shadow models）构建成对训练数据，训练二元分类器来捕获这种特有的对齐模式，实现无需模型参数或元数据的黑盒审计。

Result: 该方法在多个前沿音乐生成模型上达到了极高的识别精度（最高达98.6%），且假阳性率（1.9%）和假阴性率（1.0%）极低，证明了其在实际部署场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: 在黑盒场景下，针对音乐生成模型的训练数据版权审计是切实可行的。研究提出的审计方法能够有效检测模型是否使用了特定样本进行训练，为解决版权保护和数据透明度问题提供了技术手段。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-music generation enable high-fidelity synthesis of structured musical audio, raising growing concerns about data provenance, consent, and training transparency. These models are typically trained on large-scale corpora with little disclosure, leaving no practical mechanism to verify whether a particular audio sample was included in training. In this paper, we investigate black-box membership inference for generative music models, aiming to determine whether a candidate music sample was used during training, given only query access to the deployed system. Our key insight is that training membership induces systematically stronger semantic and structural alignment between a candidate sample and the model's generation conditioned on its caption. We query the target model with the associated caption and measure the relationship between the candidate audio and the generated output in a learned feature space. To capture features that separate members from non-members, we construct paired examples consisting of each track and its caption-conditioned generation from shadow models, and train a music auditor to classify membership. The auditor captures alignment patterns characteristic of training membership and generalizes to unseen target models in a fully black-box setting without access to model parameters or training metadata. Across multiple state-of-the-art music generators, our method achieves up to 98.6% accuracy, with false-positive and false-negative rates as low as 1.9% and 1.0%, demonstrating that reliable training-data auditing is feasible in realistic deployment scenarios.

</details>


### [144] [Inferring the Size of Large Language Models From Popular Text Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29223)
*Ivica Nikolic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对LLM参数量不公开的问题，本文提出一种基于模型对大规模预训练语料记忆能力（预测准确度）的黑盒推断方法，通过分析模型在不同长度文本片段上的预测性能，成功复现了闭源模型的规模层级并揭示了行业内各异的Scaling工程策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流大模型（LLM）的参数量通常不公开，而模型规模是评估能力与计算成本的关键指标，缺乏透明的衡量标准。

Method: 提出一种黑盒推断方法，利用模型对经典文献等广泛分布数据的“下一词预测”准确度（记忆水平）作为信号，结合主成分分析（PCA）构建 scaling-law 估计器，并通过成对统计检验进行参数规模的对比。

Result: 在开源模型上验证了该方法的准确性；在闭源模型上的应用恢复了其内部产品层级，并发现不同厂商在模型演进中存在“激进增加参数量”与“维持参数上限”两种截然不同的策略。

Conclusion: 该方法通过模型对已知共用语料的记忆能力推断其实际参数规模，揭示了闭源模型在Scaling策略上的差异，证明了即使在闭源API受限情况下，仍可通过黑盒手段系统性地探测模型的内部设计信息。

Abstract: The parameter counts of the most widely used large language models (LLMs) are often withheld by their developers, leaving model size -- a primary reference point for interpreting capabilities and costs -- largely undisclosed. We propose a black-box method to infer conservative lower bounds on LLM size from generated text outputs alone, requiring nothing beyond the ability to submit text fragments and observe next-token predictions. Our approach is grounded in a key observation: popular, widely-circulated texts -- such as classical literature, religious texts, and foundational documents -- are present in virtually every large-scale pretraining corpus, and how accurately a model predicts the next word across text fragments of varying length is a reliable signal of how much it has memorized them, which in turn is fundamentally limited by its total parameter count. We aggregate this memorization signal across a diverse corpus of texts and fragment lengths into a single accuracy profile vector per model, and build two complementary inference methods on top of it: a pairwise statistical test that determines which of two models is larger, and a scaling-law estimator that extracts a one-dimensional latent index from these vectors via Principal Component Analysis (PCA) to map the aggregated signal to a parameter count. Validated on a broad set of open-weight models, both methods produce accurate and reliable lower bounds. When applied to popular closed-weight models, our framework recovers internal product hierarchies and reveals a clear divergence in industry scaling strategies: while some developers yield significantly higher bounds indicative of large generational parameter growth, others operate under strict parameter ceilings, demonstrating that hidden design choices can be systematically probed even under strict API limitations.

</details>


### [145] [BlockBatch: Multi-Scale Consensus Decoding for Efficient Diffusion Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29233)
*Xiaoyou Wu,Cheng-Jhih Shih,Binfei Ji,Yong Liu,Yingyan,Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对dLLM推理中块大小选择的困境，BlockBatch通过并行运行多种块大小分支并动态协调一致性，在保持准确率的前提下实现了显著的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（dLLMs）的块式推理存在粒度权衡问题：小块局部约束性强但步骤多，大块并行度高但易产生累积误差；现有方法多采用单一固定块大小，未能利用不同块大小之间的互补性。

Method: 提出了一种训练无关（training-free）的在线推理框架 BlockBatch：通过在一个批处理前向传播中执行不同块大小的并行分支，利用置信度门控标记合并（confidence-gated token merging）、基于领导者的同步策略以及周期性的全序列刷新机制来协调这些分支。

Result: 在3个代表性dLLM模型和4个数据集上，BlockBatch平均减少了26.6%的去噪步数（NFE），并实现了相对于Fast-dLLM平均1.33倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: BlockBatch 验证了利用块大小（block-size）的多样性可以作为扩散语言模型推理中一种有效的并行分支维度，在保持生成质量的同时显著提高了效率。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising multiple token positions in parallel, offering an attractive alternative to strictly autoregressive decoding. In practice, however, block-wise dLLM inference exposes a difficult granularity trade-off: small blocks preserve local conditioning but require many denoising steps, whereas large blocks expose more parallelism but can make premature commitments and accumulate cache error. Existing acceleration methods typically choose a single block size per request, leaving the complementarity among block sizes unused. We show that block size itself is a useful branching dimension. Different block sizes induce related but non-identical KV-cache trajectories: branches often share an initial prefix, bifurcate at semantically decisive positions, and later agree on syntactically lightweight tokens. Motivated by this structure, we propose BlockBatch, a training-free online inference framework that executes multiple block-size branches for the same request inside a batched forward pass. BlockBatch coordinates these branches through confidence-gated token merging, leader-based synchronization, and periodic full-sequence refreshes that re-anchor local block updates to a globally consistent KV state. Across 3 representative dLLMs and 4 datasets, BlockBatch reduces denoising NFEs by 26.6\% on average and achieves a 1.33$\times$ average end-to-end speedup over Fast-dLLM while preserving accuracy. These results identify block-size diversity as a practical and previously underexplored axis for branch-parallel dLLM inference.

</details>


### [146] [KLAS: Using Similarity to Stitch Neural Networks for Improved Accuracy-Efficiency Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29259)
*Debopam Sanyal,Anantharaman Iyer,Alind Khare,Trisha Jain,Akshay Jajoo,Myungjin Lee,Clayton Kerce,Alexey Tumanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对模型缝合（Stitching）策略优化难、通用性差的问题，本文提出的 KLAS 框架利用 KL 散度衡量预训练模型的中间表征相似度，实现了自动化的高效缝合配置选择，显著提升了模型的准确率-效率权衡表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型缝合方法多依赖启发式策略，导致缝合后的模型在准确率与效率的权衡上表现欠佳，且缺乏通用性。需要一种能显式评估模型间相似性并自动优化缝合配置的系统。

Method: 提出了一种基于 KL 散度（KL Divergence）的自动化缝合选择框架。该方法通过计算不同预训练模型中间表征之间的 KL 散度，量化模型间的相似性，从而从大量组合中筛选出最优的缝合配置（Stitch Configurations）。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 数据集上，KLAS 在相同计算成本下提升了最高 1.21% 的准确率，或在保持准确率的前提下实现了 1.33 倍的 FLOPs 降低。

Conclusion: KLAS 通过显式评估层级相似度，克服了现有启发式缝合方法的局限性，在不增加微调成本的前提下，显著优化了模型缝合的准确率-效率权衡曲线。

Abstract: Given the wide range of deployment targets, flexible model selection is essential for optimizing performance within a given compute budget. Recent work demonstrates that stitching pretrained models within a model family enables cost-effective interpolation of the accuracy-efficiency tradeoff space. Stitching transforms intermediate activations from one pretrained model into another, producing a new interpolated stitched network. Such networks provide a pool of deployment options along the accuracy-efficiency spectrum. However, existing stitching approaches often yield suboptimal tradeoffs and lack generalizability, as they primarily rely on heuristics to select stitch configurations. We argue that constructing improved accuracy-efficiency tradeoffs requires explicitly capturing and leveraging the similarity between pretrained models being stitched. To this end, we introduce KLAS, a novel stitch selection framework that automates and generalizes stitch selection across model families by leveraging KL divergence between intermediate representations. KLAS identifies the most promising binary stitches from the $O(k^2n^2)$ possibilities for $k$ pretrained models of depth $n$. Through comprehensive experiments, we demonstrate that KLAS improves the accuracy-efficiency curve of stitched models at the same finetuning cost as baselines. KLAS achieves up to $1.21\%$ higher ImageNet-1K top-1 accuracy at the same computational cost, or maintains accuracy with a $1.33\times$ reduction in FLOPs.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Human-in-the-Loop Swarms: A Bionic Swarm Approach to Real-World Soil Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29091)
*Petras Swissler,Mohammadali Rashidioun,Nicholas Sahu,Raaid Kabir,Ayodeji Aderibigbe,Oladoyin Kolawole*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为了解决群体机器人硬件部署成本高昂的问题，本文提出了“Bionic Swarm”系统，通过人机协同（由人类替代物理机器人并由中央服务器指挥）来验证算法，并在室外环境中成功运行了Score-Biased-Search算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体机器人与现场机器人在现实世界中的验证门槛极高（硬件成本昂贵、开发周期长），阻碍了算法的快速迭代与实地研究。

Method: 提出一种“Bionic Swarm”（仿生群体）系统：利用人类作为移动节点，通过智能手机Web应用接收中央服务器的算法指令，并配合蓝牙传感器进行数据采集。算法层面，验证了Score-Biased-Search（分数偏置搜索）算法，该算法通过动态更新地图期望分数来优化搜索路径。

Result: 成功实现了Score-Biased-Search算法在户外真实环境中的部署；实验证明该算法具有优于线性比例的地图重建效率；验证了人机在环（Human-in-the-loop）模式在群体机器人研究中的有效性。

Conclusion: Bionic Swarm提供了一种有效的人机协同框架，通过将低级机器人任务外包给人类，大幅降低了现实世界群体机器人实验的门槛与成本，验证了在室外环境中部署复杂搜索算法的可行性。

Abstract: Swarm and field robotics face significant barriers to real-world validation due to the high cost and development time to deploy hardware. This paper introduces the ``Bionic Swarm,'' a novel system that lowers these barriers by abstracting away many of the tasks that are difficult to implement on robots but which do not contribute to the overall algorithm evaluation, giving these tasks to human users. These human users take directions from a smartphone web-app that takes measurements from Bluetooth-connected sensors and relays them to a centralized server. This server runs the swarm algorithm and directs actions to the human users. We evaluate this system through the experimental validation of a geotechnically-focused search algorithm named Score-Biased-Search, which functions by assigning a ``score'' to each location on a reconstructed map, then biases search patterns through areas of higher expected scores, and which exhibits superlinear map reconstruction relative to the number of search agents. After presenting simulation results for the algorithm, we then apply the algorithm on the Bionic Swarm platform to validate its function in a real-world, outdoor setting. This work demonstrates that this human-in-the-loop approach significantly lowers the barrier to entry for field and swarm robotics research.

</details>


### [148] [CA-AC-MPC: CUDA-Accelerated Actor-Critic Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29155)
*Antoonio Buo,Vittorio Cammarota,Michele Avagnale,Pierluigi Arpenti,Vincenzo Lippiello,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种CUDA加速的AC-MPC实现，解决了可微MPC计算瓶颈，显著提升了复杂动态系统控制任务的训练与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Actor-Critic模型预测控制（AC-MPC）中的可微MPC层需要在训练和推理的每次前向与反向传播中重复求解优化问题，导致了显著的计算延迟（训练和推理速度慢）。

Method: 提出了一种CUDA加速的AC-MPC（Actor-Critic Model Predictive Control）变体，通过硬件加速优化了可微MPC层在前向和反向传播中的计算开销。

Result: 在敏捷无人机竞赛任务中实现了最先进的表现，显著缩短了单圈时间，同时极大地降低了端到端的训练和推理耗时。

Conclusion: 通过CUDA加速的AC-MPC架构，在保持高性能控制的同时，大幅降低了训练和推理的计算开销，为复杂动力学系统的实时控制提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: In the literature, actor-critic model predictive control (AC-MPC) integrates MPC with reinforcement learning to enable high-performance control of complex dynamical systems. However, its differentiable MPC layer requires repeatedly solving an optimization problem in both the forward and backward passes, leading to substantial training and inference latency. This paper tackles this bottleneck introducing a CUDA-accelerated variant that significantly reduces end-to-end execution time while preserving the control performance of the baseline formulation. Simulation results on an agile drone racing task show that our approach achieves state-of-the-art lap times and near-limit dynamic behaviour with markedly reduced training and inference time.

</details>


### [149] [Extreme dynamic symmetry enables omnidirectional and multifunctional robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29254)
*Jiaxun Liu,Boxi Xia,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了“动态对称性”这一机器人设计新范式，通过优化驱动系统的动态各向同性，显著提升了机器人在复杂环境下的运动敏捷性、鲁棒性及多功能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人领域对对称性的应用多局限于几何外形，缺乏从动力学驱动层面对对称性的系统性应用，以解决复杂环境下的敏捷性和鲁棒性问题。

Method: 1. 提出“动态对称性”概念及其量化指标“动态各向同性”；2. 基于1000多种仿真形态进行大规模实验验证；3. 设计开发Argus机器人系列（径向线性驱动器）进行物理原型验证与功能测试。

Result: 1. 仿真显示动态各向同性与任务成功率、鲁棒性及能效呈正相关；2. Argus物理原型实现了方位不变移动、复杂地形穿越、快速自稳定及抗故障性能。

Conclusion: 将对称性从几何形式提升至动态驱动能力层面（动态对称性/动态各向同性），是实现高性能、鲁棒且多功能机器人的通用有效设计准则。

Abstract: Symmetry is a central organizing principle in natural systems, yet its use as a unifying design strategy in robotics has largely remained limited to geometric form. We show that symmetry can instead be leveraged at the level of dynamic actuation capability. We introduce dynamic symmetry, the uniformity of a robot's attainable center-of-mass accelerations, and formalize it through a measure coined as dynamic isotropy. Across more than 1000 simulated morphologies, we found that higher dynamic symmetry consistently improved trajectory tracking, task success, robustness, resiliency, and energy efficiency, with the benefits becoming most pronounced as dynamic isotropy approached its theoretical limit. To study this regime systematically, we developed Argus, a family of spherical robots designed to explore the effects of increasing dynamic symmetry.
