Members of the Argus family vary in their actuation geometry and dynamic symmetry level while sharing a common architectural principle: radially oriented linear actuators that directly shape the robot's center-of-mass dynamics. Among them, we built a physical 20-leg Argus variant that achieved near-extreme dynamic isotropy and demonstrated orientation-invariant locomotion, agile traversal of cluttered and deformable terrain, rapid self-stabilization, and resilience to partial actuator failures. Its distributed sensing further enabled omnidirectional perception and object interaction during continuous motion. These results show that designing robots for symmetry not only in morphology but also in their attainable dynamics provides a powerful and general pathway toward agility, robustness, and multifunctionality in uncertain terrestrial and extraterrestrial environments.

</details>


### [150] [MonoDuo: Using One Robot Arm to Learn Bimanual Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29298)
*Sandeep Bajamahal,Lawrence Yunliang Chen,Toru Lin,Zehan Ma,Jitendra Malik,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MonoDuo 是一种通过单臂机器人与人类协作数据合成双臂机器人训练样本的框架，有效克服了双臂机器人数据稀缺障碍，实现了高效的策略迁移与少样本微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂机器人操作对于现实任务至关重要，但由于双臂机器人硬件和数据集的稀缺，限制了相关策略的学习。该研究旨在探索如何利用广泛存在的单臂机器人数据来训练双臂操作策略。

Method: 提出 MonoDuo 框架。通过单臂机器人与人类协作交替执行任务收集数据，结合手部姿态估计、图像/点云分割以及图像修复技术，将单臂数据合成为双臂机器人的合成演示数据，并利用该数据对策略进行训练及少样本微调。

Result: 在五个任务中验证了 MonoDuo 的有效性，在未见过的双臂机器人配置上实现了最高 70% 的零样本成功率；通过 25 个目标机器人演示进行少样本微调，比从零训练成功率提升了 65% - 70%。

Conclusion: MonoDuo 成功验证了利用单臂机器人演示和人类协作数据训练双臂机器人策略的可行性，有效解决了双臂机器人数据稀缺的问题，并实现了在未见双臂机器人配置上的零样本迁移。

Abstract: Bimanual coordination is essential for many real-world manipulation tasks, yet learning bimanual robot policies is limited by the scarcity of bimanual robots and datasets. Single-arm robots, however, are widely available in research labs. Can we leverage them to train bimanual robot policies? We present MonoDuo, a framework for learning bimanual manipulation policies using single-arm robot demonstrations paired with human collaboration. MonoDuo collects data by teleoperating a single-arm robot to perform one side of a bimanual task while a human performs the other, then swapping roles to cover both sides. RGB-D observations from a wrist-mounted and fixed camera are augmented into synthetic demonstrations for target bimanual robots using state-of-the-art hand pose estimation, image and point cloud segmentation, and inpainting. These synthetic demonstrations, grounded in real robot kinematics, are used to train bimanual policies. We evaluate MonoDuo on five tasks: box lifting, backpack packing, cloth folding, jacket zipping, and plate handover. Compared to approaches relying solely on human bimanual videos, MonoDuo enables zero-shot deployment on unseen bimanual robot configurations, achieving success rates up to 70%. With only 25 target robot demonstrations, few-shot finetuning further boosts success rates by 65-70% over training from scratch, demonstrating MonoDuo's effectiveness in efficiently transferring knowledge from single-arm robot data to bimanual robot policies.

</details>


### [151] [The Open Motion Planning Library 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29301)
*Weihang Guo,Theodoros Tyrovouzis,Emiliano Flores,Clayton W. Ramsey,Zachary K. Kingston,Ioan A. Şucan,Mark Moll,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OMPL 2.0 发布，通过引入硬件加速和与现代 AI 工作流的深度集成，实现了从传统采样运动规划到高性能、智能化架构的重大飞跃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OMPL 自 2008 年发布以来已积累了广泛的算法支持，为适应当前对运动规划实时性及与 AI 工作流融合的需求，需要对库进行架构升级与功能迭代。

Method: 引入硬件加速技术以支持实时运动规划，并优化了软件架构以无缝兼容现代 AI 研究工作流。

Result: 推出了 OMPL 2.0，不仅扩展了算法功能（如优化规划、约束规划等），还通过技术革新显著增强了实时性能和对 AI 开发者的支持。

Conclusion: OMPL 2.0 代表了采样运动规划库在实时性能和 AI 集成方面的重大演进，旨在应对现代机器人领域对高效率与深度学习工作流的交叉需求。

Abstract: The Open Motion Planning Library (OMPL), first released in 2008, has become a cornerstone of the motion planning community, providing implementations of a wide range of state-of-the-art sampling-based algorithms. Over almost two decades of continuous development, we have steadily expanded the library with new planners, state spaces, and problem formulations. These additions range from asymptotically optimal and lazy planners to constrained motion planning and planning with temporal-logic goals. Building on this foundation, we introduce OMPL 2.0, a major evolution of the library that targets real-time motion planning through hardware acceleration and integrates seamlessly with modern AI research workflows. We also reflect on how OMPL and the field of motion planning have grown together over the years, and discuss the library's broader impact on the research community.

</details>


### [152] [Phase-Conditioned Imitation Learning with Autonomous Failure Recovery for Robust Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29407)
*Dayuan Chen,Kai Tang,Yukuan Zhang,Kazuhiro Kosuge,Yasuhisa Hirata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种阶段条件化、力感知的变形物体操控框架，通过FiLM调制ACT策略并引入多模态阶段预测，解决了传统模仿学习的状态混淆问题，显著提升了机器人执行复杂操控任务的成功率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模仿学习策略（如ACT）在推断时依赖马尔可夫假设，导致在视觉相似但操作要求不同的场景下产生状态混淆（State Aliasing），且缺乏对执行失败的自主恢复能力。

Method: 提出了一种闭环分层架构，包含：1. 基于FiLM调制的阶段条件ACT编码器（实现特征空间解耦）；2. 多模态阶段预测器（融合视觉、力、位姿以实时识别任务阶段并检测隐性接触故障）；3. 混合阻抗控制器（实现柔顺执行）；4. 触觉遥操作接口（用于高质量力感知数据收集）。

Result: 消融实验证明FiLM模块显著优于基线方法且特征解耦效果显著；在双臂挂衣任务中，挂衣成功率从56%提升至87%。

Conclusion: 通过引入阶段感知和力反馈机制，该框架有效解决了灵巧操控中的状态混淆和故障恢复难题，证实了结合任务分段与多模态控制对于复杂变形物体操作的重要性。

Abstract: This paper presents a phase-conditioned, force-aware framework for robust deformable object manipulation. Standard imitation learning policies such as Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) rely on a Markovian assumption at inference, causing state aliasing when visually similar observations require contradictory actions and preventing autonomous recovery from execution failures. We address this with a closed-loop hierarchical architecture. A FiLM-conditioned ACT encoder modulates feature extraction based on the current task phase, enabling a single unified policy to produce phase-specific behaviors while sharing action dynamics across phases. A multi-modal phase predictor fusing visual, force, and pose feedback estimates the phase in real time, detecting contact failures that are invisible to vision alone and autonomously triggering recovery trajectories. The system is completed by a hybrid impedance controller for compliant execution and a haptic teleoperation interface for force-aware data collection. Ablation studies show that FiLM-based modulation significantly outperforms both unconditioned and token-level conditioned baselines, and t-SNE analysis confirms that FiLM induces well-separated, phase-specific feature representations. Validated on hanging and removing a T-shirt with dual arms, the closed-loop system improves the hanging success rate from 56\% to 87\% through autonomous error recovery. Code and videos: https://leledeyuan00.github.io/phaser/

</details>


### [153] [A Progress-Aware Leader-Follower Midair Docking System for Dual-Drone Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29410)
*Yifan Cai,Jan Ming Kevin Tan,Xiangqi Li,Chenzhe Jin,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Valerio Modugno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于小型无人机空中对接的完整软硬件框架，通过领航-跟随者编队策略和被动磁吸机制，实现高效、可靠的自动化对接，并提供了一套标准化的基准评估体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型无人机在模块化合作与空中操作任务中，存在对高精度姿态控制的苛刻要求，且受限于推力和负载，目前缺乏一个可靠且可重复的空中对接实验平台。

Method: 提出了一套基于领航-跟随者（leader-follower）队形的双无人机对接系统，采用轻量化模块化框架与被动磁吸锁止机构；设计了具有阶段感知能力的任务监控系统（approach, alignment, capture, and settle）；基于ROS 2构建软硬件架构，并集成Crazyflie/PX4接口进行仿真与实机验证。

Result: 在仿真和真实飞行实验中，通过编队误差、对接成功率、对接时间及故障模式等定量指标验证了系统的有效性，验证了该平台在空中协同对接任务中的稳定性和可评估性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个可重复、模块化的空中对接测试平台，为对比不同对接策略、评估协同控制算法以及推进空中模块化组装技术奠定了基础。

Abstract: Reliable midair docking between small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) is essential for modular aerial cooperation and manipulation, but it requires precise relative-pose control and repeatable platform under tight thrust and payload constraints. We present a dual-drone docking platform where two quadrotors operate in a leader-follower formation and dock using a lightweight modular frame with passive magnetic latching. A progress-aware mission supervisor manages phase transitions: approach, alignment, capture, and settle. This platform integrates a complete hardware-software stack (ROS 2 with Crazyflie/PX4 interfaces) and synchronized logging for benchmark evaluation. We evaluate the platform in simulation and real-world experiments using quantitative metrics such as formation error, baseline and yaw consistency, docking success rate, time-to-dock, and failure-mode statistics. The platform enables statistically grounded comparison of docking supervision and synchronization strategies and provides a practical testbed for modular aerial cooperation and repeatable midair aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [154] [3DVLA: Enhancing Vision-Language-Action Models via 3D Spatial and Instance Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29416)
*Zhongyu Xia,Yousen Tang,Bingqing Wei,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 3DVLA 是一种无需额外标注的即插即用框架，通过引入多视图一致性、实例感知模块和掩码自监督分支，解决了 VLA 模型在 3D 空间理解和遮挡处理上的不足，提升了机器人的操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型缺乏 3D 场景理解能力，导致在处理空间定位、3D 实例感知及遮挡下的推理时表现脆弱，且现有 3D 感知方法难以直接集成到 VLA 中。

Method: 1. 引入具有显式多视图一致性约束的 3D 特征编码和空间条件几何聚合方法；2. 开发了基于高层实例标记的实例估计模块；3. 设计了掩码自监督 3D 编码分支以实现视觉标记补全，从而应对遮挡问题。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Plus 和 RoboTwin 2.0 数据集上的实验表明，集成 3DVLA 后，基准 VLA 模型在操作任务中的性能取得了持续且显著的提升。

Conclusion: 3DVLA 是一种即插即用的框架，能够有效为预训练的 VLA 模型注入鲁棒的 3D 推理能力，无需额外的手动标注，显著提升了机器人操作任务在复杂 3D 场景下的性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models have achieved remarkable progress in robotic manipulation, yet they suffer from a critical limitation: a lack of 3D scene understanding. This deficiency manifests as three intertwined challenges: weak extraction of 3D spatial positions without enforcing multi-view consistency, inadequate 3D instance understanding, and fragile reasoning under occlusion. Although mature 3D perception methods exist, their direct integration into VLA pipelines is hindered by architectural incompatibility and by heavy reliance on costly instance-level annotations. To address the above challenges, we propose 3DVLA, a plug-and-play framework that injects robust 3D reasoning into pretrained VLAs without requiring extra manual labels or discarding VLM priors. Specifically, 3DVLA tackles the three challenges through: (1) pervasive 3D feature encoding with explicit multi-view consistency constraints across all modalities and a Spatially-Conditioned Geometry Aggregation method, (2) an instance estimation module with high-level instance tokens for 3D instance awareness, and (3) a masked self-supervised 3D encoding branch that retains its predictor for visual token completion to handle occlusions. We integrate 3DVLA with multiple VLA baselines and evaluate on LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0. Results show consistent and significant gains in manipulation performance, validating both the effectiveness and plug-and-play compatibility of our approach.

</details>


### [155] [ElegantVLA: Learning When to Think for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29438)
*Ye Li,Huanan Liu,Kangye Ji,Yuan Meng,Jiajun Fan,Yuansong Wang,Shiyu Qin,Chenglei Wu,Shu-Tao Xia,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ElegantVLA 是一种无需微调的插件式推理框架，通过动态调度计算资源（在感知和动作生成阶段复用计算），显著提升了 VLA 模型的推理速度并增加了实时机器人控制频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型在大规模计算成本和低推理频率之间存在矛盾，且现有优化方法忽略了具身控制中不同步骤非均匀的推理需求。借鉴人类运动控制中资源动态分配的特性，旨在通过学习“何时投入全量计算，何时复用计算”来加速推理。

Method: 提出 ElegantVLA 插件式阶段自适应推理框架。通过轻量级调度器观察时间表征相似性、机器人运动线索和任务进度，对视觉编码器、大语言模型和动作头进行动态计算分配：感知层采用五级多步时间重用机制，动作生成层采用三级去噪模式重用。

Result: 在 GR00T 和 CogACT 上实现了高达 2.55 倍和 3.77 倍的加速；在 6 项现实世界 GR00T 任务中，计算量减少 2.18 倍，控制频率从 13.8 Hz 提升至 26.3 Hz。

Conclusion: ElegantVLA 通过引入动态计算调度机制，无需修改或重训基座模型即可实现 VLA 模型的高效推理，在提升控制频率的同时显著降低计算成本。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a powerful paradigm for generalist robotic control. However, their high computational cost and limited control frequency hinder real-time robotic manipulation, especially when large vision-language backbones and iterative action heads run at every control step. Existing VLA acceleration methods often optimize individual components or rely on fixed acceleration rules, treating different control steps with largely fixed computation and overlooking the non-uniform reasoning demands of sequential embodied control. Inspired by human motor control, where cognitive and feedback resources concentrate on goal-sensitive stages, we argue that VLA models should learn when to invest full computation and when to reuse prior computation. We propose ElegantVLA, a plug-in phase-adaptive inference framework that accelerates VLA models through intra-model dynamic compute scheduling. ElegantVLA introduces a lightweight scheduler that observes temporal representation similarity, robot-motion cues, and episode progress to jointly allocate computation across the vision encoder, LLM, and action head. For perception-language reasoning, the scheduler selects a five-level Vision-LLM compute mode, from full recomputation to multi-step temporal reuse, based on visual-language representation stability. For action generation, it selects a three-level denoising mode, reusing intermediate denoising states during stable motion while preserving full refinement for goal-sensitive stages. By coordinating these decisions, ElegantVLA offers a general acceleration framework for modern VLA pipelines with explicit action-generation modules, without modifying or retraining the base model. Experiments on GR00T and CogACT achieve up to 2.55x and 3.77x speedup, and on six real-world GR00T tasks ElegantVLA cuts computation by 2.18x while raising control frequency from 13.8 Hz to 26.3 Hz.

</details>


### [156] [VLA-Pro: Cross-Task Procedural Memory Transfer for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29562)
*Shengyu Si,Yuanzhuo Lu,Ruimeng Yang,Ziyi Ye,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-Pro 是一种通过检索并动态集成预训练任务特定 LoRA 适配器（作为程序性记忆）来增强 VLA 模型跨任务泛化能力的即插即用框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在跨对象、场景和动作模式的未见任务上泛化能力不足，难以有效地迁移既往经验。

Method: 提出 VLA-Pro 框架：在训练阶段将任务特定的 LoRA 适配器存储为“程序性记忆”；在推理阶段，利用当前多模态上下文检索最相关的记忆，并通过动态融合这些记忆来生成操作动作。

Result: 在 RoboTwin、RLBench 和真实世界操作任务中表现优异，模拟环境性能相对提升达 207%，真实世界操作成功率从 5.8% 显著提高至 65.0%。

Conclusion: 通过检索和融合任务相关的“程序性记忆”（LoRA适配器），VLA-Pro能够有效实现跨任务经验迁移，在保持模块化和执行稳定性的前提下，显著提升了机器人模型对未见任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action~(VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robotic manipulation, yet they still struggle to generalize to unseen tasks that necessitate transferring relevant experience across objects, scenes, and action patterns. This paper proposes VLA-Pro, a plug-and-play framework designed to enhance cross-task generalization by storing task-relevant procedural memories at training time and transferring these memories during inference. Specifically, VLA-Pro stores task-specific LoRA adapters as parameterized procedural memories during training. At inference time, VLA-Pro retrieves relevant procedural memories based on the current multi-modal context and dynamically fuses these memories for generating the current action chunk. Experiments on RoboTwin, RLBench, and real-world manipulation tasks show that VLA-Pro consistently improves cross-task generalization across multiple backbones, achieving up to a 207% relative improvement in simulation and increasing real-world success rate from 5.8% to 65.0%. These results suggest that procedural memory retrieval and adaptation provide an effective mechanism for transferring manipulation experience to novel tasks while preserving modularity and execution stability.

</details>


### [157] [VE2VF: Vision-Enabled to Vision-Free Distillation via Real-world Reinforcement Learning for Robust Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29564)
*Victor Kowalski,Chengxi Li,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需仿真领域随机化的真实世界强化学习框架，通过视觉“教师”向本体感知“学生”蒸馏知识，实现了在复杂组装任务中的高效学习与高鲁棒泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视觉强化学习策略虽能加速学习，但在真实环境下的泛化性和鲁棒性较差，极易对训练时的视觉条件过拟合。

Method: 提出一种人机协作（Human-in-the-loop）的强化学习框架，利用视觉策略作为教师（Teacher），通过知识蒸馏（Distillation）将其技能迁移给仅依赖本体感知（Pose, Twist, Wrench）的学生策略（Student）。训练过程完全在真实世界中进行，无需领域随机化或数据增强。

Result: 在NIST装配基准测试中，经50分钟训练后达到95%的整体成功率；在8个未见过的任务变体上表现出很强的泛化能力；微调后在最具挑战性的任务上达到100%成功率，且性能优于对比基线。

Conclusion: 人机协作的教师-学生蒸馏框架能够有效解决视觉策略对环境的过拟合问题，在仅依赖触觉/本体感知传感的情况下，实现了高性能、高稳健性及广泛的迁移能力。

Abstract: When using reinforcement learning (RL) for contact-rich robotic manipulation, vision can provide task-relevant information that accelerates learning beyond what proprioception alone can achieve. However, vision-enabled policies tend to overfit to the visual conditions seen during training, limiting their robustness and transferability. We present a human-in-the-loop RL framework that employs teacher-student distillation to achieve robust performance across multiple task variants, trained entirely in the real world without requiring domain randomization or data augmentation. A vision-enabled teacher distills its knowledge into a vision-free student that relies solely on pose, twist, and wrench sensing, combining fast training with strong task generalization. On the real-world NIST assembly benchmark board, our approach achieves 95\% overall success after approximately 50 minutes of training on 3 representative tasks, including robust generalization to 8 unseen task variants. Fine-tuning with distillation achieves full success on the most challenging task. We demonstrate that the resulting policies outperform baselines in both robustness and adaptability.

</details>


### [158] [Learning to Feel Materials from Multisensory Tactile Data via Interpretable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29572)
*Li Zou,Yasemin Vardar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个可解释的触觉信号计算框架，证实了多模态触觉交互（尤其是热觉与顺应性）对材料识别预测的核心作用，为高保真触觉界面与类人机器人的研发提供了理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类对材料的触觉感知机制尚不明晰，这一知识鸿沟阻碍了触觉技术在数字环境中的应用以及具备类人感知能力的机器人的开发。

Method: 提出一种包含三项互联模型的可解释计算框架：模型1将手指-表面交互特征映射为心理物理感官属性；模型2基于感知表征对材料进行分类；模型3直接利用触觉特征进行材料分类。框架整合了按压、静态接触及滑动交互。

Result: 结合按压、静态接触与滑动交互的多模态信息能提升预测准确率，其中热觉信号在感知建模与材料分类中具有极高的信息价值。

Conclusion: 热觉与形变（compliance）感官信息对人类触觉感知至关重要，但目前在机器人触觉与力反馈技术中被显著低估。将这些特征整合至人工系统中，可显著提升其对人类触觉感知的还原度。

Abstract: Human tactile perception of materials relies on complex multisensory touch cues, yet the relationship between low-level tactile signals and perceptual representations remains poorly understood. This knowledge gap hinders the integration of touch in digital environments and the development of robots capable of human-like tactile perception. Here, we present an interpretable computational framework for modeling human material perception and recognition using multisensory touch data. Our framework comprises three interconnected models: Model 1 maps finger-surface interaction features to psychophysical sensory attributes, Model 2 classifies materials based on these perceptual representations, and Model 3 directly classifies materials from tactile features. The results showed that combining information from pressing, static contact, and sliding interactions improves prediction accuracy, and that thermal cues are particularly informative for both perceptual modeling and material classification. These findings highlight the importance of thermal and compliance cues, which remain underrepresented in current robotic fingers and haptic displays. Incorporating such cues may enhance artificial systems' ability to approximate human material perception and guide the design of more perceptually grounded haptic interfaces.

</details>


### [159] [How to Relieve Distribution Shifts in Semantic Segmentation for Off-Road Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29599)
*Ji-Hoon Hwang,Daeyoung Kim,Hyung-Suk Yoon,Dong-Wook Kim,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对野外语义分割面临的分布偏移难题，本文提出了 ST-Seg 框架，通过引入风格扩展和纹理正则化，提高了模型在不同环境下的分割精度与导航稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外环境中的源-目标域差异及复杂地形导致的传感器噪声造成了显著的分布偏移，传统语义分割模型在训练条件改变时极易失效，导致导航失败。

Method: 提出了 ST-Seg 框架，包含两个核心模块：1. 风格扩展（Style Expansion, SE）：通过生成多样化的真实风格来扩大源域分布的覆盖范围；2. 纹理正则化（Texture Regularization, TR）：利用深度纹理流形来稳定受风格增强学习影响的局部纹理表示。

Result: 在多个存在分布偏移的目标域上进行了实验，结果显示 ST-Seg 相比现有方法有显著的性能提升，表现出更强的稳健性。

Conclusion: ST-Seg 通过风格扩展和纹理正则化有效缓解了野外环境语义分割中的分布偏移问题，显著提升了模型在各种目标域下的稳健性和导航任务的适用性。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is crucial for autonomous navigation in off-road environments, enabling precise classification of surroundings to identify traversable regions. However, distinctive factors inherent to off-road conditions, such as source-target domain discrepancies and sensor corruption from rough terrain, can result in distribution shifts that alter the data differently from the trained conditions. This often leads to inaccurate semantic label predictions and subsequent failures in navigation tasks. To address this, we propose ST-Seg, a novel framework that expands the source distribution through style expansion (SE) and texture regularization (TR). Unlike prior methods that implicitly apply generalization within a fixed source distribution, ST-Seg offers an intuitive approach for distribution shift. Specifically, SE broadens domain coverage by generating diverse realistic styles, augmenting the limited style information of the source domain. TR stabilizes local texture representation affected by style-augmented learning through a deep texture manifold. Experiments across various distribution-shifted target domains demonstrate the effectiveness of ST-Seg, with substantial improvements over existing methods. These results highlight the robustness of ST-Seg, enhancing the real-world applicability of semantic segmentation for off-road navigation.

</details>


### [160] [VLAConf: Calibrated Task-Success Confidence for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29605)
*Dehao Huang,Aoxiang Gu,Chengjie Zhang,Bolin Zou,Wenlong Dong,Zilang Cen,Yue Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLAConf 是一种针对视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型的轻量级、高效置信度估计框架，通过单次前向计算即可评估任务风险，克服了传统方法对大量重采样和离散动作空间的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型置信度估计方法普遍存在推理效率低（依赖重复采样）或难以适用连续动作空间的问题，无法满足开放世界中机器人任务对实时性与鲁棒性的需求。

Method: 提出 VLAConf 框架，利用冻结预训练 VLA 的内部表示，通过轻量级置信度头直接在单次前向传递中估计分步异常分数；并引入步长条件建模（step-conditioned modeling）对操作轨迹中的回放相位信息进行编码。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准测试中，VLAConf 在置信度信号质量上显著超越现有基线，且推理效率大幅提升；其实效性也在真实机器人实验中得到了验证。

Conclusion: VLAConf 提供了一种高效、通用的置信度估计方案，通过单次前向传递即可实现对机器人操作任务的风险评估，在提升推理效率的同时，解决了现有方法在连续动作空间及跨架构适用性上的局限。

Abstract: Confidence estimation for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models is essential for robots to perform manipulation tasks in the open world, providing crucial signals for risk-sensitive decision-making and failure anticipation. Existing confidence estimation methods typically rely on ensemble-based paradigms or action-token probabilities to predict the likelihood of task success. However, they still encounter challenges in computational efficiency and cross-architecture generalizability. These methods usually require repeated sampling, leading to inference inefficiency, and are restricted to VLA models with discrete action outputs, making them difficult to apply to continuous action spaces. To address this issue, we propose VLAConf, a one-class discriminative confidence framework. By leveraging frozen pretrained VLA internal representations, VLAConf directly estimates step-wise anomaly scores in a single forward pass using a lightweight confidence head, thereby eliminating the overhead of exhaustive resampling. We additionally use step-conditioned modeling to encode rollout-phase information along the manipulation trajectory. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark demonstrate that VLAConf significantly improves the quality of the confidence signal constructed for post-hoc calibration, outperforming existing baselines by a large margin in inference efficiency. The effectiveness of VLAConf is further validated in real-robot experiments. To access the source code and supplementary videos, visit https://sites.google.com/view/vlaconf.

</details>


### [161] [EXACT-MPPI: Exact Signed-Distance Navigation for Arbitrary-Footprint Robots from Point Clouds via Path Integral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29663)
*Chen Peng,Zhikang Ge,Wenwu Lu,Haiming Gao,Stavros Vougioukas,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EXACT-MPPI 是一种无需学习的局部导航框架，通过将精确的非凸足迹几何模型直接集成到 MPPI 规划器中，在 GPU 上实现实时碰撞检测，解决了传统简化足迹导致空间利用不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面机器人常携带非凸形状的负载或附件，现有的局部规划器通常将复杂的足迹简化为凸壳或膨胀区域，并利用占用网格进行规划，这种做法在空间狭窄时会丢失大量可行的运动方案。

Method: 提出一种名为 EXACT-MPPI 的无训练局部导航框架。该方法抛弃了传统的中间地图表示（如占用网格），将解析的精确有向距离评估器嵌入到 MPPI 控制器中。利用 JAX 进行批处理，通过 GPU 并行化实现实时滚动时域控制；使用多边形或矩形覆盖来表示复杂非凸足迹，直接在观测点云上评估碰撞。

Result: 实验表明，EXACT-MPPI 在计算效率上超过了基于学习的基线方法，在复杂环境（静态和动态障碍物）中比基于凸足迹的规划器表现出更好的可行性与鲁棒性，且支持多种机器人底盘模型，无需重新训练即可迁移。

Conclusion: EXACT-MPPI 证明了结合精确足迹几何与采样预测控制，无需训练即可实现通用的、足迹感知的局部导航，在保持运动可行性的同时提升了计算性能。

Abstract: Ground robots often carry payloads, implements, or other attachments that turn their effective footprint into complex, non-convex shapes. Navigating safely through clutter then requires reasoning about this true geometry, yet most local planners simplify it with convex or inflated proxies and rasterize sensor data into occupancy grids or distance fields. Both choices eliminate feasible motions when clearance is comparable to the footprint geometry. We present EXACT-MPPI, a training-free local navigation framework that maps local point-cloud observations and sparse guidance directly to motion commands, without any intermediate map representation. The framework embeds an analytic, exact signed-distance evaluator into a Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller. The footprint is represented as a simple polygon for general convex or concave planar shapes, with a rectangle-cover specialization for faster evaluation of rectilinear footprints, enabling footprint-aware collision costs without convex decomposition, inflation, or learned encoders. During each MPPI rollout, observed obstacle points are transformed into the predicted body frame and evaluated against the footprint. All operations are batched in JAX, leveraging GPU parallelism for real-time receding-horizon control. Experiments show that EXACT-MPPI accelerates batched distance evaluation over a learned point-to-robot baseline, preserves feasible motion where convex-footprint planners fail, and remains robust under dense static and moving obstacles. The same framework deploys on differential-drive, Ackermann, omnidirectional, and hybrid-mode platforms by changing only the footprint description and motion model without per-platform training. Pairing exact footprint geometry with sampling-based predictive control thus offers a practical, training-free path to footprint-aware local navigation across diverse robots.

</details>


### [162] [FLIP: Real-Time and Resilient Formation Planning for Large-Scale DIstributed Swarms via Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29704)
*Yuan Zhou,Guangtong Xu,Zhenyu Hou,Jialiang Hou,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于时空点云配准（PCR）的分布式大规模无人机编队规划方法，通过将位置匹配转化为点云配准问题，在保证计算高效的同时，提升了编队的抗干扰能力和协同性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的传统大规模编队规划方法在编队表示简化（导致性能低下）或完全协作关系（导致计算开销过大）之间存在权衡，缺乏高效的大规模方案。

Method: 将最优编队位置序列（OFPS）计算转化为时空点云配准（PCR）问题，并引入抗异常值机制；智能体分布式计算匹配结果并据此优化协作编队轨迹。

Result: 通过120架无人机的仿真实验验证，该方法在规模和效率上均优于现有的先进（SOTA）方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为大规模无人机集群提供了一种弹性、高效且分布式的轨迹规划方案，有效解决了大规模编队中计算复杂度和性能平衡的难题。

Abstract: Traditional large-scale formation planning either oversimplify the formation representation which leads to poor performance, or they employ complete collaborative relationships, which results in excessive computational load. To achieve high-performance and large-scale formation planning, we transform the Optimal Formation Position Sequence \cite{c1} (OFPS) calculation problem into a spatiotemporal Point Cloud Registration (PCR) problem. Each agent derives its OFPS by distributively computing the matching result between current positions and the desired formation positions of all other agents. Then each agent optimizes the cooperative formation trajectory by using OFPS. We leverage the PCR method with outlier rejection to rapidly perform large-scale formation position registration. This prevents suboptimal trajectories and failed agents from propagating through the cooperative network and affecting more agents. Consequently, we uniformly achieve resilient, efficient, and distributed trajectory planning for large-scale swarms. The effectiveness and the superiority of the proposed method are demonstrated through large-scale simulations of 120-drone formation, and rigorous benchmarking against state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods.

</details>


### [163] [PhAIL: A Real-Robot VLA Benchmark and Distributional Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29710)
*Sergey Arkhangelskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对现有机器人VLA模型评估方法在大样本量下表现不足的问题，本文提出了PhAIL基准与基于分布的评估指标，提升了模型性能对比的统计可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前现实世界中通过视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型评估机器人表现的方法存在样本量少、缺乏统计显著性分析以及仅依赖固定超时二进制成功率的问题，导致难以可靠区分模型性能。

Method: 提出PhAIL基准测试，采用时间成功累积分布函数（CDF）作为评估基元；引入“人类相对吞吐量”（HRT）指标，结合Bootstrap置信区间；使用柯尔莫哥洛夫-斯米尔诺夫（KS）检验进行显著性分析。

Result: 在四种公开VLA模型上的实验表明，相比传统的二进制指标，KS检验可以在较小的样本量下成功区分更接近的模型；评估显示表现最好的VLA模型的操作速度仍比人类慢约7倍。

Conclusion: 现有的二进制成功率指标在机器人评估中存在局限性，PhAIL通过引入基于CDF的分布评估方法和HRT指标，显著提升了机器人策略评估的统计效能与可信度。

Abstract: Real-world evaluation of vision-language-action (VLA) policies still rests on binary success rate at a fixed timeout with $N \le 25$ rollouts per condition, almost always without confidence intervals or paired statistical comparison; these cohort sizes struggle to resolve close comparisons reliably. We introduce PhAIL (Physical AI Leaderboard, https://phail.ai), an open real-robot benchmark on a Franka FR3 (dataset, per-rollout artifacts, and end-to-end reference implementation) of a distributional evaluation methodology: the time-to-success cumulative distribution function (CDF) as the evaluation primitive, with two separated jobs. The first is scoring via Human-Relative Throughput (HRT), a dimensionless scalar with bootstrap confidence intervals, anchored to same-fixture human teleoperation. The second is a significance test (Kolmogorov-Smirnov, computed per-object and macro-averaged across objects). On four publicly-available VLAs, the macro-averaged KS test resolves two close comparisons (GR00T vs. ACT, OpenPI vs. ACT) at $N \le 30$ rollouts per (model, object) cell where binary-threshold metrics do not; the closest pair (OpenPI vs. GR00T) remains unresolved within our budget. The best evaluated VLA is $\sim 7\times$ slower per operation (RMST ratio) than the human reference.

</details>


### [164] [MARS Policy: Multimodality Only When It Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29766)
*Jindou Jia,Tuo An,Yuxuan Hu,Gen Li,Jingliang Li,Bohan Hou,Xiangyu Chen,Jiaqi Bai,Bofan Lyu,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MARS 策略通过按需注入随机性，在保证机器人动作多模态生成能力的同时，大幅提升了训练与推理效率，实现了性能与速度的最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式机器人策略虽然具备处理复杂多模态任务的能力，但因依赖重采样和迭代去噪，导致训练复杂且推理效率极低。此外，并非所有任务阶段都需要行为多样性。

Method: 提出了一种名为“模态自适应机器人采样”（Modality-Adaptive Robot Sampling, MARS）的策略。其核心机制是在复杂的多模态阶段动态引入随机噪声，而在单模态阶段自动切换为确定性推理，从而仅在必要时刻启用生成式采样。

Result: 在8项模拟任务和4项真实世界任务中测试表明，MARS 显著优于既有模型，真实世界测试中成功率提升了16.67%，推理延迟降低了83.20%，且在近确定性任务中表现出比纯确定性策略更高的训练效率。

Conclusion: MARS 策略通过在机器人任务中按需提供随机性，成功平衡了生成式策略的多模态能力与确定性模型的执行效率，为机器人操作任务提供了一种高效且高性能的新方案。

Abstract: Imitation learning has become a cornerstone for solving complex robotic manipulation tasks. In particular, multimodality, which enables robots to capture diverse yet valid behavioral patterns, has driven the rapid emergence of generative policies as a dominant paradigm in robot learning. However, achieving such multimodality typically relies on stochastic noise initialization and iterative denoising procedures, resulting in substantial training complexity and low inference efficiency. Meanwhile, not all phases of a robotic task inherently require behavioral diversity. Motivated by this insight, we propose the Modality-Adaptive Robot Sampling (MARS) policy, which adaptively invokes tailored stochasticity only when it is truly beneficial, while reverting to an efficient deterministic learning during single-modal phases. In other words, the proper amount of noise is injected only at the proper time. By selectively activating multimodal generation, MARS policy bridges the gap between the multimodal capability of generative policies and the superior training and inference efficiency of deterministic models. Empirical studies across 8 simulated and 4 real-world tasks demonstrate that MARS exhibits robust multimodal expressivity and high efficiency, with a 16.67% success rate improvement and an 83.20% inference latency reduction in real-world tests. Counterintuitively, MARS also outpaces deterministic policies in training efficiency on near-deterministic tasks by more effectively modeling nuanced action diversity.

</details>


### [165] [Joint Angle Estimation with Customized Wristband Based on Online Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29771)
*Shuo Wang,Xiaobin Chen,Xiaoming Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于两阶段在线增量学习的腕带系统，无需复杂预训练即可通过实时自校准适应不同用户与佩戴配置，实现精确的腕关节角度追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有腕部可穿戴传感器研究多针对特定场景，缺乏通用性，难以适应不同个体、佩戴位置变化以及数据漂移带来的性能下降问题。

Method: 提出了一种两阶段在线增量学习系统：第一阶段利用IMU数据作为真值，通过在线学习实时更新模型以匹配佩戴者的运动特征；第二阶段利用更新后的模型，仅通过腕带传感器即可独立完成腕关节角度估计。

Result: 该系统在多种实验场景下表现出良好的一致性，腕关节轨迹估计的平均误差约为15度，能够有效应对传感器应变变化及不同配置环境。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的增量学习方法有效解决了软体可穿戴传感器在个体差异和佩戴位置偏差下的适应性问题，证明了该系统在动态场景下进行高精度腕关节角度估计的可行性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Intelligent wearable technology plays an increasingly important role in human-computer interaction, motion, and health monitoring. To ensure comfort and practicality of use, one common form for motion monitoring is to utilize soft wearable sensors. However, many research applications regarding wearable sensors are simplistic and difficult to adapt to different situations. This study proposes a system for estimating the angle of the wrist joint using a customized wristband based on an online incremental learning approach. It is a two-stage estimation method: the first stage updates the model based on the wearer's wrist movement characteristics using online learning, integrating real-time data from an IMU as ground truth. The second stage utilizes the updated model for estimation of wrist joint angle solely with the wristband. In other words, model training is completed during data acquisition, allowing the trained model to be used for subsequent angle estimation. This method offers advantages in adapting to data drift caused by variations in different testing configurations, such as the left and right wrists of the same subject, deviations in the wearing position on the same wrist, and even differences among various subjects. The results indicate that the sensors exhibit good performance under strain variations, and the wrist joint trajectory estimation of the proposed system has an approximate error of 15 degree in different scenarios.

</details>


### [166] [LLM-Guided Future Hypotheses for Horizon-Aware Exploration in Multi-Step Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29864)
*Mohammad Khoshnazar,Andrew Melnik,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种利用视频扩散模型预测短期未来场景，并将其作为策略先验（FEC）的新框架。实验证明，该方法能显著提升机器人在多步任务中的探索效率与策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多步机器人操作中面对环境不确定性时的探索难、策略适应性差的问题，探索如何利用短期、任务一致的未来视频预测作为控制策略的先验。

Method: 提出“未来体验条件化”（Future-Experience Conditioning, FEC）框架，通过三阶段生成未来视频片段：(1) LLM 执行推理规划；(2) 数字孪生模拟物体运动；(3) 视频扩散模型合成机器人动作一致的预期视频，并将该表示作为 BC/RL 策略的条件输入。

Result: 在 RoboCasa 和 CALVIN 基准测试中，FEC 显著优于无预测方案（NoFuture）。实验表明，虽然错误的未来预测（WrongFuture）会阻碍学习，但生成的未来预测（GenFuture）能有效提升策略的学习速度与最终性能，且与 RL 微调结合时效果最佳。

Conclusion: 短期未来的视频预测可以作为一种有效的结构化先验，促进机器人操作任务中的探索与策略自适应，即使预测存在一定偏差，该方法仍能提升学习性能。

Abstract: Multi-step robot manipulation requires acting under uncertainty about how the scene will evolve, making exploration and policy adaptation challenging. We study whether short-horizon, task-consistent future videos can provide useful structured priors for control and reinforcement-learning fine-tuning. We formalize this idea through Future-Experience Conditioning (FEC), a simple interface that conditions closed-loop policies on a latent representation of a short future video. In our simulation setup, future clips are generated in three stages, an LLM reasoner operating over a task ontology initialized from the current scene state, a robot-free digital-twin rollout of the intended object motion, and a mask-free video diffusion model that synthesizes a robot-consistent future clip without requiring segmentation at inference. We instantiate this future-conditioning interface primarily with BC and BC+RL, and compare against a future-conditioned Streaming Flow Policy (SFP) baseline on RoboCasa and CALVIN under NoFuture, GTFuture, GenFuture, and WrongFuture. Generated futures improve performance over no-future conditioning, while mismatched futures degrade it, and our BC+RL instantiation achieves the strongest overall results. An average BC+RL learning-curve analysis across 8 CALVIN tasks further shows that GTFuture improves fastest, GenFuture improves earlier and to a higher level than NoFuture, and WrongFuture remains at zero throughout training. These results suggest that short-horizon future videos can serve as useful structured priors for exploration and policy adaptation under imperfect future predictions. https://enact2026.github.io/

</details>


### [167] [Fisher-Preserving Guidance: Training-Free Manifold Constraints for Safe Diffusion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29937)
*Hao Ren,Zetong Bi,Yiming Zeng,Le Zheng,Zhi Li,Zhaoliang Wan,Lu Qi,Hui Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对扩散模型在视觉导航中易偏离训练分布的问题，本文提出了一种无需训练的Fisher Preserving Guidance引导方法，通过低秩雅可比投影实现高效、实时的动作优化与轨迹修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型在视觉导航中，由于标准的采样和测试时引导容易导致轨迹偏离训练分布，从而产生不可靠或低效的路径。

Method: 提出了一种Fisher Preserving Guidance（Fisher保持引导）方法，结合了Outer Product Span Projection（外积跨度投影）技术。该方法利用低秩雅可比矩阵分解计算更新，避免了训练分布漂移（Fisher drift），并引入截断式Fisher去噪敏感度（Truncated Fisher Denoising Sensitivity）作为不确定性度量，用于多样本动作融合。

Result: 在Maze2D、PushT以及真实与仿真环境的视觉导航实验中，该方法相比现有的强基线扩散策略，在性能和实时表现上均有显著且持续的提升。

Conclusion: Fisher Preserving Guidance 能够在不进行额外训练的情况下，有效解决轨迹偏离训练数据分布的问题，提升了扩散模型在导航任务中的实时性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Diffusion models are effective for waypoint prediction in visual navigation, but standard sampling and test time guidance can produce unreliable or inefficient trajectories when updates drift off the training manifold. We propose Fisher Preserving Guidance with Outer Product Span Projection, a training-free inference method that avoids large Fisher drift associated with off-distribution actions while optimizing a task objective. Our method computes the Fisher-preserving update via a low-rank Jacobian factorization, requiring only a single backward pass per step and enabling real-time use. We further introduce Truncated Fisher Denoising Sensitivity as an uncertainty signal and use it for robust multi-sample action blending. Experiments on toy and realistic navigation benchmarks, including Maze2D with TSDF-based guidance, PushT with official Diffusion Policy weights, and visual navigation in simulation and on real robots, demonstrate consistent improvements in performance over strong diffusion-policy baselines without additional training.

</details>


### [168] [Replicable Simulation-Based Robot Validation through Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29973)
*Argentina Ortega,Samuel Wiest,Frederik Pasch,Nico Hochgeschwender*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将数据溯源与FAIR原则整合进机器人模拟测试流程，通过自动化采集元数据，解决仿真测试结果难以重现和透明度不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模拟测试的验证结果缺乏透明度和可复现性。现有的测试配置、执行和后处理流程往往没有完善的元数据记录和溯源，导致测试结果难以重现。

Method: 通过增强现有的基于仿真的机器人测试框架，集成自动化溯源跟踪和元数据收集机制，并利用该机制 enriquecer（丰富）移动机器人导航数据集，使其符合FAIR原则。

Result: 成功验证了在测试框架中集成溯源跟踪的可行性，生成了包含结构化溯源信息和FAIR元数据的移动机器人导航数据集，并总结了实施过程中的实际障碍及应对建议。

Conclusion: 在机器人验证工作流中引入数据溯源（Provenance）和FAIR原则至关重要。尽管存在词汇对齐、属性选择和标准采纳等挑战，但通过在测试框架中集成元数据采集机制，可以实现测试过程的可追溯性和成果的可复现性，并提供了相应的实施建议。

Abstract: Robot behavior is often validated through simulation-based testing, yet the replicability of such campaigns depends critically on transparent documentation of how tests are configured, executed, and post-processed. We argue that data provenance, coupled with the FAIR principles (findability, accessibility, interoperability, and reusability), addresses this gap by explicitly tracking links between artifacts and by attaching machine-readable metadata about file origins and key design decisions. Moreover, provenance and metadata cannot be treated as an afterthought confined to final datasets; they must be integrated into the testing processes that generate those datasets so that evidence can be reconstructed end-to-end. We demonstrate this by augmenting an existing simulation-based testing framework with provenance tracking and metadata collection mechanisms, and by using these extensions to enrich a mobile robot navigation dataset with structured provenance and FAIR-aligned metadata. Finally, we discuss obstacles encountered in this integration -- such as vocabulary alignment, attribute selection, and adoption of domain standards -- and provide actionable recommendations for implementing provenance-centric, FAIR metadata in robotics validation workflows.

</details>


### [169] [BORA: Bridging Offline Reinforcement Learning and Online Residual Adaptation for Real-World Dexterous VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30226)
*Zhongxi Chen,Yifan Han,Yanming Shao,Huanming Liu,Congsheng Xu,Xiaoyu Chen,Yao Mu,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BORA是一个用于机器人灵巧操作的离线转在线RL微调框架，通过动作条件的离线价值评估与在线残差适应，解决了VLA模型在复杂物理环境中的执行误差与泛化难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的VLA模型在处理高维灵巧手控制时存在执行误差累积、样本效率低及物理风险高的问题，导致其难以直接用于复杂的真实世界灵巧操作。

Method: 提出了BORA框架：1. 离线阶段构建整合了VLM认知标记与动作块的评估器（critic），提供动作条件的价值引导；2. 在线阶段固定VLA基础模型，引入轻量级的人在回路（HiL）分块残差适应机制，通过干预驱动的奖励机制进行在线微调。

Result: 在5项复杂的真实世界灵巧操作任务中表现优异，平均成功率较纯模仿学习和传统解耦RL基线提升了33%，在未见物体泛化任务中提升高达43%。

Conclusion: BORA通过结合离线价值指导与在线残差适应，有效解决了VLA模型在处理高维灵巧手操作时的样本效率低及物理环境适应性差的问题，显著提升了机器人灵巧操作的成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for grounding visual-language understanding into real-world robotic manipulation. However, dexterous manipulation remains challenging for VLA policies due to high-dimensional hand control and compounding execution errors, which makes real-world RL post-training essential for bridging the gap between visually grounded action generation and physically reliable dexterous execution. However, high-dimensional dexterous exploration often triggers temporal inconsistency, sample inefficiency and hardware risks in the real world. To address these challenges, we propose BORA, an offline-to-online RL post-training framework designed for real-world dexterous VLA models. In the offline phase, BORA constructs a critic that takes both the VLM's cognition tokens and action chunks as inputs. This design enables action-conditioned value guidance, allowing the critic to evaluate dexterous hand motions beyond visual context alone. During the subsequent online phase, BORA freezes the VLA base and introduces a lightweight, Human-in-the-Loop (HiL) chunk-wise residual adaptation mechanism to mitigate real-world execution errors and further correct the offline-learned intents within the actual physical environment. By inheriting the offline critic and employing intervention-driven rewards, BORA effectively corrects execution discrepancies and adapts to real-world physical variances while preserving the pretrained policy as a stable prior. Extensive evaluations across five complex real-world dexterous tasks demonstrate that BORA significantly outperforms pure imitation learning and traditional decoupled RL baselines, achieving a 33% absolute increase in average success rate under standard settings and up to a 43% improvement in unseen object generalization.

</details>


### [170] [Qwen-VLA: Unifying Vision-Language-Action Modeling across Tasks, Environments, and Robot Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30280)
*Qiuyue Wang,Mingsheng Li,Jian Guan,Jinhui Ye,Sicheng Xie,Yitao Liu,Junhao Chen,Zhixuan Liang,Jie Zhang,Xintong Hu,Xuhong Huang,Pei Lin,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Shuai Bai,Jingren Zhou,Jiazhao Zhang,Haoqi Yuan,Gengze Zhou,Hang Yin,Ye Wang,Yiyang Huang,Zixing Lei,Wujian Peng,Delin Chen,Yingming Zheng,Jingyang Fan,Xianwei Zhuang,Xin Zhou,Haoyang Li,Anzhe Chen,Tong Zhang,Xuejing Liu,Yuchong Sun,Ruizhe Chen,Zhaohai Li,Chenxu Lü,Zhibo Yang,Tao Yu,Xionghui Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Qwen-VLA 是一个统一的视觉-语言-动作基础模型，通过在大规模多源异构数据上进行训练，结合 DiT 动作解码器和具身感知提示技术，成功实现了跨具身平台、跨任务和跨环境的通用智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的具身智能研究往往针对单一任务，导致能力碎片化，缺乏跨任务、跨环境和跨载体的泛化能力。因此，旨在探索是否可以通过单一的视觉-语言-动作模型统一处理异构的具身决策问题。

Method: 提出了 Qwen-VLA 模型，基于 Qwen 视觉语言模型底座，引入了基于 DiT（Diffusion Transformer）的动作解码器；使用大规模多源异构数据进行联合预训练；通过“具身感知提示调节”（embodiment-aware prompt conditioning）处理不同机器人平台；将任务建模为统一的动作与轨迹预测框架。

Result: 在 LIBERO、Simpler-WidowX、RoboTwin、R2R、RxR 等多个基准测试及真实世界 ALOHA 实验中表现优异，在多任务处理及分布外（OOD）场景中展现出稳健的泛化性能。

Conclusion: Qwen-VLA 证明了通过统一的视觉-语言-动作架构结合大规模多源数据训练，可以有效地实现跨任务、跨环境和跨载体的通用具身智能，具备强大的泛化能力。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence is often studied through specialized models for individual tasks such as manipulation or navigation, resulting in fragmented capabilities and limited generalization across tasks, environments, and robot embodiments. In this work, we study whether heterogeneous embodied decision-making problems can be unified within a single vision-language-action model. We present Qwen-VLA, a unified embodied foundation model that extends Qwen's vision-language modeling stack from perception, understanding, and reasoning to continuous action and trajectory generation through a DiT-based action decoder. Qwen-VLA is trained with a large-scale joint pretraining recipe over diverse data sources, including robotics manipulation trajectories, human egocentric demonstrations, synthetic simulation data, vision-and-language navigation data, trajectory-centric supervision, and auxiliary vision-language data. To support multiple robot platforms, we introduce embodiment-aware prompt conditioning, where robot-specific textual descriptions specify the current embodiment and control convention. We further cast manipulation, navigation, and trajectory prediction into a unified action-and-trajectory prediction framework, enabling transferable visual grounding, spatial reasoning, and continuous action generation across robot morphologies, task families, and environments. Experiments on manipulation, navigation, and trajectory-centric benchmarks show consistent multi-task performance and out-of-distribution generalization under variations in scene layout, background, lighting, object configuration, and robot embodiment. Qwen-VLA-Instruct achieves 97.9% on LIBERO, 73.7% on Simpler-WidowX, 86.1%/87.2% on RoboTwin-Easy/Hard, 69.0% OSR on R2R, 59.6% SR on RxR, 76.9% average OOD success in real-world ALOHA experiments, and 26.6% zero-shot success on DOMINO dynamic manipulation.

</details>


### [171] [Gaze2Act: Gaze-Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Policies for Interactive Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30282)
*Kuangji Zuo,Gen Li,Bofan Lyu,Yanshuo Lu,Boyu Ma,Shijia Han,Xinyu Zhou,Xichen Yuan,Chuhao Zhou,Jiaqi Bai,Geng Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Gaze2Act 引入人类视线作为 VLA 模型的动态意图信号，通过跨视角映射和视觉提示，解决了机器人操控中语言描述意图模糊的难题，实现了更高精度的人机协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型仅依赖语言指令往往难以精确表达人类意图，特别是在处理相似物体选择、精细动作位置指定或执行过程中的动态变化时存在局限性。

Method: 提出 Gaze2Act 框架：首先通过跨视角语义匹配将第一人称视线映射到机器人视角，生成物体掩码（目标范围）和视线点（精细目标）；随后通过感知级提示（perception-level prompting）和动作级调节（action-level conditioning）将视线意图融合进 VLA 策略中。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 人形机器人上的 7 类任务、16 项实机操作任务验证显示，Gaze2Act 在意图准确度和任务成功率上达到了 SOTA 水平，在物体消歧和动态意图引导方面显著优于基准模型。

Conclusion: 人类视线（Gaze）是用于人机协作的高效、低负担且具备高度表现力的模态；Gaze2Act 框架有效提升了 VLA 模型在细粒度操作、物体消歧及动态意图理解方面的表现。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently shown strong potential for robot learning by following language instructions. However, in practice, language alone is often insufficient to precisely convey human intent. It is difficult to describe which exact object to interact with among similar candidates, where to act on the object, or how the target may change during execution. To address this limitation, we propose Gaze2Act, a novel VLA framework that leverages human gaze as a dynamic and intuitive intent signal for complex interactive manipulation. Gaze2Act first bridges the ego-exo view gap by mapping first-person gaze into the robot's perspective through cross-view semantic matching, producing both an object mask and a gaze point for coarse-to-fine target specification. These cues are then integrated into the policy through perception-level prompting and action-level conditioning, allowing the robot to attend to relevant regions and execute precise interactions under dynamic intent. In a systematic evaluation across seven task categories and 16 real-robot tasks on a Unitree G1 humanoid, Gaze2Act achieves state-of-the-art performance in both intent accuracy and task success rate. It notably outperforms baselines in object disambiguation, fine-grained interaction, and dynamic intent steering. These results demonstrate that human gaze provides a natural, low-burden, and highly expressive modality for human-in-the-loop VLA control.

</details>


### [172] [A Heterogeneous Architecture for Robot RL Beyond GPU-Dominant Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30313)
*Yufei Jia,Zhanxiang Cao,Mingrui Yu,Heng Zhang,Shenyu Chen,Dixuan Jiang,Meng Li,Xiaofan Li,Yiyang Liu,Junzhe Wu,Zheng Li,XiLin Fang,Tingyu Cui,Shengcheng Fu,Haoyang Li,Anqi Wang,Zifan Wang,Dongjie Zhu,Chenyu Cao,Zhenbiao Huang,Ziang Zheng,Jie Lu,Xin Ma,Zhengyang Wei,Xiang Zhao,Tianyue Zhan,Ye He,Yuxiang Chen,Yizhou Jiang,Yue Li,Haizhou Ge,Yuhang Dong,Fan Jia,Ziheng Zhang,Meng Zhang,Xiwa Deng,Zhixing Chen,Hanyang Shao,Chenxin Dong,Yixuan Li,Yizhi Chen,Bokui Chen,Kaifeng Zhang,Hanqing Cui,Yusen Qin,Ruqi Huang,Lei Han,Tiancai Wang,Xiang Li,Yue Gao,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniLab提出了一种打破GPU仿真依赖的异构强化学习训练架构，通过优化CPU并行仿真与GPU策略学习的解耦机制，不仅实现了显著的训练加速，还极大地增强了系统的跨平台兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人强化学习偏向于“GPU常驻仿真”架构以提升速度，但这导致了对GPU物理引擎的过度依赖。作者旨在证明，高效训练的核心在于系统性地优化仿真吞吐量、策略学习与运行时同步机制，而非单纯依赖物理模拟在GPU上的执行。

Method: 提出了UniLab架构，采用CPU并行模拟与GPU策略更新解耦的策略。核心技术包括：利用MuJoCoUni和MotrixSim实现CPU批处理物理后端，并通过自研的统一运行时环境优化数据移动、缓存及同步机制，确保了异构计算下的高效端到端训练闭环。

Result: 在代表性机器人控制任务中，UniLab在相同硬件下实现了3至10倍的端到端训练效率提升；降低了对NVIDIA CUDA生态的依赖，成功支持Apple macOS、AMD ROCm及Intel XPU等多种硬件后端。

Conclusion: GPU物理模拟并非实现高效强化学习训练的必要条件。UniLab展示了通过高效的异构计算架构（CPU模拟与GPU学习解耦），同样可以实现卓越的训练性能，并显著提升了系统的硬件兼容性与跨平台能力。

Abstract: Simulation-based RL for contemporary robot control is increasingly organized around GPU-resident simulation: physics, rollout collection, and learning are placed on a single GPU-centric execution path. This paradigm has greatly improved training speed, but it has also encouraged a default assumption that efficient training requires physics to reside on the GPU. We revisit this assumption. Our view is that, in simulation-dominated robot control, the essential question is not which processor runs physics, but whether simulation throughput, policy learning, and runtime synchronization form an efficient end-to-end loop. We present UniLab, a heterogeneous CPU-simulation / GPU-learning architecture that decouples CPU-parallel simulation from GPU policy updates through a unified runtime for data movement, buffering, and synchronization. UniLab is implemented as a complete and extensible training system using MuJoCoUni and MotrixSim CPU-batched physics backends, supporting PPO, SAC, FlashSAC, TD3, and APPO. On representative simulation-based robot control tasks, UniLab improves end-to-end training efficiency by 3--10$\times$ under the same hardware configuration, while reducing dependence on the NVIDIA CUDA-based software stack and supporting cross-platform execution on the Apple macOS platform and the AMD ROCm and Intel XPU accelerator backends. These results show that GPU simulation is an effective path to efficient training, but not a necessary one, broadening the practical system choices available for robot RL training. Project page: https://github.com/unilabsim/UniLab.

</details>


### [173] [RoboWits: Unexpected Challenges for Robotic Creative Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30326)
*Chunru Lin,Hongxin Zhang,Fenghao Yu,Zhehuan Chen,Thomas L. Griffiths,Yejin Choi,David Held,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了RoboWits基准测试，旨在自动生成并评估机器人在意外挑战下的认知推理与创造性解决问题的能力，发现当前主流VLA模型在应对变异任务时表现脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准过分侧重于技能执行，缺乏针对真实世界中解决意外挑战所需的“认知推理”能力的评估。

Method: 1. 提出RoboWits：一个新的双臂机器人认知推理基准；2. 开发自动任务生成流水线：采用多智能体协作框架，包含种子任务生成、验证、指标生成、场景生成及任务变异（Task Mutation）模块。

Result: 构建了包含30个种子任务和208个涵盖几何、材料和装配推理的变异任务；实验表明，预训练VLA在基础任务上表现尚可，但在变异任务上性能大幅下降，验证了其在复杂环境下的泛化不足。

Conclusion: 现有的视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）在处理需要推理、策略调整和鲁棒性的任务时表现出明显的脆弱性，现有的机器人基准测试在评估认知决策能力方面存在显著不足。

Abstract: The ability to reason, adapt, and creatively solve problems under unexpected challenges is essential for robots operating in real-world environments. However, current robotic benchmarks primarily emphasize skill-level execution and provide limited insight into such cognitive reasoning capabilities. We introduce RoboWits, a bi-manual robotic benchmark designed to systematically evaluate cognitive reasoning, creative tool use, and robustness to unexpected conditions. To enable scalable construction of high-quality reasoning-centric unexpected scenarios, we propose an automated task generation pipeline formulated as a multi-agent cooperative framework, comprising agents for seed task generation and verification, metric generation, scene generation, and task mutation. Using the pipeline, we curated 30 diverse seed tasks and 208 tasks with mutations and graded difficulty across geometry, material, and assembly-based reasoning. We benchmark popular robot policies, pre-trained VLAs, and oracle-state planners. Our results reveal a significant performance gap: while pre-trained VLAs exhibit preliminary success on seed tasks after single-task fine-tuning, they struggle to perform on mutated tasks, implying their brittleness in manipulation tasks requiring reasoning, strategy adaptation, and robustness to deceptive or constrained environments. Project page is available at https://umass-embodied-agi.github.io/RoboWits.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Constant Depth Threshold Circuits For Exhaustive Epistasis Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29719)
*André Ribeiro,Aleksandar Ilic,Leonel Sousa*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文利用类脑硬件的阈值门电路，通过创新的LIF神经元编码与高效的流水线式总体计数电路设计，解决了生物信息学中计算复杂度极高的上位性检测问题，实现了高效的并行计算并优化了硬件资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模类脑硬件的发展，阈值门电路变得更加实用；而上位性检测（Epistasis Detection）作为生物信息学中最复杂的组合问题之一，需要利用类脑硬件的可配置并行性和存内计算优势，从而突破传统计算在处理此类问题时的复杂度限制。

Method: 1. 提出了一种基于LIF神经元的数据编码及组合生成策略；2. 设计了一种由恒定深度阈值门电路组成的总体计数（Population Count）电路；3. 针对受限的硬件特性（如有限扇入和可变精度），通过构建层次化电路，实现了对数深度和对数立方级线性连接的复杂度，以执行总体计数和二进制数组求和。

Result: 实现了上位性检测的计算效率优化，运行时间仅受限于需要计算的组合数量，无额外开销；同时电路空间复杂度被控制在对数线性尺度，且在考虑硬件限制的情况下，成功将总体计数电路的深度降至对数级别。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了类脑硬件在解决超高复杂度组合问题（如上位性检测）方面的巨大潜力，通过协同式存内计算架构，实现了在不牺牲计算效率的前提下大幅优化电路复杂度，为生物信息学等领域的算力瓶颈提供了创新的硬件加速方案。

Abstract: The development of large-scale neuromorphic hardware has made practical implementations of threshold gate-based circuits a near-term possibility. The complexity advantages regarding traditional computing classes, as evidenced in the literature, have prompted us to tackle Epistasis Detection, one of the most computationally complex combinatorial problems in bioinformatics. We propose specially designed circuits that calculate the relative frequencies of all dataset combinations in an efficient pipelined fashion, taking advantage of co-located memory and configurable parallelism, obtaining complexity gains. Overall, we obtain the runtime to be bounded by the number of combinations to calculate, without any additional complexity overhead, contrary to classical approaches, using log-linear space. To accomplish this, we propose a data encoding and combination generation strategy using Leaky Integrate and Fire (LIF) neurons, that feeds a constant depth threshold gate population count circuit. Accounting for typical hardware characteristics, such as limited fan-in and variable precisions, we obtain logarithmic depth and log-cubic linear connections, for the population count circuit by composing developed unbounded fan-in constant depth threshold gate circuits to perform population count and binary array sum.

</details>


### [175] [Design-Oriented Modeling of TSV Substrate Noise Coupling to Ring VCOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29867)
*Ilias Exouzidis,Alberto Garcia-Ortiz,George Floros,Georgios Panagopoulos*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本研究建立了一个TSV寄生耦合的分析模型，揭示了TSV在3D-IC中作为衬底噪声源对RF电路（如VCO）的具体量化影响，并证实了该耦合存在明显的低通特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D-IC和Chiplet系统中的TSV结构会引入显著的寄生耦合路径，导致敏感RF模块的频谱纯度下降，需要一种有效的手段来评估这种衬底噪声的影响。

Method: 1. 提出了一种用于评估TSV诱发衬底噪声的紧凑型、面向设计的分析方法；2. 推导了信号-地（S-G）TSV对的三端口RLGC闭式解析宏模型；3. 基于22nm FD-SOI工艺，使用环形VCO进行验证，并通过Spectre RF进行多音谐波平衡分析。

Result: 1. 在Aggressor频率为1GHz、幅度为0.5Vpp时，VCO产生-35.2 dBc的边带杂散；2. 杂散幅度随注入噪声幅度增加而单调增加；3. TSV耦合呈现低通响应（500MHz下为-20.2 dBc，2GHz下为-33.1 dBc）。

Conclusion: TSV结构的寄生耦合具有低通特性，随着频率增加对RF电路的噪声干扰减弱。通过提出的RLGC宏模型和哈密顿分析，可以有效量化TSV作为噪声源对敏感模拟/射频电路（如VCO）的影响。

Abstract: Through-silicon vias (TSVs) enable dense vertical interconnects in 3D-IC and chiplet systems, but their metal-oxide-silicon structure introduces significant parasitic coupling paths that can degrade the spectral purity of sensitive RF blocks. This paper presents a compact, design-oriented methodology for assessing TSV-induced substrate noise in mixed-signal circuits. We derive a closed-form analytical three-port RLGC macromodel for a Signal-Ground TSV pair that explicitly exposes the substrate node. The methodology is validated using a three-stage Ring VCO designed in a 22 nm FD-SOI technology, where specific RF devices from the process design kit (PDK) provide direct access to the transistor substrate terminals for controlled noise injection. Multi-tone Harmonic Balance simulations in Spectre RF quantify the impact of TSV aggressors on the oscillator's output spectrum. The results indicate that an aggressor of 1 GHz, 0.5 V$_{pp}$ induces a primary sideband spur of -35.2 dBc. Sensitivity characterization reveals that the magnitude of these sideband spurs increases monotonically with the aggressor amplitude. Furthermore, frequency sweeps demonstrate a low-pass coupling response, where the induced spur magnitude decreases from -20.2 dBc at 500 MHz to -33.1 dBc at 2 GHz.

</details>


### [176] [Precomputed 1D-CNNs for Atrial Fibrillation Detection on Tiny Smart Sensor Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29994)
*Lukas Einhaus,Natalie Maman,Julian Hoever,Andreas Erbslöh,Gregor Schiele*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分组卷积的LUT预计算优化方法，在无需FPGA专用资源（DSP/BRAM）的情况下，实现了高效且高精度的医学时间序列分析加速器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在微型智能传感器系统中利用FPGA实现1D-CNN时，LUT预计算技术虽然低延迟但扩展性差的问题。

Method: 在LUT预计算架构的基础上，引入分组卷积（Grouped Convolutions）以改进卷积块设计，并提出一种超参数寻优算法；采用AMD Spartan 7 S15 FPGA进行硬件实现。

Result: 在MIT-BIH心电图数据集上实现了高达95%的F1-Score，且硬件资源占用极低（仅使用2,844个LUT，无需DSP或BRAM）。

Conclusion: 通过使用分组卷积优化的新型卷积块及其参数优化算法，可以在极小的FPGA资源占用（仅利用LUT）下实现高性能的生物信号分析，证明了该方法在资源受限的边缘设备上的有效性。

Abstract: 1D-CNNs play a crucial role for time-series analysis on tiny smart sensor systems, e.g. for biosignal analysis, predictive maintenance, or structural health monitoring. LUTbased precomputation has emerged as an interesting optimization technique to implement such neural networks on FPGAs. The core idea is to precompute all possible outputs of a neural network layer and store them directly in the lookup tables of the FPGAs. This enables highly resource-efficient networks with ultra-low latency but suffers from poor scalability. Previous work has explored using depthwise-separable convolutions to improve scalability. In this paper, we generalize this approach to consider additional forms of grouped convolutions. Based on this, we propose a novel type of convolutional block and an algorithm to guide the choice of hyper parameters for this block. We evaluate our approach on a medical time-series dataset for predicting atrial fibrillation using the MIT-BIH database (ECG recordings). The resulting hardware accelerators are small enough to be deployed on an AMD Spartan 7 S15. They achieve a F1-Score of up to 95% while only requiring 2,844 LUTs and no DSPs or BRAM.

</details>


### [177] [elasticAI.explorer: Towards a Unified End-to-End Framework for Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30019)
*Natalie Maman,Florian Hettstedt,Andreas Erbslöh,Gregor Schiele*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: elasticAI.explorer 是一个基于 Optuna 的可扩展硬件感知 NAS 框架，通过 YAML 配置动态生成模型并集成端到端的自动化部署工具链，显著降低适配异构硬件的开发难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NAS框架存在搜索空间定义、模型实现与部署流程耦合过紧的问题，导致难以扩展到新硬件平台或自定义算子，增加了嵌入式AI部署的工程负担。

Method: 提出了一个基于Optuna构建的Python框架，采用YAML格式定义可动态转换的搜索空间（支持逐层、基于单元和分层搜索）；集成了代码生成、Docker跨编译工具链及硬件在环（HIL）自动化基准测试流程；提供通用的指标评估器（FLOPs、参数量、延迟估计）。

Result: 该框架成功实现了神经架构设计与硬件部署的标准化与自动化，支持多种硬件后端的灵活适配，有效降低了从搜索到部署的开发开销。

Conclusion: elasticAI.explorer 通过降低硬件感知神经架构搜索（NAS）的工程复杂度，不仅实现了模型结构与特定硬件的解耦，还为异构加速器平台上的嵌入式AI部署研究提供了高效、可扩展的统一框架。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has become an important approach for automatically designing neural networks under task-specific and hardware-specific constraints. However, many existing NAS frameworks tightly couple search space definitions, model implementations, and deployment pipelines, making extension to new hardware platforms and custom operators difficult. In this paper, we present the elasticAI.explorer, an extensible Python framework for hardware-aware NAS built on top of Optuna. The framework introduces a YAML-based search space specification that dynamically translates into executable neural network models during sampling. The approach supports layer-wise, cell-based, and hierarchical search spaces while maintaining a unified interface for optimization and deployment. Beyond architecture generation, the framework integrates hardware-specific code generation, Docker-based cross-compilation toolchains, and automated creation of on-device benchmarking binaries, enabling hardware-in-the-loop NAS workflows. The system further provides extensible evaluators for FLOPs, parameter count, and latency estimation. The elasticAI.explorer aims to reduce the engineering overhead of embedded AI deployment and accelerate research on hardware-aware NAS for heterogeneous accelerator platforms

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Dynamics of Stochastic Momentum with Sparse Updates in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28961)
*Katie Everett,Elliot Paquette*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文剖析了动量优化在稀疏更新下的动力学机制，发现其性能受限于动量保留与模型学习两个时间尺度的匹配程度，并指出了在大规模稀疏数据下全局动量参数失效的理论原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动量算法理论假设梯度以恒定速率到达，但在处理长尾数据分布和现代深度学习架构时，实际梯度更新具有极强的稀疏性，导致传统动量理论失效。

Method: 采用了理论建模与数学推导方法，建立了两个易处理的模型：带有稀疏输入的最小二乘模型和带有稀少类别的逻辑回归模型；分析了其二阶矩动力学，并通过大维度极限分析了稀疏度、批次大小和动量衰减三个标度指数的影响。

Result: 证明了该系统的相结构由“动量保留时间尺度”与“学习时间尺度”之比决定；当学习速度快于保留速度时系统不稳定，速度相等时恢复经典重球（Heavy-ball）动力学，且不同稀疏度的token导致全局动量参数面临难以调和的“谱冲突”。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了动量优化器在稀疏更新场景下存在内在的“时间尺度失配”问题。当学习速度与动量保留时间尺度不匹配时，动量会导致训练不稳定或退化至SGD表现，且不同频率的特征（token）对动量存在“谱冲突”。

Abstract: Existing theory of momentum assumes that gradients arrive at every parameter at a roughly constant rate, an assumption violated in practice by heavy-tailed data distributions and modern architectures. We theoretically analyze the dynamics of two tractable models of momentum under sparse updates: a least squares model with sparse inputs and a logistic regression model with a rare class. Both admit exact closed-form second-moment dynamics whose high-dimensional limits we characterize across three scaling exponents for sparsity, batch size, and momentum decay. The phase structure on both problems is governed by the ratio of two intrinsic timescales: a momentum retention timescale (how many active updates the buffer survives) and a learning timescale (how many active updates it takes to reduce the squared error). When learning is much slower than retention, the limit matches SGD; when learning is faster, the system is unstable; where the timescales coincide, we recover classical heavy-ball dynamics. The oscillatory dynamics occur at different momentum values for different token sparsity, creating a spectral conflict for global momentum across token frequencies.

</details>


### [179] [Anytime-Valid Federated Conformal RAG for LLM Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29139)
*Prasanjit Dubey,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Anytime-FC-RAG 将联邦一致性预测检索（FC-RAG）扩展为随时有效的框架，通过新的数学构建保证了在持续自适应控制下的覆盖率，并利用 e-process 预警机制在保证安全的前提下大幅降低了联邦学习系统的通信带宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Federated Conformal RAG (FC-RAG) 仅能在固定时间跨度下保证覆盖，且在面临需要实时、连续、自适应调整（如带宽控制、模型重置）的处理需求时失效。需要解决如何实现“随时有效（Anytime-valid）”的覆盖保证，同时降低在带宽受限环境下的通信开销。

Method: 提出了 Anytime-FC-RAG 框架，通过引入可求和的每步校准偏差预算，将边际界转换为条件界；并设计了一种截断的投注 e-process（betting e-process）作为非负超鞅，实现了对任意停止时的有效性保护。此外，结合 Federated Probe-Logit Distillation (FPLD) 实现了长序列下的误差传播控制。

Result: 在 MMLU、DBpedia 和 AG News 数据集上的实验验证了 Anytime-FC-RAG 在不同动态控制下的有效性；在保持报警准确率的情况下，实现了 14% 到 57% 的通信带宽节省。

Conclusion: Anytime-FC-RAG 实现了时间均匀的随时有效覆盖保证，并支持多种预测性自适应控制策略（如重新校准、带宽升级等）。在保证覆盖率的同时，通过 e-process 预警机制显著降低了通信带宽需求，在多个基准数据集上实现了 14%-57% 的带宽节省。

Abstract: Federated Conformal RAG (FC-RAG) provides distribution-free coverage for a bandwidth-limited swarm of weak language models, but only at a fixed horizon. We extend it to anytime-valid sequential coverage: validity at every stopping time, preserved under predictable adaptive control (recalibration, per-node bandwidth escalation, distilled-student refresh), at no extra cost in assumptions over fixed-horizon FC-RAG. Naive composition fails because FC-RAG's marginal coverage bound makes the betting e-process a non-supermartingale on adverse calibration draws, and Ville's inequality cannot be invoked. We give Anytime-FC-RAG, a sequential extension built on a summable per-step calibration-deviation budget that converts the marginal bound into a strict conditional bound on a calibration-good event, paired with a truncated betting e-process that is a nonnegative supermartingale on the entire probability space. From these two ingredients, we obtain four guarantees: time-uniform alarm validity $\mathbb{P}(\sup_t E_t \ge 1/δ_e) \le δ_e + δ_{\mathrm{cal}}$, a Hoeffding-stitched cumulative-miscoverage envelope at the same total budget, safety under any predictable controller (recalibration, bandwidth escalation, student refresh), and training-side error propagation across an unbounded sequence of Federated Probe-Logit Distillation (FPLD) refreshes via a summable training budget. As a practical consequence, an adaptive controller that escalates retrieval bandwidth only when the e-process crosses a warning threshold matches the alarm rate of a fixed-high-bandwidth schedule at substantially lower communication cost. Experiments on a GPT-2-small + MiniLM swarm across MMLU, DBpedia, and AG News verify the predicted alarm rate, detection delay, envelope coverage, and $14$-$57\%$ bandwidth savings; the alarm fires when and only when coverage genuinely breaks.

</details>


### [180] [Matching Rates and Optimal Allocation for Federated Probe-Logit Distillation under Heterogeneous Bandwidth Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29642)
*Prasanjit Dubey,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦语言模型在受限带宽下的通信效率，确立了带宽项的紧致下界，提出了多轮细化通信策略，并推导出了节点带宽异构下的最优分配规则（对数倾斜注水法）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦语言模型（FPLD）研究中，已知上界的带宽项速率的紧致性未被证实，且对于更切合实际的异构节点带宽分配问题缺乏理论指导。本文旨在解决这两个理论缺口。

Method: 1. 理论下界推导：利用抖动（dithered）FPLD构造证明单轮通信带宽速率的下界 $Ω(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$，实现与已知上界的匹配。
2. 算法扩展：引入嵌套/缩放残差量化器实现多轮顺序细化策略。
3. 异构分析：推导了各节点带宽预算 $B_i$ 的闭式最优分配公式（基于log-tilted water-filling规则）。
4. 自适应方案：设计了基于预热阶段权重估计的自适应带宽分配变体。
5. 实验验证：通过n-gram合成模拟验证了KL散度与理论边界的吻合度及分配策略的有效性。

Result: 1. 证明了单轮通信带宽项速率为 $Θ(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$。
2. 证明了 $T$ 轮下的最优带宽项速率为 $O(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2TB/V})$。
3. 提出了最优的异构带宽分配策略，即根据节点权重进行对数倾斜注水（log-tilted water-filling）。
4. 实验表明，提出的最优分配策略在异构场景下显著优于均匀分配和逆加权基准。

Conclusion: 本文闭合了联邦语言模型中带宽项速率紧致性的缺口，并提出了针对异构带宽的最优分配策略（对数倾斜注水算法）。研究表明，通过多轮顺序细化（T-round sequential refinement）可以进一步优化通信速率，且该理论结果与合成实验结果相符。

Abstract: In federated language modeling, $K$ nodes each hold $n$ samples but cannot pool data or exchange full-precision gradients or weights. We study the minimax rate at which a conditional distribution over $V$ tokens can be estimated when each node may upload at most $B$ bits per query in a public probe set. In federated probe-logit distillation (FPLD), each node transmits a scalar-quantized logit vector on the probe set, and an aggregator distills a global parametric student. Prior work (Dubey and Huo, 2026) establishes a high-probability KL rate $O(d/(Kn) + ρ\sqrt{V \log V / m} + K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$ plus optimization slack, with the bandwidth term in its trace-sharpened form. Whether this bandwidth-term rate is tight, and how the upper bound generalizes to heterogeneous per-node bandwidths, are left open.
  We close both gaps. First, the dithered FPLD construction has a matching single-round lower bound $Ω(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$ under non-degeneracy, pinning the bandwidth-axis rate at $Θ(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$. $T$-round sequential refinement with nested/scaled residual quantizers achieves $O(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2TB/V})$; vanilla FPLD's $T$-independent bandwidth term is suboptimal for every $T > 1$. Second, we establish a heterogeneous-bandwidth upper bound for per-node budgets $B_i$, paired with a closed-form optimal allocation $B_i^* = B_{\mathrm{tot}}/K + (V/2) \log_2(w_i / \bar{w}_g)$, a log-tilted water-filling rule that is the per-node analogue of reverse water-filling for distortion-rate optimization. A plug-in adaptive variant estimates the weights from a short warm-up phase and attains $1 + O(\sqrt{\log(K/δ)/(m T_0)})$ relative suboptimality. Synthetic n-gram simulations confirm that empirical KL is bracketed by the upper and lower bounds and that the optimal allocation strictly dominates uniform and inverse-weighted baselines under heterogeneous clipping.

</details>


### [181] [Instance-dependent Stochastic Lipschitz bandit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29748)
*Marius Potfer,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对 Lipschitz Bandit 问题，摒弃了仅依赖渐近缩放维度的传统分析模式，转而采用一种更精细的基于“最优性缺口积分”的分析方法，从而在不同结构函数上获得了更紧、更具自适应性的悔值界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Lipschitz Bandit 悔值界（如基于缩放维度的算法）仅依赖于近最优水平集的渐近增长，无法捕捉函数 f 的细粒度结构属性（如局部增长特征），导致悔值界不够精确。

Method: 提出了一种通过函数f的子最优性缺口（suboptimality gap）在水平集上进行积分的分析方法，并设计了相应的算法；对比了传统的缩放维度（zooming dimension）分析方法。

Result: 在 maximizers 集合维度为 d* > 0 的情况下，获得了新的自适应悔值界 \tilde{\mathcal{O}} \left ( T^{d_z+1 / \max(d_z,d^\star)+2}\right )，优于经典缩放维度界；此外将结果扩展至全信息设置（Lipschitz experts）并放宽了部分正则性假设。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于最优性缺口积分的分析框架，能够更精确地刻画Lipschitz Bandit问题的悔值界，摆脱了对缩放维度（zooming dimension）渐近行为的过度依赖，并在 maximizers 集合具有正维度的情况下实现了理论上的性能提升。

Abstract: We study the Lipschitz bandit problem, where a learner sequentially maximizes an unknown Lipschitz function $f$ over a domain $\mathcal{X} \subset [0,1]^d$ using noisy pointwise evaluations. Existing regret bounds are either worst-case, scaling as $\tildeΘ \left ( T^{d+1/d+2}\right )$, or adaptive via the zooming dimension $d_z$, yielding $\tildeΘ \left ( T^{d_z+1/d_z+2}\right )$. However, such zooming-based guarantees are only partially instance-dependent, as they depend solely on the asymptotic growth of near-optimal level sets and fail to capture finer structural properties of $f$. We provide an analysis and an algorithm that characterizes the regret through integrals of the suboptimality gap of $f$ over its level sets. This yields regret bounds that adapt to the local growth of level sets, rather than only their asymptotic behavior. As a corollary, when the set of maximizers has dimension $d^\star>0$, we obtain improved adaptive rates of order $\tilde{\mathcal{O}} \left ( T^{d_z+1 / \max(d_z,d^\star)+2}\right )$ strictly improving over classical zooming bounds in this regime. Finally, we extend our analysis to the full-information setting (Lipschitz experts) and show how some of the regularity assumptions can be relaxed.

</details>


### [182] [Joint Model and Data Sparsification via the Marginal Likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29908)
*Alexander Timans,Thomas Möllenhoff,Christian A. Naesseth,Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 为了解决稀疏贝叶斯学习在处理异常数据时的脆弱性，本研究提出了一种“特征+样本”联合稀疏化的对称贝叶斯框架，通过同步修剪模型参数与冗余/错误样本，显著提升了模型的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统系数贝叶斯学习假设同方差噪声，在面对数据污染（如异常值）或噪声设定错误时表现脆弱，导致性能下降。

Method: 提出了一种对称的稀疏贝叶斯学习（Sparse Bayesian Learning）框架，通过单一贝叶斯目标函数联合优化特征权重和样本权重（即同时进行模型空间和数据空间的稀疏化），并保持了共轭性以实现闭式解更新。

Result: 实验表明，该联合ARD方法在多种回归任务中均能稳定地构建既精简（稀疏）又鲁棒的预测模型。

Conclusion: 通过联合学习特征和样本相关性，该方法在实现模型稀疏化的同时提升了对异常值和噪声的鲁棒性，具备良好的可解释性和计算效率。

Abstract: Sparse recovery in linear systems underpins applications from signal processing to high-dimensional regression. Sparse Bayesian Learning, grounded in the principle of automatic relevance determination (ARD), offers a practical Bayesian mechanism for feature sparsity via marginal likelihood optimization. Yet, its reliance on a homoscedastic noise model renders it sensitive to data contaminations such as outliers or misspecified noise, harming model fit and predictions. Instead, we propose jointly learning individual feature and sample relevancies, enabling simultaneous model and data sparsification via a single Bayesian objective. This symmetric pruning of model and data offers a natural extension that preserves conjugacy, admits closed-form updates for standard optimization procedures, and aligns with perspectives from robust regression and influence functions. Empirical results across diverse regression tasks affirm that a joint ARD approach consistently yields both sparse and robust prediction models.

</details>


### [183] [Improved Guarantees for Heterogeneous Treatment-Effect Estimation via Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30319)
*Anay Mehrotra,Phuc Tran,Van H. Vu,Manolis Zampetakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将面板数据下异质性处理效应的估计转化为矩阵补全问题，设计了一种高效估计量，并首次通过行级 ℓ₂ 扰动界理论解决了现有矩阵补全方法在个体（行）层面误差界不紧密的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的矩阵补全理论主要关注全局误差或平均处理效应（ATE），难以满足针对单个单元（per-unit）的异质性处理效应（HTE）估计所需的统计精度要求。

Method: 将异质性因果效应估计构建为矩阵补全问题，利用低秩性和正则性假设，提出了一种计算高效的估计量；在分析上，建立了针对低秩近似的尖锐行（row-wise）ℓ₂扰动界。

Result: 该估计量在无需已知倾向评分（propensities）的情况下，实现了行级 ℓ₂ 误差为 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n} + \frac{n}{m^2}})$ 的收敛界。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种新的矩阵补全估计量，首次通过理论分析获得了针对低秩矩阵近似的尖锐行（row-wise）ℓ₂扰动界，为面板数据下异质性因果效应的估计提供了更强的统计保障。

Abstract: A central goal of modern causal inference is estimating heterogeneous treatment effects to answer questions like "how does an intervention affect each unit," rather than only on average. We study this problem with panel-data where we observe $n$ units across $m$ times under unknown, non-uniform treatment assignments. The data in this setting is naturally represented as a matrix of all unit--time treatment effects. Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects can then be expressed as obtaining a good estimation of each row's average in this matrix. This allows us to formulate the problem as matrix completion, which can be solved under natural low-rankness assumptions. However, existing matrix-completion guarantees are not powerful enough to get meaningful bounds for the per-row guarantee required for estimating the heterogeneous treatment effect; roughly speaking, they are only useful for estimating average treatment effect bounds, as also illustrated in a recent line of work. We give a simple, computationally efficient estimator that, without knowledge of the propensities and under standard low-rankness and regularity assumptions, achieves a row-wise $\ell_2$ error of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n} + \frac{n}{m^2}})$. Technically, our analysis establishes the first sharp row-wise $\ell_2$-perturbation bound for low-rank approximation, complementing existing spectral-, Frobenius-, and entrywise perturbation theory.

</details>


### [184] [Diffusion Models Are Statistically Optimal for Learning Low-Dimensional Multi-Modal Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30153)
*Jingda Wu,Changxiao Cai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了评分扩散模型在学习具有低维子空间结构的数据时，其样本复杂度主要取决于内在维度而非环境空间维度，从而克服了维数灾难，并无需平滑性等强假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型理论通常依赖于过强的正则性假设（如全局平滑性或密度有界），难以解释其在处理具有低维结构和多模态分布的高维数据时的出色表现。需要更符合实际的理论框架来理解其统计效率。

Method: 基于假设数据分布于一系列低维子空间的并集上，且每个子空间内的分布为次高斯（subgaussian），推导出扩散模型在 1-Wasserstein 距离下的样本复杂度。分析过程中不依赖传统的平滑性、密度有界或对数凹性假设。

Result: 证明了扩散模型达到 $\varepsilon$ 误差所需的样本复杂度上限为 $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-k \vee 2})$（其中 $k$ 为内在维度）。这一结果仅依赖于数据的内在维度，而非环境空间维度，显著优于现有的理论边界。

Conclusion: 理论上证明了评分扩散模型（Score-based Diffusion Models）能够自动适应数据的内在低维结构，通过利用子空间结构克服了维数灾难，为扩散模型在高维多模态数据上的成功提供了数学解释。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models have demonstrated remarkable empirical success in learning high-dimensional distributions, particularly those exhibiting low-dimensional and multi-modal structures. However, theoretical understanding of their statistical efficiency remains limited. Existing theories typically rely on strong regularity assumptions, such as uniformly bounded densities or globally smooth score functions, which fail to capture such intrinsic structures. In this work, we study the sample complexity of diffusion models for learning distributions supported on a union of low-dimensional subspaces. Assuming that the data distribution within each subspace is subgaussian, we show that diffusion models require at most $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-k \vee 2})$ samples to achieve $\varepsilon$ error in 1-Wasserstein distance, where $k$ is the intrinsic dimension. This near-optimal convergence rate depends only on the intrinsic dimension and significantly improves upon prior theoretical guarantees that suffer from the curse of dimensionality. Notably, our analysis applies to a broad collection of distributions without imposing smoothness, bounded-density, or log-concavity assumptions. Overall, our results show that diffusion models can statistically adapt to intrinsic low-dimensional structure while naturally accommodating multi-modal data, offering a rigorous theoretical justification for their success in complex high-dimensional learning tasks.

</details>


### [185] [Wasserstein Contraction of Coordinate Ascent Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30253)
*Rocco Caprio,Adrien Corenflos,Sam Power*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过传输信息不等式和泛函平滑性，为坐标上升变分推断（CAVI）算法提供了通用的 Wasserstein 距离收缩性分析框架，并验证了其在多种贝叶斯模型中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管坐标上升变分推断（CAVI）在机器学习中应用广泛，但其收敛性尤其是 Wasserstein 距离意义下的收缩特性缺乏系统且通用的理论分析。作者旨在建立一个能够涵盖广泛场景（包括平滑与非平滑空间）的收缩分析框架。
