Method: 通过引入基于算子理论的传输信息不等式（transport-information inequality）作为收缩条件，结合泛函平滑性假设，分析 CAVI 算法的迭代过程。通过将算法应用于贝叶斯高斯混合模型、高维贝叶斯概率回归和带有 Pólya-Gamma 增强的逻辑回归，验证了理论的普适性。

Result: 建立了 CAVI 在 Wasserstein 距离下的收缩性度量标准，证明了在满足特定传输信息不等式和泛函平滑度的情况下，算法呈现局部收缩。该理论成功解释了包括 Jaakkola-Jordan 算法在内多种典型变分推断方法的收敛行为。

Conclusion: 本文证明了坐标上升变分推断（CAVI）算法在 Wasserstein 距离下具有收缩性，并提供了收缩条件的泛化理论框架。该结果不仅适用于平滑流形，也能够覆盖某些非平滑空间，为变分推断的局部收敛性提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We study the contraction in Wasserstein distance of the coordinate ascent variational inference algorithm. This is shown to hold under a transport-information inequality at the fixed points and a functional smoothness condition. The results are general and sharp, allow for local convergence guarantees, hold for general smooth manifolds, and also in some non-smooth spaces. We consider applications to Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Models, and high-dimensional Bayesian Probit Regression, and Logistic Regression with Pólya-Gamma random variables (i.e. Jaakkola-Jordan's algorithm).

</details>


### [186] [Leave a Window Out: Modifying the Jackknife for Predictive Inference in Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30292)
*Hanyang Jiang,Rina Foygel Barber,Ashwin Pananjady,Yao Xie*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对传统共形预测在时间序列分析中的局限性，本文提出了“留窗法”（LWO），在保证覆盖有效性的同时，实现了比 Split Conformal 更窄的预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测的常用假设（如数据可交换性）在时间序列中往往不成立，而现有的 Split Conformal 方法虽然稳健但会导致预测准确度下降（区间过宽）；传统 Jackknife 方法在处理时间序列时可能导致覆盖率严重失效。

Method: 提出了一种名为“留窗法”（Leave-a-Window-Out, LWO）的改进方法，并引入了量化数据偏离环形可交换性程度的新系数，同时证明了在模型拟合过程满足温和稳定性条件下的覆盖有效性。

Result: 实验证明 LWO 方法在时间序列数据上能保持有效的覆盖率，且区间比 Split Conformal 预测更窄，解决了简单 Jackknife 方法在处理有时序依赖数据时失效的缺陷。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的“留窗法”（LWO）在时间序列预测中不仅能有效保证预测区间的覆盖率，而且在区间宽度上优于分割共形预测（Split Conformal Prediction），克服了传统 Jackknife 方法在时间序列下失效的问题。

Abstract: Conformal prediction methods enjoy strong theoretical and empirical predictive inference performance, provided the data is exchangeable, and predictors are trained in a memoryless fashion. However, these assumptions and constraints are impractical in many real-data settings, such as time series (where temporal dependence violates exchangeability, and where memoryless predictors will inevitably have poor predictive accuracy). Recent work shows that the split conformal prediction method is robust to these issues of memory-based predictors and deviations from exchangeability that are common features of time-series data. However, since using sample splitting can lead to lower accuracy, this motivates asking whether other predictive inference methods (that do not rely on data splitting) could also be reliably used in the time series setting.
  In this work, we show that the vanilla leave-one-out jackknife can suffer an arbitrary loss of coverage even in canonical time series models with mild temporal dependence. As a remedy, we propose a careful modification tailored to such settings, which we term the \emph{leave-a-window-out} (LWO) method, and show that it can achieve valid coverage provided that the model-fitting procedure satisfies mild stability properties. Our proofs are based on quantifying the degree to which the data departs from \emph{cyclic exchangeability}, and we introduce new coefficients to measure the extent of this departure. Experiments on time series data demonstrate that our LWO method often enjoys valid coverage when the vanilla jackknife fails to cover, while producing much narrower intervals than split conformal prediction.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [HPC-vQPU: A Service-Export Architecture for Virtual QPUs on Batch-Scheduled HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28845)
*Shusen Liu,Pascal Jahan Elahi,Ugo Varetto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 为了解决量子模拟在 HPC 环境中难以实现交互式、高保真仿真的问题，本文提出了 HPC-vQPU 服务架构，通过解耦控制平面与执行平面，利用原子快照技术安全地将设备特定的物理语义导出至 batch 调度系统中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 HPC 环境的批处理调度机制与量子软件所期望的交互式、后端驱动的接口之间的不兼容性，同时在不开放入站接入路径的前提下，确保虚拟量子处理单元（vQPU）能实时准确地保持目标设备的拓扑结构、原生门指令及校准参数。

Method: 提出了一种服务导出架构，将控制平面（云侧，负责任务生命周期和设备身份）与执行平面（HPC 侧，通过调度器调度 GPU 作业）分离。核心采用“拓扑与校准感知设备快照”机制，在任务领取时进行原子绑定，确保作业执行的密封性（hermetic）和语义一致性。通信仅限出站方向，以维持集群的安全性。

Result: 在 Pawsey 超算中心的 Setonix GPU 上进行了生产环境验证。实验表明，该服务架构开销极小且可控，校准快照能准确反映输出差异，任务绑定机制有效防止了执行过程中的陈旧数据问题，并具备高可靠的异常恢复能力。

Conclusion: HPC-vQPU 成功实现了将受限的批处理 HPC 环境转化为交互式虚拟 QPU 环境，能够安全、高效地执行高保真度的量子模拟任务，证明了在保证互联安全的同时导出设备特定的量子语义是可行的。

Abstract: Device-aware quantum simulation increasingly requires HPC-scale accelerators, yet secure supercomputers expose batch-scheduled execution environments rather than the interactive, backend-oriented interfaces expected by quantum software. The key obstacle is not only remote job submission: an HPC-hosted virtual QPU must preserve topology, native-gate, and calibration semantics across queue delay, scheduler allocation, compute-node isolation, and partial execution-side failures, without opening inbound paths into the cluster.
  We present HPC-vQPU, a service-export architecture for virtual QPUs on batch-scheduled HPC systems. HPC-vQPU separates a cloud-facing control plane, which owns device identity, task lifecycle, snapshot binding, and event projection, from an HPC-resident execution plane, which claims work and realises it through scheduler-backed GPU jobs. Coordination is exclusively outbound and agent initiated. The central abstraction is a topology- and calibration-aware device snapshot bound atomically at claim time and carried into execution as an immutable contract, making each scheduled job hermetic while preserving fresh device semantics.
  We implement HPC-vQPU at the Pawsey Supercomputing Research Centre using Setonix GPUs, Qiskit-Aer/cuQuantum, and IBM Fez calibration data. Production experiments show that service overhead is bounded and additive, while workload scaling remains confined to the simulator; calibration-bearing snapshots produce measurable output shifts; claim-time binding prevents stale execution after pre-claim device mutation; concurrent agents complete 50/50 tasks exactly once; and explicit recovery restores stale running tasks after agent failure. These results show that secure, scheduler-mediated HPC infrastructure can export device-faithful quantum simulation as an interactive virtual-QPU service.

</details>


### [188] [Understanding and Reducing Metadata-Driven Host Overheads in Sampling-Based GNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29346)
*Yidong Gong,Saima Afrin,Yuchen Ma,Guannan Wang,Bin Ren,Pradeep Kumar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ZEROGNN通过将动态GNN负载的控制流逻辑完全离线至GPU驻留执行，消除了CPU主机侧的同步开销，实现了动态图训练的高性能执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习工作负载（如采样GNN）表现出动态、依赖元数据的执行特性，导致CPU成为瓶颈。现有的CUDA Graphs和GPU动态并行技术无法处理这种执行结构在迭代间变化的场景，导致高昂的同步开销。

Method: 引入ZEROGNN系统，该系统将运行时元数据保留在设备端，在固定的启动结构内调度动态执行逻辑，并采用保守而紧凑（conservative yet tight）的内存分配策略，从而使动态变化的负载能够适配CUDA Graph的重放机制。

Result: 在抽样GNN工作负载中，相比现有技术实现了最高5.28倍的端到端加速，GPU执行占比接近100%，内存效率与理想的元数据驱动分配相当，并显著改善了多GPU扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过将GNN训练的元数据决策逻辑从CPU转移到GPU执行，并利用固定启动结构配合保守但紧凑的执行包络来实现CUDA Graph的可重用性，ZEROGNN成功消除了主机端瓶颈，实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Modern deep learning workloads increasingly exhibit dynamic, metadata-driven execution, where runtime-generated information determines memory provisioning and kernel launch decisions. In sampling-based graph neural network (GNN) training, this behavior places the CPU on the critical path, introducing persistent host-device orchestration overhead and frequent GPU-CPU synchronization, which dominate end-to-end runtime when GPU computation is small. Existing approaches, including CUDA Graphs and GPU dynamic parallelism, fail to address this problem because the metadata-driven control loop remains host-mediated, and execution structure varies across iterations. We present ZEROGNN, a system that removes the host from the metadata-driven control loop and enables fully GPU-resident execution under dynamic behavior. ZEROGNN keeps runtime metadata on-device, mediates dynamic execution within a fixed launch structure, and provisions a conservative yet tight execution envelope to restore CUDA Graph replayability. Experiments on sampling-based GNN workloads show that ZEROGNN achieves up to 5.28 x end-to-end speedup, near 100% GPU execution fraction, and memory efficiency comparable to ideal metadata-informed allocation, while enabling strong multi-GPU scaling by eliminating host-side bottlenecks.

</details>


### [189] [Silent Data Corruption Protection through Efficient Task Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29506)
*Mia Reitz,Claudia Fohry*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对超级计算机中异步多任务（AMT）程序的静默数据损坏（SDC）问题，本研究提出了一种基于任务树记录与选择性重计算的复制保护方案，以低开销实现了高效的故障恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超级计算机规模扩大导致静默数据损坏（SDC）风险增加，而在异步多任务（AMT）运行时中，动态任务生成和工作窃取机制使得传统的复制及错误校准难以实现。

Method: 提出一种基于任务树记录与遍历的复制机制：通过记录任务树，在结果不匹配时自顶向下遍历识别受影响的任务，并仅对损坏的任务进行重新计算，同时复用正确的子任务结果。

Result: 在Itoyori集群AMT运行时中的实验表明，识别并重新处理受影响任务的时间开销微乎其微。

Conclusion: 该方案能高效地检测并修正嵌套fork-join程序中的静默数据损坏（SDC），且通过重用正确的子任务结果实现了极低的恢复开销，同时具备扩展至支持future协作任务的潜力。

Abstract: The trend of increasing cluster sizes of supercomputers leads to a growing susceptibility to Silent Data Corruption (SDC) that can invalidate program results. A common strategy for SDC protection is replication, where the computation is repeated, and the correct result is determined as the one that is the same in at least two different computations. Applying replication to Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) runtimes on clusters is challenging due to dynamic task spawning and work stealing, which complicate the identification of replicated tasks.
  To address the challenge, this paper introduces a novel replication scheme that detects and corrects SDCs for nested fork-join programs. Briefly stated, our approach replicates the computation and records the task tree. Upon a mismatch in the final result, it traverses the tree top-down to identify all corrupted tasks that could have impacted the final result. Recovery is then performed by recomputing these tasks, while the results of correct child tasks are reused.
  We demonstrate our implementation within a variant of the Itoyori cluster AMT runtime. Our experimental results suggest that the time to identify and reprocess the affected tasks is negligible. The paper concludes by discussing the adaptability of our scheme to tasks that cooperate through futures.

</details>


### [190] [Design and Implementation of a Serverless MapReduce Framework for Scalable Data Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29573)
*Angelos Dorotheos Chatzopoulos,Babis Andreou,Kakia Panagidi,Stathes Hadjiefthymiades*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Knative和Kafka的事件驱动实时MapReduce框架，通过Serverless架构实现了物流数据的高效处理与弹性伸缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代物流系统中GPS、IoT传感器等产生的实时数据流对高效、快速的数据处理与决策能力提出了迫切需求，现有系统面临处理实时性和扩展性的挑战。

Method: 提出了一种基于Kubernetes和Knative的事件驱动MapReduce框架；利用Apache Kafka作为消息主干；采用五种松耦合服务进行数据处理；使用Redis存储元数据，AWS S3进行持久化存储；结合Function-as-a-Service（FaaS）原则实现按负载需求的可配置扩展。

Result: 实验评估表明，该系统能够随着输入数据量的增加有效地进行扩展，并支持“缩容至零”（scale-to-zero）及按需处理功能。

Conclusion: 该研究成功设计并实现了一种基于事件驱动及FaaS架构的弹性MapReduce处理框架，能够有效满足物流场景下高时效性和按需扩展的数据处理需求，验证了Serverless技术在工业实时数据处理中的可行性与性能优势。

Abstract: Modern logistics systems tend to generate continuous streams of data from sources such as GPS, IoT sensors, and logistics management systems. The aggregation, processing, and analysis of data have become vital for monitoring operations, optimizing efficiency, and responding quickly to decision making tasks. In this paper, an event-driven MapReduce framework for real-time data processing in logistics environments is presented. This system runs on Kubernetes with Knative and utilizes Apache Kafka as the backbone for communication between the components. This platform is composed of five loosely coupled services that receive, process, and aggregate the incoming data in real-time. Redis is used to preserve workflow metadata, while an AWS S3 service provides persistent storage for the framework. The design is inspired by the MapReduce programming model. It integrates Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) principles with distributed processing techniques that allow configurable scaling based on the workload demands and the underlying hardware. Experimental evaluation shows that the system can scale effectively as the input data volume increases while supporting scale-to-zero, on-demand processing.

</details>


### [191] [TC-MIS: Maximal Independent Set on Tensor-cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29604)
*Prajjwal Nijhara,Dip Sankar Banerjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文推出了 TC-MIS，这是首个利用 GPU 张量核心加速最大独立集（MIS）计算的算法，通过将图计算转化为规则的矩阵乘法，显著超越了传统 CUDA 核心算法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 GPU 在通用图算法中已有应用，但主要依赖 CUDA 核心（CC）。现代 GPU 中的张量核心（TC）具有极高的吞吐量，但目前主要用于深度学习，在图算法领域的应用尚处于未开发状态。

Method: 提出 TC-MIS 算法；将 MIS 计算的关键阶段重构为稀疏矩阵-向量乘法 (SpMV)；通过平铺图邻接矩阵并结合 Warp 矩阵乘累加 (WMMA) 指令，将不规则的图遍历操作转化为高度规则的并行矩阵计算。

Result: 在 Ampere、Ada Lovelace、Hopper 和 Blackwell 等架构上进行了测试；相比当前最优方法，TC-MIS 实现了平均 2.84x 至 18.80x 的平均加速，最高可达 44.38x (H200)，且保持了与现有启发式算法相当的解质量。

Conclusion: 通过将图计算任务转化为张量核心（TC）友好的矩阵运算，TC-MIS 有效克服了 GPU 处理图算法时内存不规则和工作负载不平衡的问题，展示了 TC 在通用图算法加速方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Maximal Independent Set (MIS) in a graph is a fundamental problem with applications in resource allocation, scheduling, and network optimization. Although graphs are inherently un-structured and challenging for GPU parallelism due to irregular memory access and workload imbalance, specialized GPU algorithms have achieved good performance, processing million-vertex graphs in milliseconds. Modern GPUs are equipped with Tensor Cores (TCs), specialized units for matrix operations with 8-16x higher throughput than CUDA Cores (CCs), which are extensively used for ML, DL, and inference tasks but remain largely unexplored for graph algorithms. In this paper, we present TC-MIS, a TC-accelerated algorithm that reformulates key phases of MIS computation as sparse matrix-vector multiplication (SpMV). TC-MIS tiles the graph adjacency matrix and employs Warp Matrix Multiply-Accumulate (WMMA) operations to transform irregular graph traversal into regular, massively parallel computation. Our evaluation across TC-enabled microarchitectures (Ampere, Ada Lovelace, Hopper, Blackwell) demonstrates that TC-MIS achieves an average speedup of 2.84x on RTX A5000, 4.84x on L40S, 18.80x on H200 GPUs, and 5.20x on RTX 5080 with a maximum speedup of 44.38x on H200 GPU over state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining solution quality comparable to that obtained by established heuristics that produce near-maximum independent sets.

</details>


### [192] [AMDP: Asynchronous Multi-Directional Pipeline Parallelism for Large-Scale Models Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29664)
*Ling Chen,Houming Wu,Wenjie Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 一种旨在解决大规模模型训练中异步流水线参数失配问题的 AMDP 方法，通过约束更新步长与改进流水线结构，在提升训练速度的同时保证了训练收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模模型训练中的异步流水线并行方法常因前向和反向传播之间的参数不匹配（Parameter Mismatch）导致模型收敛性下降。

Method: 提出异步多方向流水线并行（AMDP），核心策略包括：1. 限制第一阶段处理小批次的数量以绑定参数偏差；2. 启动并发管线并根据深度自适应调整数量以消除气泡；3. 采用梯度累积技术将参数更新约束在一个优化步骤内。

Result: 在 GPT 和 BERT 模型上的实验表明，AMDP 在保证收敛质量的前提下，显著提高了模型训练的吞吐量和资源利用率。

Conclusion: AMDP 通过限制参数失配范围并结合多管线并行策略，在维持高资源利用率的同时，实现了与同步训练相当的模型收敛性，证明了其在大规模模型训练中的有效性。

Abstract: Pipeline parallelism is essential for large-scale model training, but existing asynchronous approaches often degrade convergence due to parameter mismatch between forward and backward passes. We propose Asynchronous Multi-Directional Pipeline parallelism (AMDP) to mitigate this issue while sustaining high utilization. AMDP limits the first stage of each pipeline to process at most two minibatches before backpropagation, bounding the number of parameter updates between forward and backward passes. To alleviate the resulting pipeline bubbles, AMDP launches multiple concurrent pipelines and adapts their number according to pipeline depth. In addition, AMDP accumulates gradients across minibatches and applies them in a single update, ensuring that only a bounded number of minibatches experience parameter mismatch, limited to within one optimization step. Experiments on GPT- and BERT-style models demonstrate that AMDP significantly accelerates training while preserving convergence.

</details>


### [193] [CARM Tool: Cache-Aware Roofline Model Automatic Benchmarking and Application Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29740)
*José Morgado,Leonel Sousa,Aleksandar Ilic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个自动化跨架构CARM建模框架，支持Intel、AMD、ARM和RISC-V，通过汇编微基准测试和性能插桩技术，为HPC应用提供高精度的性能瓶颈分析，填补了现有工具对非Intel架构支持不足的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC系统日益复杂，导致应用程序的开发与性能优化愈发困难。尽管缓存感知Roofline模型（CARM）是有效的优化指导工具，但现有的工具生态极度不平衡（仅侧重Intel，而AMD、ARM、RISC-V缺乏相应支持），亟需一种跨架构的自动化CARM建模与分析工具。

Method: 1. 开发了针对主流CPU架构（x86, ARM, RISC-V）的通用分析框架；2. 设计了覆盖标量及多级矢量指令集扩展的汇编微基准测试（assembly microbenchmarks），涵盖计算单元和各级存储层级；3. 集成性能计数器（performance counters）和动态二进制插桩（dynamic binary instrumentation）技术进行应用特征分析。

Result: 实验结果显示，该自动化框架构建出的CARM性能界限（Roofline roofs）在多种测试架构下与理论上限的偏差小于1%。

Conclusion: 本文通过开发跨架构的自动化CARM建模框架，成功填补了在非Intel CPU架构（AMD, ARM, RISC-V）上缺乏高性能Roofline模型分析工具的空白。该方法为各种现代CPU架构提供了高精确度（误差<1%）的性能评估能力，简化了复杂HPC系统下的性能优化流程。

Abstract: In recent years, HPC systems and CPU architectures as their central components, have become increasingly complex, making application development and optimization quite challenging. In this respect, intuitive performance models like the Cache-aware Roofline Model (CARM) offer effective guidance by providing insights into bottlenecks that limit the application's ability to reach the system's maximum performance. To fully exploit the benefits of CARM optimization guidance for application development, automatic tools for cross-architecture model construction and in-depth application characterization are absolutely essential. Given a plethora of existing CPU architectures, the current landscape of CARM-enabled tools covers either vendor-specific (Intel Advisor), not sufficiently developed (ARM) or simply non-existing (AMD, RISC-V) tools. This is a particular gap that this work intends to close by bringing automatic CARM support to all major CPU architectures and ISAs, i.e., x86 (Intel, AMD), ARM, and RISC-V, by developing assembly microbenchmarks specifically tailored to cover a full performance spectrum of modern CPUs (from scalar to all supported vector ISA extensions) for both computational units and all memory hierarchy levels. Additionally, this work integrates application analysis within the CARM framework using performance counters and dynamic binary instrumentation. Experimental results show that the CARM roofs constructed with the proposed automated framework provide less than a 1% deviation across various tested architectural maximums.

</details>


### [194] [Effective MPI: User-defined Datatypes and Cartesian Communicators for Zero-copy All-to-all Communication in Multidimensional Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29970)
*Jesper Larsson Träff*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用MPI派生数据类型实现$d$维环面架构下高效、零拷贝的全对全通信算法，有效提升了大规模并行通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对通用MPI全对全通信在特定架构（如$d$维环面）上效率不足的问题，旨在开发一种高性能、零拷贝的通信算法，以优化大规模并行计算中的数据交换。

Method: 采用$d$维环面拓扑分解，通过MPI Cartesian通信器将进程组划分为跨各个维度的子通信器；利用MPI派生数据类型（Derived Datatypes）实现隐含的数据重排，避免了显式的本地数据移动（零拷贝）；结合双缓冲技术处理数据传输。

Result: 实验结果表明，该算法在多种场景下性能与原生MPI_Alltoall实现具有竞争力，并克服了传统实现中存在的一些潜在缺陷。

Conclusion: 该方法通过维度分解和MPI派生数据类型实现了零拷贝（zero-copy）的全对全（all-to-all）通信，在$d$维环面拓扑上具有极高的灵活性、可调优性，且在实验中表现出与原生MPI实现相当甚至更优的性能。

Abstract: We present and show how to implement a non-trivial all-to-all communication algorithm for arbitrary $d$-dimensional tori effectively in MPI. Given a factorization of the number of processes $p$ into $d$ factors that can be mapped onto a $d$-dimensional torus, we first utilize a Cartesian communicator to split a given $p$-process MPI communicator into, for each MPI process, $d$ smaller communicators spanning each of the dimensions of the torus to which the process belongs, and cache these communicators in order to avoid expensive splitting at each all-to-all operation. The all-to-all operation itself is decomposed into a sequence of $d$ MPI_Alltoall operations on the dimension-wise communicators. The non-trivial data rearrangement before and after each MPI_Alltoall call is implicit only and effected by MPI derived datatypes. This makes the implementation of the algorithm formally \emph{zero-copy}, meaning that no explicit process-local reordering of data blocks ever has to be performed. In order to achieve this, the algorithm employs a double-buffering scheme with modest temporary buffer requirements. By choosing the factorization of $p$ and selecting appropriate implementations for the component MPI_Alltoall operations, the presented implementation gives ample opportunities for algorithm tuning and adaptation to the particular high-performance system. A few, select experimental results show competitive performance with native MPI_Alltoall implementations and illustrate problems that common MPI_Alltoall implementations may have.

</details>


### [195] [RAFI -- A Ray/Work Forwarding Infrastructure for Data Parallel Multi-Node/Multi-GPU Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30294)
*Ingo Wald,Serkan Demirci,Alper Sahistan,Stefan Zellmann,Andrea Paris,Patrick Moran,Milan Jaros,Tatiana von Landesberger,Ugur Gudukbay,Valerio Pascucci*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RaFI是一个基于CUDA和MPI的软件框架，通过提供简单的接口抽象，简化了分布式GPU计算中工作项（如光线）的跨设备动态迁移与负载均衡开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPU加速的数据并行任务（如光线追踪）中，跨GPU的任务迁移往往涉及复杂的CUDA与MPI底层实现，RaFI旨在降低此类分布式异构编程的门槛。

Method: 开发了一个基于CUDA和MPI的软件框架，封装了底层的通信与内存管理，并为内核提供了直接进行跨GPU工作项转发的编程接口。

Result: 展示了该框架在多个示例应用中的有效性，能够显著简化支持跨GPU任务迁移的并行软件开发。

Conclusion: RaFI为跨GPU的工作项迁移提供了一个高效、易用的抽象层，简化了异构计算环境下分布式GPU应用的开发难度。

Abstract: We present RaFI, a CUDA and MPI based software framework that simplifies the task of building GPU-enabled data-parallel software where rays or similar work items need to migrate between different GPUs. RaFI provides a simple interface for CUDA kernels to forward such work items to other GPUs, while under the hood managing all the CUDA and MPI related work required to make this happen. We describe RaFI's motivation and implementation, and show its potential in several example applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Residual-Entropy Accounting for Routed Atom-Budgeted Learned Indexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29061)
*Faruk Alpay,Levent Sarioglu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了受限原子预算学习索引的精确排名搜索，通过条件残差答案熵建立了性能评估的会计定理，并通过对现有索引结构的 benchmark 分析揭示了当前设计的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决学习索引性能评估中缺乏统一理论框架的问题，特别是针对如何在有限的原子预算（atom-budget）下通过认证修复实现精确前驱和排名搜索。

Method: 建立了以条件残差答案熵为核心的性能会计理论（accounting theorem），并开发了基于计数的局部预测器和认证修复机制的架构；具体应用了 shadow-price 分配规则，并在 SOSD 和 Zenodo 数据集上对 RGapM 指标进行了实证。

Result: 证明了给定架构下的查询时间缩放规律；在 SOSD 和 Zenodo 数据集上测得的 RGapM 指标显示，该原型在与 PGM-index、RadixSpline 和二分查找的对比中，暴露了明显的系统开销与瓶颈。

Conclusion: 文章提出了一套评估学习索引性能的理论框架，证明了查询时间与条件残差答案熵直接相关，揭示了原型系统的性能瓶颈而非盲目追求速度。

Abstract: We study exact predecessor and rank search in a routed, atom-budgeted, certified-repair learned-index architecture. An ordered directory routes each query to a contiguous interval, a counted local predictor returns a certified rank window, and exact repair resolves the remaining uncertainty by comparisons. The result is scoped to this architecture and does not claim guarantees for arbitrary learned-index designs such as unconstrained RMI dispatch, hash routing, neural routing, or exact-payload indexes without additional accounting.
  The main parameter is conditional residual answer entropy: the entropy of the exact answer after the leaf, predictor output, certificate, and charged pre-repair information are observed. We prove a two-sided accounting theorem showing that this functional gives the query-time scale under the stated architecture and local predictor-atom budget. Directory space, sorted-array storage, and transcript-indexed repair-program space are treated as separate system costs, so the theorem is not a byte-level space lower bound or a compact implementation recipe.
  We also give a rank-spread specialization in which the radius term log(1 + Delta) is valid only when many residual ranks remain likely after the predictor transcript is known. For counted piecewise-linear segments, we make the profile term non-oracular, derive a shadow-price allocation rule, compute finite-instance RGapM and GapM values on real SOSD and Zenodo samples, and report benchmarks against PGM-index, RadixSpline, and binary search. The benchmarks expose overheads and bottlenecks rather than claiming speed for the shadow prototype.

</details>


### [197] [An Improved Greedy Approximation for (Metric) $k$-Means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29165)
*Moses Charikar,Vincent Cohen-Addad,Ruiquan Gao,Fabrizio Grandoni,Euiwoong Lee,Ernest van Wijland*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种 k-Means 问题的通用度量空间算法，通过修正拉格朗日乘子保留（LMP）算法以处理平方距离成本，将近似比提升至 5.83 以下，打破了长期以来该类方法在 k-Means 问题上的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-Means 问题的优化至关重要。既往研究受限于无法直接将最优的 LMP 算法适配到处理 k-Means 的平方度量成本上（因为平方距离违反三角不等式），本文旨在克服这一理论障碍并提升近似比。

Method: 将 Jain 等人提出的具有改进 LMP 特性的贪婪式 2-近似设施选址算法，适配至具有平方度量代价的 k-Means 问题中，并结合 Cohen-Addad 等人近期提出的 k-Median 问题处理框架进行集成。

Result: 在通用度量空间下实现了 3+2√2+ε < 5.83 的多项式时间近似算法，显著优于之前的 (9+ε)-近似，并略微改进了针对欧几里得特殊情况下的 5.92-近似。

Conclusion: 该论文通过克服拉格朗日乘子保留（LMP）算法在处理平方度量成本时的理论障碍，成功设计出一种针对通用度量空间下 k-Means 问题的改进算法，并设定了新的近似比理论上限。

Abstract: Clustering is a basic task in data analysis and machine learning, and the optimization of clustering objectives are well-studied optimization problems; amongst these, the $k$-Means objective is arguably the most well known. Given a collection of points in a metric space, the goal is to partition them into $k$ clusters, each with an associated center, so as to minimize the sum of squared distances of points to their cluster centers. In this paper, we present a polynomial-time $3+2\sqrt{2}+ε<5.83$-approximation algorithm for $k$-Means in general metrics. This substantially improves on the current-best $(9+ε)$-approximation in [Ahmadian, Norouzi-Fard, Svensson, Ward - FOCS'17, SICOMP'20], and even slightly improves on the $5.92$-approximation in [Cohen-Addad, Esfandiari, Mirrokni, Narayanan - STOC'22] for the Euclidean special case.
  A natural approach for $k$-Means is to leverage Lagrangian Multiplier Preserving (LMP) approximations for the facility location problem. The previous best results for $k$-Means build upon an adaptation of an LMP $3$-approximation for facility location with metric connection costs in [Jain, Vazirani - J.ACM'01] based on a primal-dual method, rather than on the improved LMP greedy $2$-approximation for the same problem in [Jain, Mahdian, Markakis, Saberi, Vazirani - J.ACM'03]. The barrier to using the improved LMP algorithm was that no adaptation of this algorithm and its analysis to the case of squared metric connection costs was known (since squared distances violate triangle inequality). Our main contribution is overcoming this barrier by providing such an adaptation. This new LMP approximation algorithm is then combined with the framework recently introduced in [Cohen-Addad, Grandoni, Lee, Schwiegelshohn, Svensson - STOC'25] for the related (metric) $k$-Median problem.

</details>


### [198] [Sampling Directed Eulerian Tours in $\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$ Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29566)
*Nima Anari*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能在 $\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$ 时间内对任意有向欧拉多重图进行几乎均匀抽样的随机算法，打破了此类问题在稀疏图上的传统复杂度限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对有向欧拉多重图，旨在突破传统采样方法在稀疏图上面临的$mn$复杂度瓶颈，实现更高效的近似均匀采样。

Method: 提出了一种基于“翻转-修复”（flip-repair）的新型局部马尔可夫链，并使用动态弦数据结构（dynamic chord data structure）进行实现；通过逐点度规约（pointwise degree-reduction）方法将2-入/2-出的核心情况扩展至任意度数的图。

Result: 证明了该马尔可夫链在接近线性的步数内混合，并确立了$\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$的总运行时间界限。

Conclusion: 该算法成功在$\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$时间内实现了几乎均匀的欧拉回路采样，突破了稀疏图上$mn$级复杂度的时间限制。

Abstract: We give a randomized algorithm that samples a nearly uniform Eulerian tour of a directed Eulerian multigraph with $m$ arcs in $\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$ time. The guarantee is worst-case, applies to arbitrary directed Eulerian multigraphs, and breaks the $mn$-type arborescence-sampling barrier on sparse graphs.
  The core case is a $2$-in/$2$-out graph. We introduce a new local Markov chain, the flip--repair walk: one step locally splits a tour into two circuits and then chooses uniformly among the local flips that repair the state to one tour. We prove that this walk mixes in nearly linear many steps and implement the walk using a dynamic chord data structure. A pointwise degree-reduction wrapper extends the sampler from this degree-two core to arbitrary degrees while preserving the $\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$ total running time.
  The high-level algorithmic plan, the switching-network reduction, and the dynamic data-structure argument were devised by the author. The author conjectured the mixing theorem underlying the analysis, and GPT 5.5 Pro Extended produced its linear-algebra proof. Codex assisted with manuscript assembly and typesetting.

</details>


### [199] [On Language Generation in the Limit with Bounded Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30324)
*Jon Kleinberg,Anay Mehrotra,Amin Saberi,Grigoris Velegkas*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有限内存约束下的语言生成与识别任务。研究表明，内存限制对生成任务（仍可处理可数集合）和识别任务（仅限有限集合）的影响具有显著不对称性，并量化了不同内存策略（无记忆、滑动窗口、自适应存储）对生成密度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言生成研究多假设学习者可访问全部历史数据，这在实际应用中是不现实的。本文旨在探究在有界内存约束下，语言生成与识别任务的“极限学习”（learning in the limit）能力及可行性边界。

Method: 结合学习理论与组合数学方法。利用Sperner定理和对称链分解来分析极小极大密度；对比无记忆、滑动窗口及可自适应存储的生成器性能；研究增量式识别的局限性并提出近似识别的放松条件。

Result: 1. 证明了在枚举限制下，无记忆生成器可处理任何可数无限语言集合；否则给出了无记忆条件的精确表征。2. 推导了无记忆生成器的最优极小极大密度界。3. 发现滑动窗口无法改善最坏情况密度，而自适应存储可有效改进密度。4. 展示了增量式识别（仅记前一假设）在有限语言集合下存在本质局限，但通过“近似”放松条件可实现识别。

Conclusion: 有限内存对语言生成和有限识别的影响显著不同。生成在可数集合下仍可行，而密度和识别任务仅限于有限集合，且随着集合规模扩大，其性能保证会减弱。此外，自适应存储比固定的滑动窗口更能优化生成密度。

Abstract: We study language generation in the limit under bounded memory. In this task, a learner observes examples from an unknown target language one at a time and must eventually output only new valid examples. Prior work assumes access to the entire history, a strong assumption since realistic algorithms retain limited past information. Classical work in learning theory shows memory constraints dramatically alter learnability; we extend this to language generation.
  First, we study memoryless generators. Under a mild enumeration restriction, every countable collection of infinite languages remains generable without memory. Without this restriction, we exactly characterize when memoryless generation is possible. For finite collections, we characterize the optimal minimax density achievable by memoryless generators -- the best density guaranteed against any collection of a given size. This combinatorial bound relies on Sperner's theorem and symmetric chain decompositions.
  We further show that a sliding window of the last $W$ examples does not improve this worst-case density, whereas allowing it to store $b$ adaptively chosen past examples improves the achievable density for every $b \geq 1$.
  Finally, we revisit identification in the limit, where the learner must converge to a single correct hypothesis for the target language. We focus on its incremental variant, where the learner remembers only its previous guess. Here, although exact identification fails on a collection of just three languages, a mild relaxation requiring convergence to an ``approximate'' version of the target is achievable for every finite collection.
  These results show bounded memory affects these tasks differently: generation remains achievable for every countable collection, while density and identification are confined to finite collections, with guarantees weakening as the collection grows.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [IORM: Hierarchical I/O Governance for Thousands of Consolidated Databases on Oracle Exadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29006)
*Rajarshi Chowdhury,Akshay Shah,Zakaria Alrmaih,Chenhao Guo,Anubhav Singh,Sue Lee*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向 Oracle Exadata 多租户数据库的高效存储侧调度器 IORM，通过注入数据库语义并实施全栈分层资源治理，解决了大规模数据整合中的资源竞争与噪声干扰问题，显著提升了系统性能稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Oracle Exadata 在实现大规模数据库多租户整合时，现有的商用块层调度器缺乏数据库语义及租户边界的感知能力，无法处理多层级资源竞争（容器数据库、租户数据库及租户内工作负载），导致难以避免“吵闹邻居”带来的性能干扰问题。

Method: 提出了存储端调度器 IORM，包含三个核心机制：1. I/O 标签（I/O Tagging）：将数据库内核的语义上下文传输至存储调度器；2. 分层资源配置文件（Hierarchical Resource Profiles）：利用份额（shares）和限制（limits）定义多层级资源分配策略；3. 统一存储治理（Unified Storage Governance）：在持久内存、闪存和硬盘等所有介质层级上实施一致的策略，并结合缓存放置决策。

Result: 在生产环境的测试中，IORM 显著提升了延迟的一致性，几乎消除了尾部延迟异常，并在混合负载下将平均读取延迟提高了多倍。分层限制策略能够跨三个治理层级准确执行，比例份额分配在负载极度倾斜时依然保持精确。

Conclusion: IORM 成功解决了大规模数据库整合中的“吵闹邻居”问题，通过语义感知和分层资源治理，实现了在共享存储基础设施上的高性能、高隔离度与可预测性。

Abstract: Oracle Exadata consolidates thousands of tenant databases onto shared storage infrastructure deployed at hundreds of customer sites worldwide. Oracle Multitenant architecture enables this extreme density, with thousands of tenant databases sharing a single Exadata storage system -- but this creates a multi-level resource hierarchy (container databases, tenant databases, and workloads within tenants) that commodity block-layer schedulers cannot govern, as they lack visibility into database semantics and tenant boundaries. This paper presents the I/O Resource Manager (IORM), a storage-side scheduler built on three mechanisms: I/O Tagging, which propagates semantic context from the database kernel to the storage scheduler; Hierarchical Resource Profiles, which express compositional allocation policies across consolidation tiers using shares and limits; and Unified Storage Governance, which applies these policies consistently across all tiers of the storage hierarchy -- persistent memory, flash, and hard disk -- including cache placement decisions. IORM enables successful cloud deployments where thousands of tenants coexist on shared storage: production OLTP workloads run alongside concurrent analytical workloads from the same or different databases without noisy-neighbor interference. Evaluation on production Exadata systems demonstrates that IORM dramatically improves latency consistency, virtually eliminating tail latency outliers and delivering several-fold improvements in average read latency under mixed workloads. Hierarchical limits compose correctly across all three levels, and proportional share allocation tracks configured ratios closely even under highly skewed demand.

</details>


### [201] [ScanTwin: Simulating Performance Regressions Without Access to Tenant Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29093)
*Donghyun Sohn,Jennie Rogers*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: ScanTwin 是一个轻量级框架，通过共享带差分隐私保护的 Parquet 行组元数据（而非原始数据），帮助开发者在本地精确复现云数据平台中的性能回归问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云数据平台中，开发者因隐私限制无法接触真实租户数据，导致难以重现特定数据集下的性能回归问题；现有合成数据方法多关注统计属性，忽略了影响查询引擎扫描行为（如行组剪枝）的关键物理属性。

Method: 提出 ScanTwin 框架：从 Parquet 文件尾部提取行组级别的“草图”（边界值与压缩大小），并通过边界参数化（boundary parameterization）施加 $\varepsilon$-差分隐私保护，以生成满足隐私合规的模拟数据。

Result: 在 TPC-H 和 SSB 数据集上，$\varepsilon=\infty$ 时行组剪枝误差为 0%，字节大小误差小于 1%；在 $\varepsilon=5$ 的严格隐私约束下，高选择性查询的剪枝误差低于 8.5%，且在 DuckDB 上的扫描耗时与原始数据高度吻合。

Conclusion: ScanTwin 是一种有效的合成数据框架，能在保护隐私的前提下，通过提取 Parquet 元数据（行组边界和压缩大小）来精确模拟物理执行特性（如行组剪枝），从而协助开发者在不接触原始数据的情况下重现性能回归问题。

Abstract: In cloud data platforms, developers often encounter performance regressions that occur in specific tenant datasets. However, due to confidentiality constraints, they cannot access the original data, which makes it difficult to reproduce these regressions locally. Current methods for synthetic data usually focus on statistical properties, such as matching data distributions or improving query accuracy. However, they overlook the physical properties that control how the engine behaves during scans, including row-group pruning.
  We propose ScanTwin, a lightweight framework that extracts a per-row-group sketch from the Parquet footer, including boundary values and compressed sizes, and releases them under $\varepsilon$-differential privacy using a boundary parameterization. On TPC-H and SSB (6M rows), ScanTwin achieves 0% pruning error and less than 1% byte error at $\varepsilon{=}\infty$. Under $\varepsilon{=}5$, high-selectivity queries ($>$30%) incur below 8.5% pruning error on both datasets, and per-query scan timing on DuckDB closely tracks the original.

</details>


### [202] [One Ring to Shuffle Them All: Scalable Intra-Process Data Redistribution with Ring-Buffer Shuffle in Redpanda Oxla](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29099)
*Adam Szymański,Tyler Akidau*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对高核心数 CPU 下查询引擎数据重分布性能低下问题，提出了一种基于环形缓冲区（Ring-buffer）的无锁流式 shuffle 设计，显著提升了多核架构下的并行查询性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着服务器 CPU 核心数增加至数百个，现有的批处理分区（产生全局屏障和缓存局部性差问题）及通道流式传输（产生严重的同步开销问题）无法在扩展性上满足高核心数硬件的需求。

Method: 设计了一种基于环形缓冲区（Ring-buffer）的流式 shuffle 机制，利用无锁原子槽位获取（lock-free atomic slot acquisition）将数据分发到固定大小的批次组中，实现了摊销 O(1) 的同步成本和与输入大小无关的 O(M) 内存占用。

Result: 在 72 核至 192 核的多平台测试中，Ring-buffer 方案性能显著优于传统方法。在 192 核系统上，其比通道流式传输快 100% 以上，比批处理分区快 300% 以上；在 TPC-H 和 ClickBench 工作负载测试中验证了其实际应用表现。

Conclusion: Ring-buffer streaming shuffle 是一种有效提升高核心数 CPU 环境下查询引擎性能的方案，但在具有分散 L3 缓存的小芯片（chiplet）架构中，中心化原子计数器可能成为瓶颈，此时通道流式传输仍具有竞争力。

Abstract: As server CPUs scale to dozens and now hundreds of cores per socket, parallel query engines must rethink how they redistribute data between threads. Partitioned operators such as hash joins and aggregations require frequent data redistribution across threads, yet existing intra-process shuffle designs fundamentally fail to scale with core count: batch partitioning avoids cross-thread synchronization in the hot path but materializes all intermediate data, introduces a global producer/consumer barrier, and requires a consumption approach with low cache locality, while channel-based streaming avoids materialization but incurs per-channel synchronization that scales poorly with core count. As core counts rise, these architectural tradeoffs increasingly prevent engines from fully utilizing modern hardware.
  We present a ring-buffer streaming shuffle design that addresses these shortcomings through lock-free atomic slot acquisition into fixed-size batch groups, achieving amortized O(1) synchronization cost per batch and O(M) memory independent of input size. Ring-buffer shuffle has been implemented in Redpanda's Oxla query engine for two years, where it currently powers production queries for Redpanda SQL users.
  We evaluate all three approaches on a 72-core NVIDIA GraceHopper, a 192-core dual-socket AWS Graviton4, and a 96-core (192-thread) AMD EPYC. On a 72-core single-socket system the ring buffer outperforms channel streaming by up to 44% and batch partitioning by up to 79%; at 192 cores the advantage over channel grows to over 100% and over 300% versus batch partitioning. Even so, on chiplet architectures with many partitioned L3 caches, the shared atomic counter becomes a cross-die bottleneck and channel-based streaming remains competitive. End-to-end Graviton4 evaluation on TPC-H (21 queries) and ClickBench (43 queries) shows the advantage is workload-shape-dependent.

</details>


### [203] [Towards Reliable Agentic Progressive Text-to-Visualization with Verification Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29692)
*Xu Wenxin,Chen Jason Zhang,Xiaoyong Wei,Haoyang Li,Hwanhee Kim,Yuanfeng Song,Raymond Chi-Wing Wong*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对Text-to-Vis任务的一轮生成范式缺陷，本文提出了支持多轮渐进式交互的PMVis框架与相应数据集PMVisBench，并通过具备自纠错能力的智能体PMVisAgent实现了更准确、用户友好的可视化结果生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Text-to-Vis方法多采用“一轮生成（one-shot）”模式，要求用户一次性详尽描述需求，导致用户认知负担重且模型理解偏差大，难以处理复杂的可视化查询。

Method: 1. 提出了PMVis：一种支持多轮交互的渐进式Text-to-Vis范式；2. 构建了PMVisBench：首个针对多轮交互的可视化查询基准数据集；3. 开发了PMVisAgent：一套基于ReAct风格的智能体框架，利用验证智能体进行多轮循环交互与错误纠偏。

Result: PMVisAgent在PMVisBench上表现优异，在单表和多表设置下执行准确率分别提升了17.57%和23.21%。消融实验证实了渐进式交互结合确认机制对提升性能的关键作用。

Conclusion: PMVis框架及PMVisAgent通过引入多轮交互和验证机制，有效解决了Text-to-Vis任务中“一锤子”式解析导致的认知负荷高与准确率低的问题。验证了渐进式交互与澄清机制对提高复杂数据可视化生成任务可靠性的重要性。

Abstract: Text-to-Visualization (Text-to-Vis) translates natural language queries into visualization query languages, enabling non-expert users to perform data analysis. However, most existing methods follow a one-shot paradigm that requires users to specify all visualization details in a single round, often leading to cognitive overload and incorrect visualizations. In this paper, we propose PMVis, a progressive multi-turn paradigm for text-to-vis, where users' intents are refined through multi-turn interactions. To support research in this paradigm, we construct PMVisBench, the first dataset designed to capture the progressive and iterative nature of real-world user queries. It is built through VQL simplification and NLQ reconstruction, with explicit rule constraints to ensure each intermediate VQL remains valid and meaningful. Building upon PMVis, we further introduce PMVisAgent, an agent-based framework that simulates realistic user-system dialogues. PMVisAgent consists of a User, a System, and a Validation Agent that performs verification and repair via a ReAct-style tool-use loop to mitigate error accumulation across rounds, with explicit interaction and verification rules to ensure reliability of the multi-agent system. Extensive experiments on PMVisBench demonstrate that PMVisAgent significantly outperforms state-of-the-art text-to-vis baselines. It achieves up to 17.57\% and 23.21\% improvements in execution accuracy in single-table and multi-table settings, respectively, while ablation studies confirm the importance of combining progressive interaction with clarification. The code is available at https://github.com/wxxv/PMVis.

</details>


### [204] [Zero-Scan Data Quality: Leveraging Table Format Metadata for Continuous Observability at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30308)
*Mohit Verma,Shantanu Rawat,Christian Bush,Sumedh Sakdeo,Lokesh Amarnath Ravindranathan,Dwarak Bakshi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出复用 Apache Iceberg 等现代表格式的元数据（及扩展的草图信息）进行数据质量监听，无需扫描原始数据即可降低 50% 的监控成本并覆盖约 90% 的 DQ 规则，建议将草图存储标准化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据质量监控通常需要全量扫描数据，计算开销巨大；而现代表格式中已有的元数据统计信息与数据质量监控需求高度重合，可被复用于实现低成本的持续观测。

Method: 提出一种“元数据优先”的方法，利用 Apache Iceberg 写入时生成的统计信息（如行数、null值、边界值）进行数据质量监控，并扩展引入轻量级计数器和可增量合并的草图（如 Theta sketch 用于去重，KLL sketch 用于分位数）来满足更复杂的数据质量规则。

Result: 在 LinkedIn 的 20 万张表（800+ PB）上的实践证明，该方法在零边际计算成本下满足了约 60% 的 DQ 规则，Profiling 资源消耗降低了约 50%；通过引入草图，预期可将覆盖率提升至 90%。

Conclusion: 通过在表格式（如 Apache Iceberg）的元数据层集成轻量级统计信息和草图（sketch），可以以零边际计算成本实现高效、高覆盖率的数据质量监控，并建议将此类草图集成至 Puffin sidecar 文件标准中。

Abstract: Modern table formats such as Apache Iceberg compute and store metadata-commit timestamps, record counts, and column-level statistics such as null counts and value bounds at write time as part of file writing. These statistics serve query planning, yet they overlap substantially with data quality (DQ) monitoring needs. We describe a metadata-first approach that repurposes write-time statistics for continuous DQ observability: anomaly detection, drift monitoring, null-rate tracking; without scanning any data. Deployed at LinkedIn across 200,000+ Iceberg tables (800+ PB), this approach satisfies approximately 60% of user-defined DQ rules at zero marginal compute cost and reduces profiling resource consumption by around 50%. Extending manifest statistics with lightweight counters (sum, zero-value counts, boolean counts) and incrementally mergeable sketches; Theta sketches for distinct counts, KLL sketches for quantiles; can further raise metadata-satisfiable coverage to close to 90% of production DQ rules. We validate sketch accuracy, mergeability, and storage overhead on production data and propose that table formats should store per-file sketches in Puffin sidecar files, following the same store-then-aggregate pattern used for existing manifest statistics.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [From Roofline to Ruggedness: Decomposing and Smoothing the GEMM Performance Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29752)
*Aditya Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 论文提出了“性能崎岖性分析”框架，解释并优化了GPU在处理GEMM任务时因软硬件交互导致的剧烈性能波动，通过动态瓦片选择和填充优化，实现了平均吞吐量的大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GEMM算例在极小的维度变化下会出现显著的吞吐量差异（可达30%），现有的Roofline模型无法解释这种“性能崎岖性”，且该现象在非峰值负载中极其普遍。

Method: 提出了“性能崎岖性分析”（Performance Ruggedness Analysis）框架；利用32,768种配置的GEMM算例进行大规模实验；开发了两阶段优化堆栈：(i) 六种瓦片尺寸的动态选择，(ii) 基于动态规划的填充与拆分优化器。

Result: 将性能波动的崎岖度降低了70%，平均算力提升了30%。通过实验证实性能锯齿周期与软件瓦片大小强相关，并将剩余波动归因于四种硬件相关机制（内核开销、波形量化、DPAS几何结构、内存通道映射）。

Conclusion: GPU的“性能崎岖性”主要由软件瓦片大小（tile size）与硬件结构（如量化、存储交互）的交互引起。通过动态瓦片选择和填充优化技术，可以显著平滑性能波动并提升整体吞吐量。该研究为理解非峰值性能提供了优于Roofline模型的分析框架。

Abstract: Adjacent GEMM problems that differ by a single 128-element step in N can show 30% different throughput on the same GPU. This pervasive performance ruggedness - invisible to roofline analysis and peak-FLOPs intuition, yet dominant for every non-peak workload - is the subject of this paper.
  We propose performance ruggedness analysis as an analytical framework complementary to roofline: rather than summarizing GPU performance with a scalar bound, treat the full multidimensional performance surface as the object of study, decompose its texture into mechanism-attributable components and separate software-removable contributions from hardware-bound ones. The framing is directly analogous to deep-learning loss landscapes - a continuous quantity (the idealized time 2MNK / compute_throughput_peak) made rugged by interaction with discrete hardware substrates (tiles, sub-groups, cache lines, DRAM channels).
  We apply the framework to BF16 NN (no transpose) GEMM on Intel Battlemage (Arc B580, sycl-tla) via a 32,768-configuration sweep (M, N, K) belongs to {128, ..., 4096}^3. The peak is 110.8 TFLOPs at the non-square shape M=3840, N=2048, K=4096 with the default tile size; the initial landscape roughness is 16.8 TFLOPs per 128-step against an ideal of 2.0. A two-stage software stack - (i) best-of-six dynamic tile selection and (ii) a novel dynamic-programming based padding-and-splitting optimizer with O(1) runtime lookup - reduces roughness by 70% and raises mean throughput by 30%. Cross-tile experiments establish that the residual sawtooth period scales exactly with software tile size, ruling out cache set conflicts and attributing the remaining variance to four hardware-bound sources (per-kernel base overhead, wave quantization, DPAS atom geometry and GDDR6 channel-hash interactions).

</details>


### [206] [Demystifying VEINS: A Reality Check Against Living Lab Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29988)
*Antonio Solida,Giovanni Gambigliani Zoccoli,Gaetano Orazio Cauchi,Filip Valgimigli,Salvatore Iandolo,Martin Klapez,Maurizio Casoni,Mirco Marchetti,Carlo Augusto Grazia*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本研究通过实证对比分析，指出VEINS模拟器在默认配置下会高估RSSI并低估消息接收率，通过量化这些差异为改进VANET仿真精确度提供了依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车联网（V2X）与合作智能交通系统（C-ITS）的安全应用高度依赖仿真平台（如VEINS）的有效性，但现有仿真模型在现实场景中的准确性尚存疑，需通过实证分析进行验证。

Method: 通过对比实验法，将VEINS模拟器在默认配置下的仿真数据（RSSI、消息接收数量、信号衰减）与MASA现实实验室收集的真实环境数据进行一致性分析。

Result: VEINS模拟器系统性地高估了RSSI值，且消息接收数量比真实数据少了约18%，揭示了现有仿真模型与现实环境之间存在实质性差距。

Conclusion: 当前VEINS模拟器在默认配置下与现实存在显著偏差，研究量化了这种差异，为后续修正仿真参数、提升VANET交通安全应用仿真保真度提供了基础。

Abstract: Safety applications in vehicle-to-everything communications and Cooperative Intelligent Transport Systems rely on reliable and timely message exchange, which in turn depends on accurate modeling of wireless signal propagation. Simulation frameworks such as VEINS are widely adopted to design and evaluate such systems before deployment; however, their realism strongly depends on the validity of the underlying channel and antenna models. This work presents an empirical validation of the VEINS simulator against real-world data collected from the MASA living laboratory. Using the default configuration, we compare Received Signal Strength Indicator (RSSI), number of messages, and attenuation of the signal. The results show that VEINS systematically overestimates the RSSI value, while losing approximately 18% of the total number of messages received compared to the MASA, revealing inconsistencies between simulation and reality. The contribution of this study is a direct comparison between simulated and real world data, establishing a quantitative basis for future calibration of VEINS parameters to improve the fidelity of VANET simulations in C-ITS safety research.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [RTP-LLM: High-Performance Alibaba LLM Inference Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29639)
*Boyu Tan,Jiarui Guo,Zongwei Lv,Hanbo Sun,Tong Yang,Kan Liu,Xinfei Shi,Zetao Hu,Yaxin Yu,Chi Zhang,Jianning Zhang,Xi Yang,Wei Zhang,Bo Cai,Silu Zhou,Xiyu Wang,Na He,Yinghao Yu,Wending Bao,Guiyang Huang,Yuxing Yuan,Juncheng Yin,Nan Wang,Lin Yang,Zechao Zhang,Lu Chen,Guoding Li,Tao Lan,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: RTP-LLM 是阿里巴巴推出的一款高性能 LLM 推理引擎，通过计算/内存解耦架构、多级缓存管理及多种加速技术，显著提升了工业级场景下的部署效率、响应速度和吞吐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 推理引擎在应对工业级大规模部署（如阿里巴巴超 1 亿用户需求）时面临加载时间长、吞吐量瓶颈、KV Cache 利用率低等多重挑战。

Method: 1. 模型加载优化：采用文件顺序驱动的 I/O 和计算通信重叠技术；2. 架构创新：引入 Prefill-Decode Disaggregation 架构，解耦计算密集型预填充与内存密集型解码阶段；3. 内存管理：实现层级化 KV Cache 管理；4. 进阶功能：集成模块化推测解码、自适应 KV Cache 量化、解耦的多模态处理及多级并行支持。

Result: 性能大幅超越 vLLM 和 SGLang：模型加载加速 4.7-6.3 倍；生产环境中 TTFT 延迟降低 35-37%，缓存利用率提升 215%；推测解码和多模态推理吞吐量分别提升 1.12-2.48 倍和 1.86-2.52 倍；量化推理下批处理延迟降低 35-40%，TTFT 提升 1.9-3.0 倍。

Conclusion: RTP-LLM 作为一种经过工业界大规模验证的推理引擎，通过高性能的系统设计（如 Disaggregation 架构和多层次缓存优化），有效提升了 LLM 在生产环境下的吞吐量、响应延迟及资源利用率，是工业级大规模部署的综合解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized AI applications, but deploying them at scale presents significant challenges. We present RTP-LLM, a high-performance inference engine for industrial-scale LLM deployment, successfully deployed across Alibaba Group serving over 100 million users. RTP-LLM addresses fundamental bottlenecks through integrated design. It optimizes model loading via file-order-driven I/O and parallel I/O-communication overlapping. The Prefill-Decode Disaggregation architecture decouples compute-intensive prefill from memory-bound decode phases, combined with hierarchical multi-tiered KV cache management enabling efficient cache reuse. In addition, RTP-LLM incorporates modular speculative decoding supporting multiple algorithms, adaptive KV cache quantization, and decoupled multimodal processing, with support for multi-level parallelism.
  Comprehensive evaluations across diverse model architectures (8B-235B parameters) have been conducted, where both controlled benchmarks and real production workloads are used. The results demonstrate RTP-LLM's superior performance against vLLM and SGLang: 4.7x-6.3x model loading speedup, 35-37% TTFT P95 latency reduction with 215% cache reuse improvement in production traffic scheduling, 1.12x-2.48x and 1.86x-2.52x throughput improvements in speculative decoding and multimodal inference, respectively, and 35-40% batch latency reduction with 1.9x-3.0x TTFT improvement in quantized inference. RTP-LLM's production-proven architecture and open-source availability make it a comprehensive solution for industrial LLM deployment.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Rethinking Literature Search Evaluation: Deep Research Helps, and Human Citation Lists Are Not a Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29234)
*Gaurav Sahu,Laurent Charlin,Christopher Pal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高性能的深度检索流水线，并揭示了现有学术引用中人类偏见（如倾向引用合作者）导致测评基准质量受限的问题，主张通过多维度指标体系对文献检索进行综合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文献检索系统在召回率方面表现不足，且作为评估基准的人类引用列表存在潜在的偏见（如引用合作者）和质量问题，缺乏系统性的测评标准。

Method: 1. 构建深度搜索流水线：处理完整查询论文，并基于参考文献进行广度优先搜索扩展；2. 机制评估：利用中立的LLM作为评审员评估人类参考列表的质量；3. 数据分析：基于OpenAlex合著图谱分析人类引用与AI推荐之间的偏好差异。

Result: 1. 深度检索流水线将RollingEval基准的召回率从20%提升至80%以上；2. 人类引用的相关性仅51%，而AI重排序器可达86-88%；3. 人类引用其直接合作者的概率是AI的2.5倍。

Conclusion: 单一维度的文献检索评价（如仅关注召回率）是不够的。应当采用包含召回率、主题相关性、排序列表多样性及合作关系距离等多维度指标进行综合评价，以全面衡量引文质量。

Abstract: We study large-scale literature search from two complementary angles: improving the retrieval pipeline, and stress-testing the human reference list as an evaluation target. First, we implement a Deep Research pipeline that processes the full query paper and expands the retrieved results breadth-first along their bibliographies, and show that it substantially outperforms vanilla API-only search, raising recall on RollingEval-Jun25 (a 250-paper literature-search benchmark) from below 20% to above 80%. Second, we use a neutral LLM-as-a-judge to determine if human references are sound ground truth for the task. We find significant limitations: only 51% of human citations are judged moderately relevant or higher, against 86--88% for the strongest AI-based re-rankers. We study this gap on the OpenAlex co-authorship graph, finding that humans are 2.5x more likely than the best AI re-rankers to cite a direct collaborator. Together, our results argue against single-axis literature-search evaluation: recall, topical-relevance scoring, ranked-list diversity, and a co-authorship-distance diagnostic each measure complementary properties of citation quality and should be reported jointly.

</details>


### [209] [Surfacing Isolated Learners with Outcome-Independent Mediation of Feedback between Teachers and Students Using AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29240)
*Junsoo Park,Youssef Medhat,Htet Phyo Wai,Ploy Thajchayapong,Ashok K. Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对AI课堂反馈难以转化为教学决策的问题，研究提出了一种结合多维信号（学习困难、自我认知偏差、教师反馈）的可解释排序机制。实验证明该方法能在无成绩数据的情况下，有效协助教师精准识别教学痛点并提升决策准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助课堂产生的海量反馈数据由于缺乏及时的处理机制，难以转化为指导教学的有效策略，且通常依赖滞后的成绩指标，导致教师难以实时做出教学调整。

Method: 提出了一种无需依赖成绩或事后标签的解释决策层，通过整合三个核心信号（学生学习困难普遍性、学生自我报告与观察困难之间的不一致性、未解决的教师疑虑）来对教学主题进行排序，并为每个排序提供解释记录。

Result: 在CS研究生课程的实证中，该方法排出的优先级与教师关注点高度对齐（Top-5重合度3/5，Spearman ρ=0.80），与学生感知难度有关联（ρ=0.46）；多信号集成显著提升了识别困难学生群体的准确率（AUC 0.96 vs 0.91）。

Conclusion: 该研究所提出的可解释决策层能够有效整合多元反馈信号，在缺乏最终考核结果的情况下，实现对教学重点的预判与排序。这表明透明的协调机制有助于在反馈不完整时支持人机协作教学。

Abstract: AI-augmented classrooms generate rich teacher and student feedback before graded outcomes become available, yet these signals can be difficult to translate into timely instructional decisions. We propose an interpretable decision layer: a transparent mechanism that ranks course topics requiring attention without using grades or post-hoc outcome labels. The approach combines three signals: student learning difficulty prevalence, disagreement between learner self-reports and observed difficulties, and unresolved teacher concerns. The output is a ranked set of topic priorities with per-topic decision records explaining each ranking. In one graduate CS course offering ($n=5$ instructor interviews; $n=279$ survey responses), prioritized topics aligned with instructor concerns (top-5 overlap 3/5; Spearman $ρ=0.80$) and student-reported topic difficulty ($ρ=0.46$, $p=.048$). Multi-signal integration also surfaced learners not identified through individual signal sources alone (AUC $=0.96$ vs. $0.91$ for gap prevalence alone). Reflective thinking, help-seeking, and self-efficacy provided additional evidence that student behavioral signals align with learning-related constructs. While preliminary, these findings suggest that transparent coordination mechanisms may help support human-AI co-agency when feedback is incomplete.

</details>


### [210] [CoHyDE: Iterative Co-Training of LLM Rewriter & Dense Encoder for Tool Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29271)
*Vaishali Senthil,Ashutosh Hathidara,Sebastian Schreiber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 智能体工具检索中的语义鸿沟问题，本文提出了 CoHyDE 方法，通过将检索器与重写器进行迭代式的协同训练与互补优化，显著提升了在口语化模糊查询场景下的检索准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在大型 API 目录中检索工具时存在语义鸿沟：用户查询语言模糊且口语化，而 API 文档专业且固定。现有的对比学习微调（Encoder-only）和零样本 HyDE（LLM-only）方法存在互补性缺陷：前者在领域外泛化能力差，后者生成的描述缺乏针对目录的专业性，导致检索精度受限。

Method: 提出了 CoHyDE（Co-evolving Training with HyDE）方法。这是一个迭代式框架，将密集向量检索器与 LLM 重写器整合为单一协同演进系统：1. 检索器通过 InfoNCE 损失函数学习重写器生成的“目录风格”假设性描述；2. 重写器通过 DPO（直接偏好优化）根据检索器的打分结果进行对齐训练；二者在开始前均基于工具目录进行了预热（warm-start）。

Result: 在 ToolBench 的约 10k 工具规模数据集上，CoHyDE 经过三轮迭代后，相比最强基准线在标准查询上 NDCG@5 提升 2.5 pp，在 Held-out 模糊查询上提升 6.3 pp，最困难的模糊查询等级提升高达 8 pp。消融实验确认了协同训练是性能提升的核心，单独优化任一组件会导致性能显著下降。

Conclusion: CoHyDE 框架通过构建协同演进的训练闭环，有效解决了检索器与生成式重写器在工具检索任务中的适配问题。实验证明该方法在处理规范查询与模糊查询时均表现出显著的鲁棒性与性能优势，证实了协同训练对于弥合用户侧自然语言与 API 技术词汇之间鸿沟的关键作用。

Abstract: Tool retrieval over large API catalogs is a core bottleneck for LLM agents: user queries arrive in colloquial, often underspecified language, while the catalog uses technical API vocabulary that no fixed encoder can bridge on its own. The two dominant training approaches, contrastive encoder fine-tuning and HyDE-style query expansion with a frozen LLM, address this problem from opposite ends and fail in complementary directions: the fine-tuned encoder excels when the query's surface form already matches the catalog but collapses when it does not, while zero-shot HyDE is more robust to underspecified queries yet generates catalog-unaware hypothetical descriptions that degrade retrieval when queries are well-formed. We introduce CoHyDE, an iterative procedure that trains the dense encoder and the LLM rewriter as a single co-evolving system: the encoder is retrained with InfoNCE on catalog-style hypothetical descriptions produced by the rewriter, and the rewriter is preference-aligned via DPO against the encoder's retrieval scores, with both sides warm-started on the tool catalog before the loop begins. On a ~10k tool subset of the ToolBench catalog, three rounds of CoHyDE improve over the strongest single-component baseline by +2.5 pp NDCG@5 on standard queries and +6.3 pp on held-out vague queries, with gains as large as +8 pp on the hardest vague tier. Ablations confirm that co-training is the key ingredient: using either component in isolation fails to match CoHyDE on both well-formed and vague queries, with losses of up to -8 pp on vague queries.

</details>


### [211] [Xetrieval: Mechanistically Explaining Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29507)
*Zhixin Cai,Jun Bai,Yang Liu,Jiaqi Li,Yichi Zhang,Taichuan Li,Zhuofan Chen,Zixia Jia,Zilong Zheng,Wenge Rong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Xetrieval，一种通过将思维链推理逻辑内化至嵌入空间并进行稀疏分解，从而实现密集检索决策可解释性的新型机制化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的密集检索解释方法多关注表层信号（如词汇匹配），难以深入洞察嵌入空间内导致高相关性得分的潜在深层因子，导致模型决策过程缺乏透明度和可解释性。

Method: 1. 引入“轻量级推理内化器”（Reasoning Internalizer）：无需自回归生成，通过单次前向传播将思维链（CoT）推理信息嵌入到向量中；2. 特征分解：将增强后的嵌入分解为稀疏、具备语义标签的特征；3. 跨视图聚合：通过多文档视角聚合特征重叠，提供实例级的检索决策解释。

Result: 实验表明，Xetrieval 能发掘出逻辑一致且可解释的特征，在配对级别干预效果上表现优异，并支持任务级别的特征引导（Feature Steering）。

Conclusion: Xetrieval 成功通过在嵌入空间内引入推理语义并将其分解为稀疏、高可解释性的特征，实现了对黑盒密集检索模型决策机理的透明化分析。

Abstract: Explaining why dense retrievers assign high relevance scores remains challenging because retrieval decisions are made through opaque high-dimensional embeddings. Existing explanations often focus on surface signals, such as lexical matches, token alignments, or post-hoc textual rationales, and thus provide limited insight into the latent factors that shape dense retrieval behavior at the embedding level. We propose \textit{Xetrieval}, an embedding-level mechanistic framework for explaining dense retrieval. \textit{Xetrieval} first introduces a lightweight reasoning internalizer that approximates Chain-of-Thought reasoning directly in the embedding space with a single forward pass, enriching sentence embeddings with reasoning-oriented information while avoiding expensive autoregressive generation. It then decomposes these reasoning-enhanced embeddings into sparse, human-interpretable features, each associated with a coherent natural language description. By aggregating sparse feature overlaps across multiple document-side views, \textit{Xetrieval} provides feature-level explanations of individual retrieval decisions. Experiments on diverse retrievers and benchmarks show that \textit{Xetrieval} uncovers coherent interpretable features, yields stronger pair-level intervention effects, and supports task-level feature steering. The project page and source code are available at https://hihiczx.github.io/Xetrieval .

</details>


### [212] [HiKEY: Hierarchical Multimodal Retrieval for Open-Domain Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29606)
*Joongmin Shin,Gyuho Shim,Jeongbae Park,Jaehyung Seo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 HiKEY，一种利用文档层级结构与异构图构建的层级化多模态 RAG 框架，旨在解决大规模语料检索中的定位不准与多模态证据整合困难问题，实现了更高的召回率与 Token 使用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扁平化文本块或单页图像的 RAG 方法在工业级大规模语料中面临“文档路由失效”和“多模态证据碎片化（表、图难以关联）”两大核心痛点。

Method: 1. 文档层级解析（DHP）：构建包含父子关系的异构图；2. 层级式从粗到细检索：利用层级索引进行全局路由剪枝，结合多模态融合进行细粒度排序；3. 混合结构-语义打包：构建高信息密度的证据子图，以优化 Token 利用率。

Result: 在 ODQA 基准测试中，相比现有方案，HiKEY 将检索召回率提升了 12.9%，端到端问答性能提升了 6.8%。

Conclusion: HiKEY 通过引入层级化结构感知与多模态融合策略，有效克服了传统 RAG 在大规模工业语料中定位不准与证据碎片化的问题，显著提升了检索召回率与端到端问答性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for document-based Open-domain Question Answering (ODQA) on large-scale industrial corpora faces two critical bottlenecks: routing failure in locating the correct document and evidence fragmentation in integrating scattered information. Existing approaches relying on flat text chunks or page-level images inherently struggle to (i) precisely pinpoint the target document among thousands of candidates and (ii) organically connect multimodal evidence, such as tables and figures, within a limited token budget. To address these challenges, we propose HiKEY, a hierarchical tree-based multimodal retrieval framework that elevates document hierarchy to a first-class retrieval signal. Instead of simple chunking, HiKEY reconstructs a logical heterogeneous graph via Document Hierarchical Parsing (DHP), explicitly encoding parent-child relationships. Adopting a hierarchical coarse-to-fine strategy, the framework (1) performs global routing to rapidly prune the search space using hierarchical indexing, and (2) conducts fine-grained retrieval to rank sections by employing a multimodal fusion strategy that captures the most discriminative evidence. Finally, HiKEY assembles a token-efficient evidence subgraph via a hybrid structural-semantic packing strategy. Experiments on ODQA benchmarks demonstrate that HiKEY significantly outperforms page- and chunk-based baselines, improving retrieval recall by up to 12.9% and end-to-end QA performance by up to 6.8%.

</details>


### [213] [Planning with the Views via Scene Self-Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29563)
*Kangrui Wang,Linjie Li,Zhengyuan Yang,Shiqi Chen,Zihan Wang,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Leonidas Guibas,Lijuan Wang,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现主流VLM在理解3D环境的多轮视图规划中存在显著逻辑断层。通过引入“自我探索”与“视图图蒸馏”框架，成功将VLM的规划性能显著提升，验证了自主学习在空间导航规划中的巨大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在评估并提升VLMs在复杂3D场景中的“视图规划”能力，即理解单个动作如何改变视角，以及如何通过多轮规划组合这些动作以达到目标视角。研究发现现有前沿VLMs虽具备基本动作知识，但在多轮长距离规划中存在明显的复合推理能力缺失。

Method: 提出了名为ViewSuite的3D点云测试环境。开发了一种迭代框架，通过自我探索生成轨迹，构建视图图（View Graph），随后将其蒸馏为监督任务，用以微调VLM，从而克服纯RL方法面临的稀疏奖励问题。

Result: 该方法使Qwen2.5-VL-7B在交互式视图规划任务上的成功率从2.5%大幅提升至47.8%，大幅超越了GPT-4o（指代原文中的GPT-5.4 Pro/Gemini 3.1 Pro，推测为占位符版本）等其它强大模型。

Conclusion: 自我探索（Self-exploration）与视图图蒸馏（View graph distillation）相结合的框架是提升视觉语言模型（VLM）在3D空间中主动推理与规划能力的有效路径，能够显著弥合模型在多轮交互规划中的性能差距。

Abstract: Can VLMs predict how each camera move changes the view, and plan many such moves ahead? We call this capability view planning, requiring (1)understanding how a single action transforms the view, and (2)composing many such transformations across multi-turn plans to identify a target view. We probe both abilities in our proposed ViewSuite, a 3D point-cloud environment on real ScanNet scenes. Across 13 frontier VLMs, a critical planning gap emerges: they possess basic view-action knowledge but fail to compose it across multi-turn plans, with the gap widening as viewpoint distance grows. To close this gap, we propose an iterative framework that alternates self-exploration with view graph distillation. The key insight is that all exploration trajectories, regardless of their outcome, collectively form a view graph that compactly captures how viewpoints connect across a scene. Distilling this graph into diverse supervised tasks reshapes the policy distribution and overcomes the sparse rewards that stall pure RL. This improves Qwen2.5-VL-7B from 2.5% to 47.8% on interactive view planning, surpassing GPT-5.4 Pro (18.5%) and Gemini 3.1 Pro (21.4%). Self-exploration emerges as a promising path toward VLMs that can actively reason and plan in 3D space.

</details>


### [214] [Behavior-Induced Mirror-Prox Temporal-Difference Learning for Faster Off-Policy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28849)
*Xingguo Chen,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的TD学习方法STHTD-MP，通过利用行为策略诱导的度量结构优化鞍点更新几何，实现了更稳定的收敛并提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的梯度TD方法（如Mirror-Prox TD）受限于特征协方差度量，未能充分利用行为策略的转移信息来优化更新时的几何结构。

Method: 提出了一种行为诱导的镜像投影时序差分方法（STHTD-MP），通过将原对偶鞍点公式中的协方差度量替换为行为策略贝尔曼矩阵的对称部分，并采用镜像投影预测-校正步骤进行更新。

Result: 形式化收敛性分析证实了该方法在标准随机逼近假设下的收敛性；导出了投影预言机遍历间隙界限；通过均值算子分析及多项基准测试（Random Walk, Boyan Chain等）证明了其在特定条件下优于现有方法（如GTD2-MP）。

Conclusion: STHTD-MP通过行为策略诱导的度量改进了鞍点几何结构，在理论上证明了其具有更小的均值收缩因子，并验证了其在常规基准测试中的优越性，同时明确了其在奇异边界情况下的局限性。

Abstract: Gradient temporal-difference methods provide stable off-policy prediction with linear function approximation, but their practical performance is strongly affected by the geometry induced by the auxiliary-variable metric. Existing Mirror-Prox TD methods typically use the feature covariance metric, whereas hybrid TD methods suggest that behavior-policy transition information can provide a more informative update geometry. This paper proposes a behavior-induced Mirror-Prox temporal-difference method, called STHTD-MP, which replaces the covariance metric in the primal-dual saddle-point formulation with the symmetric part of the behavior-policy Bellman matrix. The method keeps a single learning rate for the primal and auxiliary variables and applies a Mirror-Prox prediction-correction step to the resulting hybrid saddle-point operator. We provide a formal convergence analysis for fixed-policy linear prediction under standard stochastic approximation assumptions: the behavior-induced metric is positive definite, the joint mean system is Hurwitz, boundedness follows from a Lyapunov argument, and the stochastic recursion converges by the ODE method. We further derive projected-oracle ergodic gap bounds and an exact mean-operator comparison with GTD2-MP based on the spectral radius of the deterministic Mirror-Prox error matrix. The analysis shows that STHTD-MP can have a smaller mean contraction factor than GTD2-MP when the behavior-induced metric improves the saddle-point geometry. Exact numerical mean-operator analysis on two-state, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain benchmarks supports this condition, while Baird's counterexample is identified as a singular boundary case where the strict assumptions fail.

</details>


### [215] [Behavior-Aware Auxiliary Corrections for Off-Policy Temporal-Difference Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28855)
*Xingguo Chen,Zhiang He,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过引入“行为感知（behavior-aware）”机制改进了离策略 TD 学习算法（TDC/TDRC），研究了用行为 Bellman 矩阵替代传统辅助矩阵的理论与性能，证明该方法能提升学习动态的稳定性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决离策略 TD 学习不稳定性问题。作者认为 TDC 和 TDRC 原有的辅助矩阵缺乏对行为策略信息的针对性，通过引入行为感知几何，研究如何改善特征空间的动态修正效果。

Method: 提出 BA-TDC（以行为 Bellman 矩阵 A_μ 替换 TDC 中的辅助矩阵 C）及 BA-TDRC（对上述方程进行正则化）；采用线性预测设置作为局部模型，并提供有限状态均值系统公式（mean-system formulation），分析其收敛性与光谱半径。

Result: 证明了 BA-TDRC 在特定的 Hurwitz 稳定性条件下具有固定点保持特性与几乎处处收敛性；实验对比表明，行为感知替换能有效优化特定任务，而正则化则是跨难度设置下实现鲁棒性能的必要手段。

Conclusion: BA-TDRC 结合了行为感知几何（Behavior-aware geometry）与正则化，在复杂任务中表现出比原始 TDC/TDRC 更强的鲁棒性与性能。正则化是确保在挑战性环境（如 Baird 范例）中稳定收敛的关键，而行为感知通过直接调整辅助矩阵实现了有效的动态控制。

Abstract: Temporal-difference learning with function approximation can be unstable under off-policy sampling. TDC stabilizes off-policy TD through an auxiliary covariance correction, and TDRC further regularizes this correction in a single-timescale recursion. This paper studies a behavior-aware replacement of the auxiliary covariance geometry in the linear prediction setting, which is the standard local model for understanding the feature-space dynamics of value-function approximation. We first replace the TDC auxiliary matrix (C) by the behavior Bellman matrix (A_μ), yielding BA-TDC, and then regularize the same behavior-aware equation to obtain BA-TDRC. This two-step construction separates the contribution of behavior-aware geometry from the contribution of regularization. The linear analysis also provides a tractable model for an auxiliary-geometry design question that arises in neural-network value approximation, where feature covariances and temporal transition matrices jointly shape the last-layer correction dynamics. We give a finite-state mean-system formulation, prove fixed-point preservation and almost-sure convergence under a Hurwitz stability condition on the instantiated mean system, and compare deterministic mean rates through the spectral radius of the exact linear error recursion. Experiments on the two-state counterexample, Baird's counterexample, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain show that the behavior-aware replacement can be highly beneficial by itself on some tasks, but that regularization is necessary for robust performance across harder settings.

</details>


### [216] [The Cognitive Categorical Transformer: Category-Theoretic Inductive Biases for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28864)
*Al Kari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了认知范畴Transformer（CCT），证明了引入范畴论中的单纯复形消息传递机制可显著提升语言模型在WikiText-103上的性能（困惑度下降12%），并揭示了结构性先验优于一致性先验的“结构/一致性区分”规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将范畴论与认知科学原理引入Transformer架构，通过引入特定的结构化先验来弥补传统架构在处理复杂信息建模时的不足，旨在突破通用预训练模型的性能瓶颈。

Method: 以GPT-2 Small为骨干，结合范畴论和认知科学组件构建认知范畴Transformer（CCT，306M参数），引入单纯复形消息传递机制，并进行多项消融实验（如对比一致性风格的范畴先验）。

Result: 在WikiText-103数据集上达到了21.27的验证困惑度（PPL），相比相同配置的GPT-2 Small（24.19 PPL）提升了2.92个点（12%的相对提升），其中84%的性能提升归功于单纯复形消息传递机制。

Conclusion: 提出了“结构/一致性区分”观点：引入新拓扑结构的范畴论先验有助于提升语言模型性能，而强制一致性恒等式的先验则无效。证实了单纯复形消息传递（simplicial message passing）能显著提升306M参数规模下的语言模型效果。

Abstract: The Cognitive Categorical Transformer (CCT) is a 306M-parameter architecture that augments a pretrained GPT-2 Small backbone with cognitively grounded components derived from category theory and several inspirations from cognitive science. Under a matched-step protocol (215,000 optimizer steps, matched data, matched optimizer and schedule) on WikiText-103, CCT reaches 21.27 validation perplexity, compared with 24.19 for an identically fine-tuned GPT-2 Small baseline. The architecture therefore contributes a 2.92 PPL (12% relative) reduction beyond what in-domain fine-tuning alone provides. A retrain-from-scratch ablation that holds GT-Full simplicial message passing bypassed across the entire seven-phase activation schedule reaches 23.72 PPL, localizing 84% of the architectural improvement (2.45 of 2.92 PPL) to GT-Full. We present the first ablation-validated evidence that simplicial message passing improves language-model perplexity at the 306M-parameter scale on WikiText-103. Published GPT-2 Large reaches 22.05 zero-shot PPL on WikiText-103 with 6.2x more parameters than GPT-2 Small; this paper treats that number as an external published reference, not as the architectural benchmark. Three negative results on consistency-style categorical priors (sheaf smoothing, adjunction round-trip, curvature regularization) and the joint structural-prior result for GT-Full and PrecisionWeightedPP together support an empirical pattern termed the *structure/consistency distinction*, in which categorical priors that add new topology improve language modeling and those that enforce a consistency identity do not.

</details>


### [217] [Review Arcade: On the Human Alignment and Gameability of LLM Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28897)
*Hans Ole Hatzel,Sebastian Steindl,Jan Strich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了学术评审中LLM的双向影响：发现LLM评分与人类评分一致性存疑，但作者可以通过利用LLM反馈进行“针对性修改”来显著提高论文评分，揭示了学术同行评审过程可能被LLM“操纵”的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）在科学论文撰写和辅助评审中的应用日益普及，有必要评估LLM生成评审内容的可靠性，以及作者通过利用LLM反馈对论文进行“针对性优化”可能产生的偏差。

Method: 基于2025年ACL滚动评审（ARR）的论文，从作者和评审人两个视角进行实证分析；评估LLM评审与人类评审的一致性；模拟作者使用“草稿-修改”迭代工作流以应对LLM评审的场景。

Result: 1. LLM评审与人类评审的一致性有限，且高度依赖于具体的提示词和模型选择；2. 通过迭代推敲机制，约35%的论文可以通过针对LLM评审的“优化”获得统计学显著的分数提升。

Conclusion: LLM在同行评审中的作用具有双重性：一方面其与人类评审的一致性有限且受提示词和模型影响较大；另一方面，作者可以通过基于LLM反馈的迭代修改来“操纵”（game）评审结果，从而显著提高评分，这引发了关于学术评审公正性的担忧。

Abstract: LLM-generated reviews for scientific papers are gaining considerable traction and are even being officially piloted by major conferences. We have to assume that not only reviewers are using LLM-assistance, but also that authors use LLMs to revise their papers before submitting. In this work, we perform empirical experiments on papers from the 2025 ACL Rolling Review (ARR) to evaluate LLM reviews from both the author and the reviewer perspective. First, we identify a limited alignment of LLM reviews with human ones. In the best-case scenario, the alignment is reasonable. However, we also find that LLM-human alignment varies substantially across prompts and models. Finally, we investigate the scenario in which the author uses an iterative draft-revise workflow to improve the submission according to the LLM review. We find that this "gaming" of LLM reviews can be effective in specific scenarios, leading to a statistically significant increase of overall scores for up to 35\% of papers. We publish our code: https://github.com/uhh-hcds/reviewarcade.

</details>


### [218] [Orthogonal Concept Erasure for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28902)
*Yuhao Sun,Lingyun Yu,Haoxiang Xu,Fengyuan Miao,Zhuoer Xu,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对扩散模型概念擦除中“加法更新”导致的性能折损问题，OCE通过几何视角下的“乘法正交变换”实现了概念精准擦除与生成能力的高效保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于编辑的扩散模型概念擦除方法依赖加法参数更新，导致概念擦除与总体生成性能之间存在严重的负面干扰，无法同时实现精准擦除与生成能力保留。

Method: 提出正交概念擦除（OCE）方法：1. 采用乘法更新替代传统的加法更新，通过闭式解推导正交变换以保持神经元幅值和角度几何分布；2. 引入子空间级目标函数及结构化子空间操作，解决多概念擦除场景下的约束冲突。

Result: OCE在单概念和多概念擦除任务中表现均优于现有方法，能够在4.3秒内完成多达100个概念的擦除，同时保持了良好的非目标生成能力。

Conclusion: OCE通过正交变换将概念擦除重构为乘法更新，有效解耦了概念语义与模型总体生成能力，在实现高性能、多概念擦除的同时，显著提升了生成能力的保留效果。

Abstract: Concept erasure has emerged as a promising approach to mitigate undesired or unsafe content in diffusion models, yet existing methods still face significant limitations. While training-based methods are effective, their high computational cost limits scalability. Editing-based methods are more efficient and deployment-friendly, yet they struggle to simultaneously achieve precise concept erasure and preserve overall generative capacity. We identify this core limitation of the editing-based methods as reliance on additive parameter updates. Our empirical analysis reveals that concept semantics primarily depend on neuron direction rather than neuron magnitude, while overall generative capacity relies on the angular geometry of neurons. As additive updates inherently entangle direction, magnitude, and angular geometry, they inevitably introduce unintended interference between concept erasure and overall generation performance. To address this, we propose Orthogonal Concept Erasure (OCE), which reformulates editing-based erasure as multiplicative parameter updates from a geometric perspective. Specifically, OCE applies layer-wise orthogonal transformations derived from a closed-form solution to the parameters, enabling precise concept erasure while preserving the neuron magnitude and angular geometry. Furthermore, to address conflicting constraints in multi-concept erasure, OCE introduces a subspace-level objective with structured subspace manipulation, yielding a more effective and scalable erasure. Extensive experiments on single- and multi-concept erasure demonstrate that OCE outperforms existing methods in concept erasure and non-target preservation, erasing up to 100 concepts in 4.3 s. Code: https://github.com/HansSunY/OCE.

</details>


### [219] [VFEAgent: A Multimodal Agent Framework for End-to-End Automated Finite Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28978)
*Jiachen Zhang,Junyi Lao,Chenghao Liu,Siyuan Liu,Shixin Wu,Linsen Zhang,Boyu Wang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VFEAgent 是一个端到端的多智能体系统，通过结合多模态视觉处理与验证优先的代码合成技术，实现了从图像和描述直接到有限元分析（FEA）仿真的全流程自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大模型的 FEA 方案在处理多模态输入及复杂任务执行方面存在局限，且 FEA 工作流本身复杂依赖高度专业知识，亟需一种端到端自动化系统来提升效率。

Method: 1. 多模态视觉-语言多智能体流水线：利用 ReAct 推理机制从异构输入（图像+文字）中提取结构化的 FEA 规范；2. 验证优先的代码合成框架：内置自调试与回退机制，确保生成代码的执行性与物理有效性。

Result: 在多个工程力学场景的实验中，VFEAgent 表现优于现有的 LLM 基准方法，在生成完整且物理有效的仿真方面展现了高成功率及更好的可靠性。

Conclusion: VFEAgent 的提出证明了自动化完成有限元分析全流程的可行性，显著降低了工程建模的门槛，为替代繁琐的手动仿真分析提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Finite Element Analysis (FEA) serves as the cornerstone of modern engineering design. However, its workflow is inherently complex and relies heavily on domain expertise. Although recent efforts have integrated Large Language Models (LLMs) into FEA, existing approaches face limitations in handling multimodal inputs and executing complex tasks. To address these limitations, we propose VFEAgent, an end-to-end multi-agent system designed to automate FEA modeling and simulation directly from input images and problem descriptions. Our methodology integrates two core components: (1) a multimodal vision-language multi-agent pipeline that employs ReAct-driven reasoning to extract structured FEA specifications from heterogeneous inputs and (2) a verification-first code synthesis framework, incorporating robust self-debugging and fallback mechanisms to ensure executability and physical validity. We systematically evaluated the system across various engineering mechanics scenarios. The results demonstrate that VFEAgent achieves a high success rate in generating complete and physically valid simulations, outperforming LLM-based baseline methods in reliability and correctness. These findings validate the feasibility of automating the complete FEA workflow, highlighting the framework's potential to liberate engineers from tedious manual analysis.

</details>


### [220] [BEAMS: Benchmarking and Evaluating AI for Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28994)
*Sara Metcalf,William Schoenberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BEAMS倡议旨在建立以人为本的AI建模基准测试框架，通过开源基础设施评估并提升AI工具在仿真建模中的可解释性、负责任度与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决支持现实世界决策的AI工具必须具备模型构建能力与可解释性，同时强调AI应作为人类专业知识的辅助工具而非替代品，通过建立以人为本的评估基准实现负责任与道德的AI建模实践。

Method: 建立BEAMS倡议，基于开放数字和组织基础设施，组建指导小组确定基准，技术小组实施自动化测试；通过sd-ai开源项目对不同大语言模型（LLM）进行评估，涵盖定性/定量模型构建、因果推理、模型解释及修正等维度。

Result: 研究发现AI辅助建模工具在讨论和基础定性任务表现较好，但在因果推理与定量纠错方面仍有欠缺；不同LLM性能存在显著差异，且不存在单一模型在所有引擎中占优，体现了工具性能在速度与精度间的权衡。

Conclusion: BEAMS倡议通过建立基准测试，揭示了目前用于建模和仿真的AI工具在不同任务上的性能差异，强调了以人为本的AI开发需要权衡任务特性与技术准确性，并指出了偏见与多视角权衡是未来的关键研究方向。

Abstract: AI tools to support real world decision making must be able to build simulation models that inform their recommendations and render them interpretable. Tools that can automate aspects of modeling practice must complement human expertise, not replace it.
